/** Shoukhin Home Decor v2.2.0. Bound Google Apps Script, owner-only web app. */
let requestReads_ = null;
const V2 = {version:'2.2.0', tz:'Asia/Dhaka', cutoff:'2026-09-01', historicalRate:15, tables:['Orders','Combos','Stock','Expenses','Budgets','Settings','Log','Stage','Ledger','Purchases','Months','Tasks','Checks','Insights','ProductMeta','AdDaily']};
function props_(){return PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();}
function db_(){const id=props_().getProperty('SHD_SHEET');if(!id)throw Error('Run setupSystem in the bound Sheet first.');return SpreadsheetApp.openById(id);}
function owner_(){const email=Session.getActiveUser().getEmail();if(!email || email!==props_().getProperty('SHD_OWNER'))throw Error('Owner sign-in required. Deploy with access: Only myself.');}
function locked_(fn){const lock=LockService.getScriptLock();lock.waitLock(30000);try{return fn();}finally{lock.releaseLock();}}
function sheet_(name){const s=db_().getSheetByName('SHD_'+name);if(!s)throw Error('Run setupSystem first: '+name);return s;}
function read_(name){if(requestReads_&&requestReads_[name])return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(requestReads_[name]));const s=sheet_(name);const a=s.getLastRow()<2?[]:s.getRange(2,1,s.getLastRow()-1,2).getValues().filter(r=>r[0]).map(r=>JSON.parse(r[1]));if(requestReads_)requestReads_[name]=a;return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(a));}
function rows_(s){if(!s||s.getLastRow()<2)return [];const a=s.getDataRange().getValues(),h=a.shift();return a.filter(r=>r.some(v=>v!==''&&v!==null)).map(r=>Object.fromEntries(h.map((k,i)=>[String(k).trim(),r[i]])));}
function writeAllLegacy_(name,items){if(requestReads_)delete requestReads_[name];const s=sheet_(name),old=s.getLastRow()-1;const data=items.map(x=>{const j=JSON.stringify(x);if(j.length>45000)throw Error('Record too large: '+x.id);return [x.id,j];});if(data.length)s.getRange(2,1,data.length,2).setValues(data);if(old>data.length)s.getRange(data.length+2,1,old-data.length,2).clearContent();}
function basePutLegacy_(name,item){if(requestReads_)delete requestReads_[name];const s=sheet_(name),n=s.getLastRow();const ids=n>1?s.getRange(2,1,n-1,1).getValues().flat().map(String):[];const i=ids.indexOf(String(item.id));const j=JSON.stringify(item);if(j.length>45000)throw Error('Record too large');s.getRange(i<0?n+1:i+2,1,1,2).setValues([[item.id,j]]);return item;}
function now_(){return new Date().toISOString();}
function today_(){return Utilities.formatDate(new Date(),V2.tz,'yyyy-MM-dd');}
function uid_(){return Utilities.getUuid();}
function str_(x){return x===null||x===undefined?'':String(x).trim();}
function num_(x){if(x===''||x===null||x===undefined||/^null$/i.test(String(x)))return null;const n=Number(x);return isFinite(n)?n:null;}
function amount_(x,label){const n=num_(x);if(n===null||n<0)throw Error(label+' must be zero or positive');return n;}
function day_(x){const s=str_(x).slice(0,10);if(!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(s)||isNaN(Date.parse(s))||new Date(s).toISOString().slice(0,10)!==s)throw Error('Invalid date: '+s);return s;}
function phone_(x){return str_(x).replace(/[০-৯]/g,c=>'০১২৩৪৫৬৭৮৯'.indexOf(c)).replace(/[^0-9]/g,'').replace(/^880/,'0');}
function baseSettings_(){return Object.assign({id:'main',businessName:'Shoukhin Home Decor',profitRate:15,geminiModel:'gemini-2.5-flash-lite',syncEnabled:false,syncMinutes:15,aiEnabled:false,aiLimit:40},read_('Settings').find(x=>x.id==='main')||{});}
function log_(type,message){const a=read_('Log');a.push({id:uid_(),time:now_(),type,message:str_(message).slice(0,500)});writeAll_('Log',a.slice(-200));}
function setupSystem(){return locked_(()=>{const ss=SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();if(!ss)throw Error('Open this project from the existing App Sheet.');const email=Session.getActiveUser().getEmail();if(!email)throw Error('Sign in as the Sheet owner.');const old=props_().getProperty('SHD_OWNER');if(old&&old!==email)throw Error('Only the configured owner can run setup.');props_().setProperties({SHD_OWNER:email,SHD_SHEET:ss.getId()});V2.tables.forEach(n=>{let s=ss.getSheetByName('SHD_'+n);if(!s){s=ss.insertSheet('SHD_'+n);s.getRange(1,1,1,2).setValues([['ID','Record JSON — edit through App']]);s.setFrozenRows(1);}});importProducts_();if(!read_('Settings').length)put_('Settings',settings_());migrateExpenses_();seedStock_();upgrade21_();upgrade214_();removeCourierSchedule_();setupSimple_();return {ok:true,message:'Setup completed. Existing orders have NOT been deleted. Open Import to preview and migrate.',version:V2.version};});}
function importProducts_(){const ss=db_();let s=ss.getSheetByName('Products');if(s&&s.getLastRow()>1)return;const source=rows_(ss.getSheetByName('Product Info'));if(!s)s=ss.insertSheet('Products');const headers=['SKU','Product Name','Category','Cost Price','Sales Price','Current Stock','Reorder Point','Status'];if(s.getLastRow()===0)s.getRange(1,1,1,headers.length).setValues([headers]);const h=s.getRange(1,1,1,s.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];const used=new Set();const data=source.filter(r=>str_(r['Product Name'])).map((r,i)=>{let sku=str_(r['Product ID'])||'SHD-'+String(i+1).padStart(5,'0');if(used.has(sku))sku+='-'+i;used.add(sku);const p={'SKU':sku,'Product Name':str_(r['Product Name']),'Category':str_(r.Category)||'Other','Cost Price':num_(r['Total cost']),'Sales Price':num_(r['Selling price']),'Current Stock':0,'Reorder Point':0,'Status':'Active'};return h.map(k=>p[k]===undefined?'':safeCell_(p[k]));});if(data.length)s.getRange(2,1,data.length,h.length).setValues(data);}
function safeCell_(v){return typeof v==='string'&&/^[=+@-]/.test(v)?"'"+v:v;}
function seedStock_(){const all=read_('Stock'),orders=read_('Orders');products_().forEach(p=>{if(!all.some(b=>b.id===p.id)){const included={};orders.filter(o=>o.date>=V2.cutoff&&o.date<=today_()).forEach(o=>included[o.id]=o.imported&&!o.lines.length?null:used_(o)[p.id]||0);all.push({id:p.id,date:today_(),qty:p.legacyStock,included,movements:[],provisional:true});}});writeAll_('Stock',all);}
function monthString_(value){if(value instanceof Date)return Utilities.formatDate(value,V2.tz,'yyyy-MM');const s=str_(value);if(/^\d{4}-\d{2}/.test(s))return s.slice(0,7);throw Error('Invalid existing expense projection month. Correct source month before setup.');}
function baseProducts_(){return rows_(db_().getSheetByName('Products')).filter(r=>r.SKU).map(r=>({id:str_(r.SKU),name:str_(r['Product Name']),category:str_(r.Category),cost:num_(r['Cost Price']),price:num_(r['Sales Price']),legacyStock:num_(r['Current Stock'])||0,reorder:num_(r['Reorder Point'])||0,active:r.Status!=='Inactive'}));}
function migrateExpenses_(){if(props_().getProperty('SHD_EXP_MIGRATED'))return;const ex=rows_(db_().getSheetByName('Expenses')).filter(r=>r['Expense ID']).map(r=>({id:str_(r['Expense ID']),date:Utilities.formatDate(new Date(r.Date),V2.tz,'yyyy-MM-dd'),category:str_(r.Category),amount:num_(r.Amount)||0,note:str_(r.Notes)}));if(!read_('Expenses').length)writeAll_('Expenses',ex);const budgets=rows_(db_().getSheetByName('Expense_Projections')).filter(r=>r['Projection ID']).map(r=>({id:str_(r['Projection ID']),category:str_(r.Category),amount:num_(r['Monthly Amount'])||0,frequency:'monthly',start:monthString_(r['Start Month'])+'-01',end:r['End Month']?new Date(Date.UTC(Number(monthString_(r['End Month']).slice(0,4)),Number(monthString_(r['End Month']).slice(5,7)),0)).toISOString().slice(0,10):'',active:String(r.Active).toLowerCase()==='true'}));if(!read_('Budgets').length)writeAll_('Budgets',budgets);props_().setProperty('SHD_EXP_MIGRATED','1');}
function doGet(){owner_();return HtmlService.createTemplateFromFile('Index').evaluate().setTitle('Shoukhin Home Decor').addMetaTag('viewport','width=device-width, initial-scale=1');}
function include_(name){return HtmlService.createHtmlOutputFromFile(name).getContent();}
/** Only public data entry point. All helpers ending in _ are inaccessible to google.script.run. */
function shdApi(action,payload){try{owner_();requestReads_={};const p=payload||{};const actions={bootstrap:bootstrap_,dashboard:dashboard_,dashboardSeed:dashboardSeed_,orders:orderList_,customers:customers_,saveOrder:saveOrder_,saveProduct:saveProduct_,saveCombo:saveCombo_,stock:stockView_,saveStock:saveStock_,expenses:expenseView_,saveExpense:saveExpense_,saveBudget:saveBudget_,settings:getSettings_,saveSettings:saveSettings_,importPreview:importPreview_,importCommit:importCommit_,book:book_,sync:sync_,updateStatus:updateStatus_,updateStatuses:updateStatuses_,attachCourier:attachCourier_,courier:extraCourier_,finance:cashView_,saveCash:saveCash_,savePurchase:savePurchase_,payBill:payBill_,closeMonth:closeMonth_,reopenMonth:reopenMonth_,monthReview:monthReview_,guidance:guidanceView_,revision:revision_,saveDamage:saveDamage_,saveTarget:saveTarget_,ads:adView_,saveAd:saveAd_,syncAds:syncAds_,setCourierSync:setCourierSync_,refreshCourier:refreshCourier_,refreshBalance:refreshBalance_,customerType:customerType_,catalog:catalogView_,receivePurchase:receivePurchase_,inventoryMetrics:p=>inventoryMetrics_(p,read_('Orders')),logs:()=>read_('Log').reverse()};if(!actions[action])throw Error('Unknown action');if(action==='revision')return {ok:true,data:revision_()};return {ok:true,data:locked_(()=>{if(['bootstrap','dashboardSeed','catalog','inventoryMetrics','stock'].includes(action))applyStockEntries_();const data=actions[action](p);if(!['bootstrap','dashboard','dashboardSeed','orders','customers','stock','expenses','settings','finance','monthReview','guidance','customerType','catalog','inventoryMetrics','logs','ads'].includes(action))touch_();return data;})};}catch(e){return {ok:false,error:String(e.message||e)};}}
function scheduledSync(){return {skipped:true,message:'Background courier sync is retired. Use Update Status or Update active statuses manually.'};}



<script>

const $=s=>document.querySelector(s),$$=s=>Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(s));

const escapeHTML=x=>String(x??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const e=escapeHTML;const money=n=>n===null||n===undefined?'—':'৳'+Number(n).toLocaleString('en-US',{maximumFractionDigits:2});const number=n=>Number(n||0).toLocaleString('en-US',{maximumFractionDigits:1});
const statuses=['Draft','Confirmed','In Review','Pending','Shipped','Hold','Delivered Approval Pending','Partial Approval Pending','Cancelled Approval Pending','Delivered','Partial','Cancelled','Returned','Unknown'];
function toast(s){$('#toast').textContent=s;$('#toast').style.display='block';clearTimeout(toast.timer);toast.timer=setTimeout(()=>$('#toast').style.display='none',7000);}
let busyCount=0;function busy(on){busyCount+=on?1:-1;$('#busy').style.display=busyCount>0?'block':'none';}
function apiBase(action,payload={}){busy(true);return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>google.script.run.withSuccessHandler(r=>{busy(false);if(!r||!r.ok){const msg=r?.error||'No server response';toast(msg);reject(Error(msg));}else resolve(r.data);}).withFailureHandler(err=>{busy(false);toast(err.message||String(err));reject(err);}).shdApi(action,payload));}
window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',ev=>{if(!ev.reason?.staleView)toast(ev.reason?.message||String(ev.reason));ev.preventDefault();});
function opts(a,value='',blank=false){return (blank?'<option value="">All / select</option>':'')+a.map(x=>{const v=typeof x==='object'?x.id:x,n=typeof x==='object'?x.name:x;return `<option value="${e(v)}" ${String(v)===String(value)?'selected':''}>${e(n)}</option>`;}).join('');}
function field(name,label,value='',type='text',extra=''){return `<label>${e(label)}<input name="${e(name)}" type="${type}" value="${e(value??'')}" ${extra}></label>`;}
function select(name,label,a,value='',blank=false){return `<label>${e(label)}<select name="${e(name)}">${opts(a,value,blank)}</select></label>`;}
function check(name,label,on=false){return `<label><input type="checkbox" name="${e(name)}" ${on?'checked':''}> ${e(label)}</label>`;}
function table(headers,rows){if(!rows.length)return '<div class="empty">No records for this selection</div>';return `<div class="table-wrap"><table><thead><tr>${headers.map(h=>`<th class="${h[0]==='#'?'num':''}">${e(h.replace(/^#/,''))}</th>`).join('')}</tr></thead><tbody>${rows.map(r=>`<tr>${r.map((v,i)=>`<td class="${headers[i][0]==='#'?'num':''}">${v&&v.html!==undefined?v.html:e(v??'—')}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>`;}
const raw=html=>({html});const badge=s=>raw(`<span class="badge">${e(s)}</span>`);const btn=(label,action,id='')=>`<button type="button" data-action="${e(action)}" data-id="${e(id)}">${e(label)}</button>`;
function modal(title,body,save,handler){$('#modal').innerHTML=`<form id="modalForm"><div class="modalhead"><h2>${e(title)}</h2><button type="button" class="close" id="closeModal">×</button></div>${body}<div class="modalfoot"><button type="button" id="cancelModal">Close</button>${save?`<button class="primary" type="submit">${e(save)}</button>`:''}</div></form>`;$('#modal').showModal();$('#closeModal').onclick=$('#cancelModal').onclick=()=>$('#modal').close();$('#modalForm').onsubmit=async ev=>{ev.preventDefault();const b=ev.submitter,label=b.textContent;b.disabled=true;b.textContent='Saving…';try{await handler(Object.fromEntries(new FormData(ev.target)),ev.target);}finally{b.disabled=false;b.textContent=label;}};}
function close(){if($('#modal').open)$('#modal').close();}
function dateToday(){return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA',{timeZone:'Asia/Dhaka',year:'numeric',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit'}).format(new Date());}
function branding(){$('#brandName').textContent=S.settings.businessName;$('#brandIcon').innerHTML=S.settings.logo?`<img src="${e(S.settings.logo)}" alt="Business logo">`:'SH';}
function highlight(){$$('#nav button').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('selected',b.dataset.page===S.page));$('#pageTitle').textContent=S.page;}
function chart(sel,rows,key,line=false){const el=$(sel);if(!rows.length){el.innerHTML='<div class="empty">No data</div>';return;}const width=Math.max(800,rows.length>90?rows.length*8:800),height=270,left=70,right=25,top=20,bottom=45,max=Math.max(1,...rows.map(x=>Number(x[key])||0)),scale=(height-top-bottom)/max,step=(width-left-right)/rows.length;let svg='';for(let i=0;i<=4;i++){const value=max*i/4,y=height-bottom-value*scale;svg+=`<line x1="${left}" x2="${width-right}" y1="${y}" y2="${y}"/><text x="${left-8}" y="${y+4}" text-anchor="end">${e(number(value))}</text>`;}const points=rows.map((r,i)=>[left+step*(i+.5),height-bottom-(Number(r[key])||0)*scale]);if(line)svg+=`<polyline points="${points.map(p=>p.join(',')).join(' ')}" fill="none" stroke="var(--brand)" stroke-width="2.5"/>`;rows.forEach((r,i)=>{const [x,y]=points[i],tip=e(r.name+': '+number(r[key]));svg+=line?`<circle cx="${x}" cy="${y}" r="4" tabindex="0"><title>${tip}</title></circle>`:`<rect x="${x-step*.35}" y="${y}" width="${Math.max(1,step*.7)}" height="${Math.max(.5,height-bottom-y)}" rx="2" tabindex="0"><title>${tip}</title></rect>`;if(i%Math.max(1,Math.ceil(rows.length/8))===0)svg+=`<text x="${x}" y="${height-18}" text-anchor="middle">${e(r.name.length>14?r.name.slice(0,13)+'…':r.name)}</text>`;});el.innerHTML=`<svg viewBox="0 0 ${width} ${height}" role="img" aria-label="${e(key)} chart" style="min-width:${width}px">${svg}</svg>`;}
async function loadOrders(){const r=await api('orders',{query:S.query,status:S.status,offset:S.offset,pendingProducts:S.pendingProducts});S.orders=r.rows;$('#content').innerHTML=`<div class="card actions"><input id="orderSearch" placeholder="Name, phone, tracking, area" value="${e(S.query)}"><select id="orderStatus">${opts(statuses,S.status,true)}</select><label><input type="checkbox" id="missingProducts" ${S.pendingProducts?'checked':''}> Product assignment pending</label><button id="findOrders">Search</button></div><div class="card"><p>${r.total} matching orders</p>${table(['Date','Customer','District','#Total','Status','Gross / historical est.','After courier','Actions'],r.rows.map(o=>[o.date,o.name,o.district||'Review',money(o.total),raw(`<span class="badge">${e(o.status)}</span><small class="status-time">${e(o.lastSync?'Updated '+new Date(o.lastSync).toLocaleString():'Not updated')}</small>`),o.finance.reason||money(o.finance.historical?o.finance.estimate:o.finance.gross),o.finance.historical?'—':o.finance.reason||money(o.finance.contribution),raw(btn('Open','order',o.id)+((o.cid||o.tracking)?btn('Update Status','updateStatus',o.id):'')+btn('Invoice','invoice',o.id))]))}<div class="actions section-gap"><button id="prevOrders" ${S.offset===0?'disabled':''}>Previous</button><span>${S.offset+1}–${Math.min(S.offset+50,r.total)}</span><button id="nextOrders" ${S.offset+50>=r.total?'disabled':''}>Next</button></div></div>`;$('#findOrders').onclick=()=>{S.query=$('#orderSearch').value;S.status=$('#orderStatus').value;S.pendingProducts=$('#missingProducts').checked;S.offset=0;loadOrders();};$('#prevOrders').onclick=()=>{S.offset=Math.max(0,S.offset-50);loadOrders();};$('#nextOrders').onclick=()=>{S.offset+=50;loadOrders();};}
function lineRow(l={}){const c=S.catalog.find(x=>x.id===l.sku);return `<div class="linegrid"><label>Product / combo<select class="sku">${opts(S.catalog.filter(x=>x.active||x.id===l.sku),l.sku,true)}</select></label><label>Qty<input class="qty" type="number" min="0.01" step="any" value="${e(l.qty||1)}"></label><label>Unit price<input class="price" type="number" min="0" step="any" value="${e(l.price??c?.price??'')}"></label><label>Unit cost<input class="cost" type="number" min="0" step="any" value="${e(l.cost??c?.cost??'')}" ${l.cost!==undefined&&l.cost!==null?'readonly':''}></label><label>Returned qty<input class="returned" type="number" min="0" step="any" value="${e(l.returned||0)}"></label><button type="button" class="removeLine">×</button></div>`;}

function openOrderBase(order={}){S.editing=order;const o={date:S.today,status:'Draft',source:'Facebook',paymentStatus:'Unpaid',total:'',cod:'',shippingCharge:'',codCharge:'',returnCharge:0,...order};const warning=o.imported?'Imported COD kept as source value. required for courier)' ,o.phone)}${field('address','Full address',o.address)}${field('district','District',o.district)}${field('thana','Thana',o.thana)}${select('source','Order source',['Facebook','WhatsApp','Website','Phone','Walk-in','Old Data','Other'],o.source)}${select('paymentStatus','Customer payment',['Unknown','Unpaid','Partial','Paid','Refunded'],o.paymentStatus)}${field('total','Full order value incl. customer shipping',o.total,'number',o.imported?'min="0" step="any"':'min="0" step="any" required')}${field('cod','COD to collect (after advance)',o.cod,'number',o.imported?'min="0" step="any"':'min="0" step="any" required')}${field('shippingCharge','Courier shipping cost — blank if unknown',o.shippingCharge,'number','min="0" step="any"')}${field('codCharge','Courier COD fee — blank if unknown',o.codCharge,'number','min="0" step="any"')}${field('returnCharge','Extra return charge (do not repeat shipping)',o.returnCharge,'number','min="0" step="any"')}${field('finalAmount','Partial: verified final collected amount',o.finalAmount,'number','min="0" step="any"')}${check('amountVerified','Partial collected amount verified',o.amountVerified)}${check('itemsVerified','Partial kept/returned line quantities verified',o.itemsVerified)}${check('dispatched','Parcel physically dispatched',o.dispatched)}<label class="full">Note<textarea name="note">${e(o.note)}</textarea></label></div><h3 class="section-gap">Items / combo</h3><div id="lineItems">${(o.lines?.length?o.lines:[{}]).map(lineRow).join('')}</div><button type="button" id="addLine">+ Item</button><p id="lineSum" class="muted"></p><h3 class="section-gap">Partial: financially returned components</h3><p class="muted">For part of a combo, enter component quantities here instead of fractional combo returns. Cost of kept components is calculated automatically.</p><div class="formgrid">${Object.keys(ret).map(sku=>field('fin:'+sku,(S.catalog.find(c=>c.id===sku)?.name||sku)+' (max '+ret[sku]+')',o.financialReturns?.[sku]||0,'number',`min="0" max="${ret[sku]}" step="any"`)).join('')}</div><h3 class="section-gap">Physical stock received back</h3><p class="muted">Cancelled after dispatch does not automatically restock. async function resizeImage(file,max,quality){const bitmap=await createImageBitmap(file),scale=Math.min(1,max/Math.max(bitmap.width,bitmap.height));const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=Math.round(bitmap.width*scale);canvas.height=Math.round(bitmap.height*scale);const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');ctx.fillStyle='#fff';ctx.fillRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);ctx.drawImage(bitmap,0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);bitmap.close();return canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg',quality);}

/** Minimal XLSX reader: stored/deflated ZIP, shared strings and first worksheet. No remote parser. */
async function readXlsx(file){if(file.size>10000000)throw Error('XLSX exceeds 10 MB');const bytes=new Uint8Array(await file.arrayBuffer()),v=new DataView(bytes.buffer),decoder=new TextDecoder();let end=-1;for(let i=bytes.length-22;i>=Math.max(0,bytes.length-65557);i--){if(v.getUint32(i,true)===0x06054b50){end=i;break;}}if(end<0)throw Error('Invalid XLSX ZIP');let at=v.getUint32(end+16,true),count=v.getUint16(end+10,true);const entries={};for(let i=0;i<count;i++){if(v.getUint32(at,true)!==0x02014b50)throw Error('Invalid ZIP index');const method=v.getUint16(at+10,true),size=v.getUint32(at+20,true),uncompressed=v.getUint32(at+24,true),nl=v.getUint16(at+28,true),el=v.getUint16(at+30,true),cl=v.getUint16(at+32,true),offset=v.getUint32(at+42,true),name=decoder.decode(bytes.slice(at+46,at+46+nl));entries[name]={method,size,uncompressed,offset};at+=46+nl+el+cl;}async function xml(name){const entry=entries[name];if(!entry)return null;if(entry.uncompressed>20000000)throw Error('Worksheet too large');const o=entry.offset,begin=o+30+v.getUint16(o+26,true)+v.getUint16(o+28,true);let data=bytes.slice(begin,begin+entry.size);if(entry.method===8)data=new Uint8Array(await new Response(new Blob([data]).stream().pipeThrough(new DecompressionStream('deflate-raw'))).arrayBuffer());else if(entry.method!==0)throw Error('Unsupported XLSX compression. Use included CSV.');return new DOMParser().parseFromString(decoder.decode(data),'application/xml');}const shared=await xml('xl/sharedStrings.xml'),strings=shared?Array.from(shared.getElementsByTagName('si')).map(si=>Array.from(si.getElementsByTagName('t')).map(t=>t.textContent).join('')):[];const sheet=await xml('xl/worksheets/sheet1.xml');if(!sheet)throw Error('First worksheet missing. Use CSV.');const rows=Array.from(sheet.getElementsByTagName('row')).map(row=>{const out=[];Array.from(row.getElementsByTagName('c')).forEach(c=>{const ref=c.getAttribute('r')?.replace(/\d/g,'')||'A';let col=0;for(const ch of ref)col=col*26+ch.charCodeAt(0)-64;const t=c.getAttribute('t'),value=c.getElementsByTagName('v')[0]?.textContent||'';out[col-1]=t==='s'?strings[Number(value)]:t==='inlineStr'?Array.from(c.getElementsByTagName('t')).map(x=>x.textContent).join(''):value;});return out;});const h=rows.shift();return rows.filter(r=>r.some(Boolean)).map(r=>Object.fromEntries(h.map((k,i)=>{let x=r[i]??'';if(['Created At','Approved At','Delivery Time'].includes(k)&&/^\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(x)){x=new Date((Number(x)-25569)*86400000).toISOString().replace('T',' ').replace('Z','');}return [k,x];})));}

const tones={green:'#139d75',blue:'#3878db',yellow:'#b99312',orange:'#d87720',red:'#dc4f59',purple:'#865bce'};
const pct=n=>n===null||n===undefined?'—':number(n)+'%';

const quickDates=['Today','Yesterday','Last 7 Days','Last 30 Days','This Month','Last Month','Last 3 Months','This Year','All Time','Custom'];
function dAdd(d,n){return new Date(Date.parse(d+'T00:00:00Z')+n*86400000).toISOString().slice(0,10);}
function dateRange(kind){const t=S.today,first=t.slice(0,7)+'-01',prev=dAdd(first,-1);switch(kind){case 'Today':return [t,t];case 'Yesterday':return [dAdd(t,-1),dAdd(t,-1)];case 'Last 7 Days':return [dAdd(t,-6),t];case 'Last 30 Days':return [dAdd(t,-29),t];case 'This Month':return [first,t];case 'Last Month':return [prev.slice(0,7)+'-01',prev];case 'Last 3 Months':{const d=new Date(first+'T00:00:00Z');d.setUTCMonth(d.getUTCMonth()-2);return [d.toISOString().slice(0,10),t];}case 'This Year':return [t.slice(0,4)+'-01-01',t];case 'All Time':return [(S.dashboard?.options.months||[]).slice().sort()[0]+'-01',t];default:return [S.start,S.end];}}
function dashboardFilter(){return {...S.filters,start:S.start,end:S.end};}

function kpi(label,value,note='',tone='blue',action=''){return `<${action?'button':'div'} ${action?'type="button" data-v21="'+e(action)+'"':''} class="kpi tone-${tone}"><small>${e(label)}</small><strong>${e(value)}</strong><small>${e(note)}</small></${action?'button':'div'}>`;}
function monthEndLocal(m){return new Date(Date.UTC(Number(m.slice(0,4)),Number(m.slice(5,7)),0)).toISOString().slice(0,10);}
function localDates(start,end){const out=[];for(let d=start;d<=end;d=dAdd(d,1)){out.push(d);if(out.length>5000)throw Error('Select a period shorter than 5000 days');}return out;}
function stat21(name){return {name,orders:0,sales:0,qty:0,returnQty:0,returnQtyUnknown:false,fees:0,gross:0,contribution:0,estimate:0,incomplete:0,delivered:0,partial:0,returned:0,cancelled:0,pending:0,finalized:0,soldOrders:0,partialUnverified:0,conflicts:0,historyIncomplete:0,customers:{}};}
function metric21(g){g.returnRate=g.finalized?g.returned/g.finalized*100:null;g.cancelRate=g.finalized?g.cancelled/g.finalized*100:null;g.problemRate=g.finalized?(g.returned+g.cancelled)/g.finalized*100:null;g.partialRate=g.finalized?g.partial/g.finalized*100:null;g.aov=g.soldOrders?g.sales/g.soldOrders:null;const vals=Object.values(g.customers||{});g.repeatRate=vals.length?vals.filter(Boolean).length/vals.length*100:null;return g;}
function currentInventory21(seed,p){const rows=(seed.inventory?.rows||[]).filter(x=>x.active!==false&&(!p.product||x.id===p.product)&&(!p.category||x.category===p.category)),physical=rows.filter(x=>!x.components);return {rows,totalItems:physical.length,activeProducts:rows.length,comboCount:rows.length-physical.length,totalQty:physical.reduce((a,x)=>a+Number(x.qty||0),0),value:physical.some(x=>x.value===null||x.value===undefined)?null:physical.reduce((a,x)=>a+Number(x.value||0),0),low:rows.filter(x=>x.low).length,dead:physical.filter(x=>x.dead).length,deadValue:physical.filter(x=>x.dead).reduce((a,x)=>a+Number(x.value||0),0),unverified:rows.filter(x=>!x.verified).length,componentValueOnly:!!(p.product&&rows.some(x=>x.components))};}
function dashboardFromSeedLegacy(input={}){
  const seed=S.dashboardSeed;if(!seed)throw Error('Dashboard data is not loaded');const p={start:input.start||S.start,end:input.end||S.end,district:input.district||'',category:input.category||'',product:input.product||'',marketplace:input.marketplace||'',customerType:input.customerType||'',query:String(input.query||'').toLowerCase()};
  const filteredLines=!!(p.product||p.category),segmented=filteredLines||!!(p.district||p.marketplace||p.customerType||p.query),maps={days:{},months:{},districts:{},products:{},statuses:{},marketplaces:{}},d=stat21('Total'),globalByMonth={},closed=new Set((seed.closedMonths||[]).filter(x=>x.closed).map(x=>x.id));
  const group=(k,n)=>maps[k][n]||(maps[k][n]=stat21(n)),final=o=>['Delivered','Partial','Returned','Cancelled'].includes(o.status),matchOrder=o=>(!p.district||o.district===p.district)&&(!p.marketplace||o.source===p.marketplace)&&(!p.customerType||o.customerType===p.customerType)&&(!p.query||[o.name,o.phone,o.invoice,o.cid,o.tracking,o.id].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(p.query)),matchPart=l=>(!p.product||l.sku===p.product)&&(!p.category||l.category===p.category);
  function add(g,o,parts){const ratio=parts?parts.reduce((a,l)=>a+Number(l.share||0),0):1;g.orders++;if(o.phone){if(!(o.phone in g.customers))g.customers[o.phone]=false;if(o.customerType==='Repeat')g.customers[o.phone]=true;}if(final(o))g.finalized++;g[{Delivered:'delivered',Partial:'partial',Returned:'returned',Cancelled:'cancelled'}[o.status]||'pending']++;if(['Delivered','Partial'].includes(o.status)&&o.sales!==null&&o.sales!==undefined)g.soldOrders++;if(o.status==='Partial'&&!o.partialVerified)g.partialUnverified++;if(o.approvalConflict)g.conflicts++;if(o.historical&&final(o)&&!o.known)g.historyIncomplete++;g.sales+=Number(o.sales||0)*ratio;g.modernSales=(g.modernSales||0)+(o.historical?0:Number(o.sales||0)*ratio);g.fees+=Number(o.fee||0)*ratio;const facts=parts||o.parts||[];g.qty+=facts.reduce((a,l)=>a+Number(l.qty||0),0);facts.forEach(l=>{g.returnQty+=Number(l.returned||0);if(l.returned===null)g.returnQtyUnknown=true;});if(!o.historical&&final(o)){const known=o.sales!==null&&o.fee!==null&&(parts?parts.length&&parts.every(l=>l.cogs!==null):o.cogs!==null);if(!known||o.approvalConflict)g.incomplete++;else{const gross=parts?parts.reduce((a,l)=>a+Number(l.gross||0),0):Number(o.gross||0);g.gross+=gross;g.contribution+=gross-Number(o.fee||0)*ratio;}}g.estimate+=Number(o.estimate||0)*ratio;}
  const inRange=(seed.orders||[]).filter(o=>o.date>=p.start&&o.date<=p.end);inRange.forEach(o=>{const m=o.date.slice(0,7);if(!o.historical)globalByMonth[m]=(globalByMonth[m]||0)+Number(o.sales||0);if(!matchOrder(o))return;const parts=(o.parts||[]).filter(matchPart);if(filteredLines&&!parts.length)return;const slice=filteredLines?parts:null;add(d,o,slice);[['days',o.date],['months',m],['statuses',o.status]].forEach(([k,n])=>add(group(k,n),o,slice));[['districts',o.district||'Unknown',slice],['marketplaces',o.source||'Unknown',slice]].concat(parts.map(l=>['products',l.sku,[l]])).forEach(([k,n,items])=>{const g=group(k,n);add(g,o,items);g.byMonth=g.byMonth||{};const gm=g.byMonth[m]||(g.byMonth[m]=stat21(m));add(gm,o,items);});});
  localDates(p.start,p.end).forEach(dt=>group('days',dt));[...new Set(localDates(p.start,p.end).map(x=>x.slice(0,7)))].forEach(m=>group('months',m));
  const exRows={},monthExpenses={};(seed.expenseDaily||[]).filter(x=>x.date>=p.start&&x.date<=p.end).forEach(x=>{const mk=x.date.slice(0,7),g=monthExpenses[mk]||(monthExpenses[mk]={actual:0,used:0}),rk=mk+'|'+String(x.category).toLowerCase(),r=exRows[rk]||(exRows[rk]={month:mk,category:x.category,actual:0,projection:0,used:0});g.actual+=Number(x.actual||0);g.used+=closed.has(mk)?Number(x.actual||0):Number(x.used||0);r.actual+=Number(x.actual||0);r.projection+=Number(x.projection||0);r.used+=Number(x.used||0);});
  const months=Object.values(maps.months).sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name));let allocatedActual=0,allocatedUsed=0;const allocated=(gm,m,isSegment)=>{const x=monthExpenses[m]||{actual:0,used:0},share=isSegment?(globalByMonth[m]>0?(gm.modernSales||0)/globalByMonth[m]:0):1;return {actual:x.actual*share,used:x.used*share};};months.forEach(m=>{const a=allocated(m,m.name,segmented),historical=m.name<seed.cutoff.slice(0,7),isClosed=closed.has(m.name);Object.assign(m,{historical,closed:isClosed,actualExpense:a.actual,estimatedExpense:a.used,net:historical||!isClosed||m.incomplete?null:m.contribution-a.actual,projectedNet:historical?null:m.contribution-a.used,displayNet:historical?m.estimate:(historical?m.estimate:(isClosed&&!m.incomplete?m.contribution-a.actual:m.contribution-a.used))});allocatedActual+=a.actual;allocatedUsed+=a.used;metric21(m);});
  d.net=months.some(m=>m.historical||!m.closed||m.incomplete)?null:d.contribution-allocatedActual;d.projectedNet=months.some(m=>!m.historical)?d.contribution-allocatedUsed:null;const modern=months.filter(m=>!m.historical),history=months.filter(m=>m.historical),modernComplete=modern.length>0&&modern.every(m=>m.closed&&!m.incomplete),modernActual=modernComplete?modern.reduce((a,m)=>a+m.contribution-m.actualExpense,0):null,modernEstimated=modern.length?modern.reduce((a,m)=>a+m.contribution-m.estimatedExpense,0):0,historyEstimate=history.reduce((a,m)=>a+m.estimate,0);d.displayNet=history.length?(historyEstimate+(modernComplete?modernActual:modernEstimated)):(modernComplete?modernActual:modernEstimated);d.displayNetLabel=history.length?(modern.length?'Includes historical estimates':'Historical data estimated at '+seed.historicalRate+'%'):(modernComplete?'Actual':'Estimated');d.displayNetMode=history.length&&modern.length?(modernComplete?'Actual + historical estimate':'Estimated + historical estimate'):d.displayNetLabel;d.displayMargin=d.sales?d.displayNet/d.sales*100:null;d.historicalRate=seed.historicalRate;
  ['districts','products','marketplaces'].forEach(k=>Object.values(maps[k]).forEach(g=>{let act=0,est=0;Object.entries(g.byMonth||{}).forEach(([m,gm])=>{const a=allocated(gm,m,true);act+=a.actual;est+=a.used;});g.net=d.net===null||g.incomplete?null:g.contribution-act;g.projectedNet=g.contribution-est;metric21(g);delete g.byMonth;}));
  ['days','districts','products','statuses','marketplaces'].forEach(k=>d[k]=Object.values(maps[k]).map(metric21).sort(k==='days'?(a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name):(a,b)=>b.sales-a.sales));const cat=seed.catalog||S.catalog;d.products.forEach(g=>{g.id=g.name;g.name=cat.find(c=>c.id===g.id)?.name||g.id;});metric21(d);if(!modern.length)d.gross=null;d.filter=p;d.months=months;d.expenses={rows:Object.values(exRows),allocatedActual,allocatedUsed};d.segmented=segmented;d.inventory=currentInventory21(seed,p);d.cash=seed.cash;d.options=seed.options;d.allocation='Operating expenses are allocated monthly by recognized sales share across the business.';return d;
}

  S.dashboard=d;const f=dashboardFilter(),inv=d.inventory,c=d.cash,netTone=Number(d.displayNet)<0?'red':'green',grossNote=d.incomplete||d.missingDelivery?'Missing dates, costs or charges':d.gross===null?'Historical period — product costs unavailable':d.months.some(m=>m.historical)?'Sep 2026 onward only · historical uses net estimate':d.incomplete?d.incomplete+' order(s) still incomplete':'Before courier and operating expenses';
  const sel=(id,label,values,val,blank=true)=>`<label>${label}<select id="${id}">${opts(values,val,blank)}</select></label>`,updated=c.balanceUpdated?new Date(c.balanceUpdated).toLocaleString():'Not fetched yet';

function readDashboardFields(){return {district:$('#districtFilter').value,category:$('#categoryFilter').value,product:$('#productFilter').value,marketplace:$('#marketFilter').value,customerType:$('#customerFilter').value,query:$('#dashSearch').value};}
function bar21(selector,rows){const max=Math.max(1,...rows.flatMap(x=>[x.actual,x.projection]));$(selector).innerHTML=rows.length?'<div class="expense-bars">'+rows.map((r,i)=>`<div><strong>${e(r.name)}</strong><div class="bar-track"><span style="width:${r.projection/max*100}%;background:${Object.values(tones)[i%6]}"></span></div><small>Actual ${money(r.actual)} · Accrued ${money(r.projection)}</small></div>`).join('')+'</div>':'<div class="empty">No expenses or projections in this period</div>';}
function payModal(id,kind){const x=S.finance.upcoming.find(x=>x.id===id&&x.kind===kind);if(!x)return;const requestId=crypto.randomUUID();modal('Pay outstanding bill',`<p>Outstanding: ${money(x.outstanding)}. This payment updates bank only; it does not count the expense again.</p><div class="formgrid">${field('date','Payment date',S.today,'date','required')}${field('amount','Amount',x.outstanding,'number',`required min="0.01" max="${x.outstanding}" step="any"`)}${field('reference','Bank payment reference')}</div>`,'Record payment',async p=>{await api('payBill',{...p,id,kind,requestId});close();loadFinance();});}
async function loadExpenses(){const r=await api('expenses');S.expenses=r;$('#content').innerHTML=`<div class="card actions"><button id="addExpense" class="primary">+ Actual expense</button><button id="addBudget">+ Daily / monthly projection</button></div><div class="banner">Each actual expense covers a date or date range. A complete actual entry replaces only that category’s projection for those days. Projections accrue automatically without reducing bank. Payments are recorded separately and never reduce profit twice.</div><div class="card"><h2>Actual expenses</h2>${table(['Coverage','Category','Marketplace','#Expense','#Paid','Due',''],r.expenses.map(x=>[(x.periodStart||x.date)+' → '+(x.periodEnd||x.date),x.category,x.marketplace||'Business-wide',x.void?'Voided':money(x.amount),Array.isArray(x.payments)?money((x.paidBeforeOpening||0)+x.payments.reduce((a,p)=>a+p.amount,0)):'Not tracked',x.dueDate||'',raw(x.nonCash?'Non-cash loss':x.metaManaged?'Manage in Ads':btn('Edit','expense',x.id))]))}</div><div class="card"><h2>Daily / monthly expense projections</h2>${table(['Category','Frequency','#Amount','Start','End','Status',''],r.budgets.map(x=>[x.category,x.frequency,money(x.amount),x.start,x.end||'Ongoing',x.active?'Active':'Paused',raw(btn('Edit','budget',x.id))]))}</div>`;$('#addExpense').onclick=()=>expenseModal();$('#addBudget').onclick=()=>budgetModal();}
function expenseModal(x={}){const requestId=crypto.randomUUID();modal('Actual expense',`<div class="formgrid">${field('date','Entry / incurred date',x.date||S.today,'date','required')}${field('category','Category: Ads / Salary / Packing / Other',x.category,'text','required')}${field('periodStart','Expense covers from',x.periodStart||x.date||S.today,'date','required')}${field('periodEnd','Expense covers through',x.periodEnd||x.date||S.today,'date','required')}${field('amount','Total expense for coverage period',x.amount,'number','min="0" step="any" required')}${select('marketplace','Marketplace (for attributable ads)',S.dashboard?.options.marketplaces||['Facebook','Website','WhatsApp','Other'],x.marketplace,true)}${!x.id?field('paid','Paid from bank now',0,'number','min="0" step="any"')+field('paidDate','Payment date',S.today,'date'):''}${x.id?field('paidBeforeOpening','Amount already paid before opening balance',x.paidBeforeOpening||0,'number','min="0" step="any"')+(!Array.isArray(x.payments)?check('confirmPaymentStatus','I checked prior payments; the remaining amount is unpaid',false):''):''}${field('dueDate','Due date for unpaid amount',x.dueDate,'date')}${check('coverageComplete','Complete actual cost for this category and coverage period',x.coverageComplete!==false)}${field('note','Note',x.note)}${check('void','Void this expense',x.void)}</div><p class="muted">A monthly salary amount is spread over its coverage dates. Confirmed zero expense is allowed. If incomplete, actual plus remaining projection is an estimate; mark complete when reconciled. Record later payments in Purchases & Bank.</p>`,'Save expense',async(p,f)=>{p.id=x.id;p.requestId=requestId;p.void=f.elements.namedItem('void').checked;p.coverageComplete=f.elements.namedItem('coverageComplete').checked;p.confirmPaymentStatus=!!f.elements.namedItem('confirmPaymentStatus')?.checked;await api('saveExpense',p);close();loadExpenses();});}

Closing confirms all actual expense entries are complete, including categories where the actual expense is zero.</p>${r.existing?.closed?`<div class="banner">Closed ${e(new Date(r.existing.closedAt).toLocaleString())}</div><button data-v21="reopenMonth">Reopen with reason</button>`:`<label><input type="checkbox" id="confirmMonth"> All actual expenses and required order data for this month are complete. I understand any unentered projected cost will not be treated as actual.</label><button class="primary section-gap" data-v21="closeMonth" ${r.canClose?'':'disabled'}>Close Month</button>`}`;}
function renderProducts(){$('#content').innerHTML=`<div class="card actions"><button id="addProduct" class="primary">+ Product</button><button id="addCombo">+ Combo</button></div><div class="card"><h2>Products & combos</h2>${table(['Product','Category','#Cost','#Price','#Available','Status',''],S.catalog.map(p=>[p.name,p.category,money(p.cost),money(p.price),p.stock,badge(p.active?'Active':'Inactive'),raw(btn('Edit',p.components?'combo':'product',p.id))]))}</div>`;$('#addProduct').onclick=()=>productModal();$('#addCombo').onclick=()=>comboModal();}
function openOrderLegacy(order={}){
  openOrderBase(order);const f=$('#modalForm'),grid=f.querySelector('.formgrid');grid.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',`${select('customerType','Customer type',['New','Repeat','Unknown'],order.customerType||'New')}${check('customerTypeManual','Keep my manual New / Repeat choice',order.customerTypeManual)}`);
  const phone=f.querySelector('[name=phone]');phone.onblur=async()=>{if(f.elements.namedItem('customerTypeManual').checked)return;const r=await api('customerType',{phone:phone.value,date:f.elements.namedItem('date').value,id:order.id,createdAt:order.createdAt});f.elements.namedItem('customerType').value=r.type;};f.elements.namedItem('customerType').onchange=()=>f.elements.namedItem('customerTypeManual').checked=true;
}
document.addEventListener('click',async ev=>{
  const b=ev.target.closest('[data-v21]');if(!b||b.disabled)return;const action=b.dataset.v21,id=b.dataset.id;const rule=S.guidance?.suggestions?.find(r=>r.id===id);b.disabled=true;
  try{
    if(action.startsWith('inventory:')){S.inventoryMode=action.split(':')[1];await navigate('Inventory',true);}
    else if(action==='finance')await navigate('Purchases & Bank');
    else if(action==='balance'){await api('refreshBalance');await navigate(S.page);}
    else if(action==='cash')cashModal(id);
    else if(action==='newPurchase')purchaseModal();
    else if(action==='payBill')payModal(id,b.dataset.kind);
    else if(action==='receivePurchase'){const date=prompt('Actual stock receipt date (YYYY-MM-DD):',S.today);if(date){await api('receivePurchase',{id,date});await refreshCatalog();loadFinance();}}
    else if(action==='closeMonth'){await api('closeMonth',{month:S.closingMonth,confirm:$('#confirmMonth').checked});reviewMonth();}
    else if(action==='reopenMonth'){const reason=prompt('Reason for reopening this month:');if(reason){await api('reopenMonth',{month:S.closingMonth,reason});reviewMonth();}}
  }finally{b.disabled=false;}
});

// Short-lived memory cache. No financial data is kept in localStorage.
const viewCache=new Map(),viewPending=new Map();let viewEpoch=0;
const cachedReads=new Set(['dashboard','dashboardSeed','catalog','orders','customers','inventoryMetrics','expenses','finance','tasks','guidance','settings','monthReview']);
const volatileReads=new Set(['bootstrap','customerType','logs']);
const pageReads=new Set(['dashboard','orders','customers','inventoryMetrics','expenses','finance','ads','guidance','monthReview']);
function copyView(x){return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(x));}
function clearViews(){viewEpoch++;viewCache.clear();viewPending.clear();}
function rememberView(key,value){viewCache.delete(key);viewCache.set(key,{time:Date.now(),value:copyView(value)});while(viewCache.size>12)viewCache.delete(viewCache.keys().next().value);}
function staleView(){const e=new Error('A newer view is open.');e.staleView=true;return e;}
async function reloadGuidance(){clearViews();return loadGuidance();}
// Simple edition: retain actual DOM nodes between visits; memory cache is session-only.
const simplePages=new Map();let simpleEpoch=0;
function invalidateSimple(){simpleEpoch++;S.dashboardSeedDirty=true;viewCache.clear();viewPending.clear();viewEpoch++;}
const readActions=new Set(['bootstrap','dashboard','dashboardSeed','catalog','orders','customers','inventoryMetrics','stock','expenses','finance','guidance','settings','monthReview','logs','ads','revision','customerType']);
async function api(action,payload={}){
 const before=simpleEpoch,route=S.navToken||0;
 if(!readActions.has(action)){
  invalidateSimple();try{return await apiBase(action,payload);}finally{invalidateSimple();}
 }
 if(action==='revision')return apiBase(action,payload);
 const key=action+':'+JSON.stringify(payload),hit=viewCache.get(key);if(hit&&hit.epoch===simpleEpoch&&Date.now()-hit.time<300000)return copyView(hit.value);
 let job=viewPending.get(key);if(!job){job=apiBase(action,payload).then(r=>{if(before===simpleEpoch)viewCache.set(key,{value:copyView(r),time:Date.now(),epoch:simpleEpoch});return r;});viewPending.set(key,job);}
 try{const r=await job;if(before!==simpleEpoch||pageReads.has(action)&&route!==(S.navToken||0))throw staleView();return copyView(r);}finally{if(viewPending.get(key)===job)viewPending.delete(key);}
}
function updateNote(s){$('#pageUpdate').textContent=s;}
async function navigate(page,force=false){
 const content=$('#content'),oldPage=S.page;
 if(S.renderedPage&&content.firstChild)simplePages.set(S.renderedPage,{nodes:Array.from(content.childNodes),scroll:window.scrollY||0,epoch:S.renderEpoch,orders:S.orders,stock:S.stock,expenses:S.expenses,finance:S.finance});
 S.page=page;highlight();const token=S.navToken=(S.navToken||0)+1,saved=simplePages.get(page);
 if(saved&&!force&&saved.epoch===simpleEpoch){content.replaceChildren(...saved.nodes);Object.assign(S,{orders:saved.orders,stock:saved.stock,expenses:saved.expenses,finance:saved.finance});S.renderedPage=page;S.renderEpoch=simpleEpoch;window.scrollTo(0,saved.scroll);updateNote('');return;}
 updateNote('Updating '+page+'…');
 const routes={'Dashboard':loadDashboard,'Orders':loadOrders,'Products & Combos':renderProducts,'Inventory':loadStock,'Expenses':loadExpenses,'Purchases & Bank':loadFinance,'Month Closing':loadClosing,'Suggestions':loadGuidance,'Customers':loadCustomers,'Import':renderImport,'Courier':renderCourier,'Settings':renderSettings,'Ads':loadAds,'Help':renderHelp};
 try{if(!routes[page])throw Error('Page unavailable');await routes[page]();if(token!==S.navToken)return;S.renderedPage=page;S.renderEpoch=simpleEpoch;updateNote('');window.scrollTo(0,saved?.scroll||0);}
 catch(err){if(!err.staleView){S.page=oldPage;highlight();updateNote('Update failed: '+err.message+' · Previous data retained.');}}
}
async function refreshCurrentView(){
 if(S.refreshing)return;if($('#modal').open){S.pendingRefresh=true;toast('Finish your entry; data refresh will wait.');return;}
 S.refreshing=true;$('#refresh').disabled=true;updateNote('Checking Sheet…');
 try{const oldToday=S.today;invalidateSimple();const r=await api('bootstrap');Object.assign(S,{catalog:r.catalog,combos:r.combos,settings:r.settings,today:r.today,revision:r.revision,dashboardSeed:r.dashboardSeed,dashboardSeedDirty:false,migrated:r.migrated});if(oldToday!==r.today&&S.quick!=='Custom'&&!S.month)[S.start,S.end]=dateRange(S.quick);branding();await navigate(S.page,true);}
 catch(err){updateNote('Refresh failed: '+err.message+' · Saved view retained.');}finally{S.refreshing=false;$('#refresh').disabled=false;}
}
async function checkSheetChanges(){
 if(document.hidden||S.refreshing)return;if($('#modal').open){S.pendingRefresh=true;return;}
 try{const r=await api('revision');if(r.revision!==S.revision||r.today!==S.today||S.pendingRefresh){S.pendingRefresh=false;await refreshCurrentView();}}catch(e){updateNote('Automatic check failed; use Refresh.');}
}
async function init(){
 S.filters={};S.groupBy='Daily';S.dashToken=0;S.quick='This Month';S.page='Dashboard';
 try{const r=await api('bootstrap');Object.assign(S,{...r,start:r.today.slice(0,7)+'-01',end:r.today,dashboardSeedDirty:false});branding();
 const pages=['Dashboard','Orders','Products & Combos','Inventory','Expenses','Purchases & Bank','Month Closing','Suggestions','Customers','Ads','Import','Courier','Settings','Help'];
 $('#nav').innerHTML=pages.map(n=>`<button data-page="${e(n)}">${e(n)}</button>`).join('');$('#nav').onclick=ev=>{if(ev.target.dataset.page)navigate(ev.target.dataset.page);};await navigate('Dashboard');setInterval(checkSheetChanges,300000);
 }catch(err){updateNote('Could not load data: '+err.message);}
}
function dashboardFromSeed(input={}){
 const seed=S.dashboardSeed,p={...input},original=seed.orders,match=o=>(!p.district||o.district===p.district)&&(!p.marketplace||o.source===p.marketplace)&&(!p.customerType||o.customerType===p.customerType)&&(!p.query||[o.name,o.phone,o.invoice,o.cid,o.tracking,o.id].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(p.query.toLowerCase()))&&(!(p.product||p.category)||(o.parts||[]).some(l=>(!p.product||l.sku===p.product)&&(!p.category||l.category===p.category)));
 const start=p.start||S.start,end=p.end||S.end;
 seed.orders=original.filter(o=>o.eventDate).map(o=>({...o,date:o.eventDate}));let d;try{d=dashboardFromSeedLegacy(p);}finally{seed.orders=original;}
 const created=original.filter(o=>o.createdDate>=start&&o.createdDate<=end&&match(o));d.orders=created.length;d.days.forEach(x=>x.orders=0);const daymap=new Map(d.days.map(x=>[x.name,x]));created.forEach(o=>{if(daymap.has(o.createdDate))daymap.get(o.createdDate).orders++;});
 const selected=original.filter(o=>o.eventDate>=start&&o.eventDate<=end&&match(o)),ratio=o=>(p.product||p.category)?(o.parts||[]).filter(l=>(!p.product||l.sku===p.product)&&(!p.category||l.category===p.category)).reduce((a,l)=>a+l.share,0):1;
 d.deliveredAmount=selected.filter(o=>o.status==='Delivered').reduce((a,o)=>a+Number(o.sales||0)*ratio(o),0);
 const pending=created.filter(o=>!['Delivered','Partial','Returned','Cancelled','Draft'].includes(o.status));d.pending=pending.length;d.pendingAmount=pending.some(o=>o.cod===null)?null:pending.reduce((a,o)=>a+Number(o.cod||0)*ratio(o),0);if(selected.some(o=>o.status==='Delivered'&&o.sales===null))d.deliveredAmount=null;
 d.missingDelivery=original.filter(o=>['Delivered','Partial'].includes(o.status)&&!o.deliveredAt&&match(o)&&o.createdDate<=end).length;const finalRows=selected.filter(o=>['Delivered','Partial','Returned','Cancelled'].includes(o.status));d.chargeMissing=finalRows.filter(o=>o.fee===null).length;d.fees=finalRows.reduce((a,o)=>a+Number(o.fee||0)*ratio(o),0);if(finalRows.length&&d.chargeMissing===finalRows.length)d.fees=null;
 const missingAmounts=new Set(selected.filter(o=>['Delivered','Partial'].includes(o.status)&&o.sales===null).map(o=>o.eventDate));d.days.forEach(x=>{if(missingAmounts.has(x.name))x.sales=null;});
 if(d.incomplete||d.historyIncomplete||d.missingDelivery){d.displayNet=null;d.displayMargin=null;d.displayNetLabel='Incomplete data';if(d.incomplete||d.missingDelivery)d.gross=null;}
 const cross=d.months.filter(m=>!m.historical&&m.estimate!==0),extra=cross.reduce((a,m)=>a+m.estimate,0);if(cross.length&&d.displayNet!==null){d.displayNet+=extra;d.projectedNet=d.projectedNet===null?null:d.projectedNet+extra;d.net=null;d.displayNetLabel='Includes historical estimates';d.displayMargin=d.sales?d.displayNet/d.sales*100:null;cross.forEach(m=>{m.projectedNet+=m.estimate;m.net=null;});}
 d.months.forEach(m=>{if(m.incomplete||m.historyIncomplete){m.projectedNet=null;m.net=null;m.displayNet=null;}if(d.missingDelivery&&!m.historical){m.projectedNet=null;m.net=null;m.displayNet=null;m.incomplete++;}});
 d.districts.concat(d.products,d.marketplaces).forEach(x=>{if(x.incomplete){x.gross=null;x.net=null;x.projectedNet=null;}});
 d.inventory=currentInventory21(seed,{});d.target=targetView(seed,S.today);d.chartDays=groupPeriods(d.days,S.groupBy||'Daily');d.targetSeries=[{key:'sales',label:'Delivered sales',color:'blue'}];
 if((S.groupBy||'Daily')==='Daily'&&!d.segmented&&start.slice(0,7)===S.today.slice(0,7)&&end.slice(0,7)===S.today.slice(0,7)&&d.target.amount>0){d.chartDays.forEach(x=>x.required=d.target.required);d.targetSeries.push({key:'required',label:'Required/day now',color:'orange',dashed:true});}return d;
}
function groupPeriods(rows,kind){
 if(kind==='Daily')return rows.map(x=>({...x,label:x.name.slice(5),range:x.name}));const by=new Map();
 rows.forEach(r=>{const day=new Date(r.name+'T00:00:00Z'),year=r.name.slice(0,4),month=r.name.slice(0,7);let key;
 if(kind==='Weekly'){const offset=(day.getUTCDay()+6)%7;key=dAdd(r.name,-offset);}else if(kind==='Monthly')key=month;else if(kind==='Quarterly')key=year+'-Q'+Math.ceil(Number(r.name.slice(5,7))/3);else key=year;
 if(!by.has(key))by.set(key,{name:key,first:r.name,last:r.name,sales:0,orders:0});const g=by.get(key);g.last=r.name;g.sales=(g.sales===null||r.sales===null)?null:g.sales+(r.sales||0);g.orders+=r.orders||0;
 });return [...by.values()].map((g,i)=>({...g,label:kind==='Weekly'?'W'+(i+1):kind==='Monthly'?monthLabel(g.name):kind==='Quarterly'?g.name.slice(5)+'-'+g.name.slice(2,4):g.name,range:g.first+' to '+g.last+(kind==='Weekly'&&localDates(g.first,g.last).length<7?' (partial week)':'')}));
}
function targetView(seed,today){const month=today.slice(0,7),amount=Number(seed.targets?.find(x=>x.month===month)?.amount||0),rows=seed.orders.filter(o=>o.deliveredAt?.slice(0,7)===month&&o.deliveredAt<=today&&['Delivered','Partial'].includes(o.status)),achieved=rows.reduce((a,o)=>a+Number(o.sales||0),0),remaining=Math.max(0,amount-achieved),days=localDates(today,monthEndLocal(month)).length;return {month,amount,achieved,remaining,required:remaining/days,days};}
function renderTarget(d){const t=d.target;$('#targetSummary').innerHTML=`<div class="target-strip"><span>Monthly target <b>${money(t.amount)}</b></span><span>Delivered <b>${money(t.achieved)}</b></span><span>Remaining <b>${money(t.remaining)}</b></span><span>Required/day <b>${money(t.required)}</b></span><button id="setTarget">Set target</button></div><small>Whole business · ${monthLabel(t.month)} · ${t.days} days including today · Known delivered amounts</small>`;$('#setTarget').onclick=()=>modal('Monthly sales target',field('month','Month',t.month,'month','required')+field('amount','Target BDT',t.amount,'number','min="0" required'),'Save',async p=>{S.dashboardSeed=await api('saveTarget',p);S.dashboardSeedDirty=false;close();loadDashboard();});}
function shortNumber(v){if(v===null||v===undefined)return '—';const n=Number(v),a=Math.abs(n);return a>=1e6?(n/1e6).toFixed(a%1e6?1:0)+'m':a>=1000?(n/1000).toFixed(a%1000?1:0)+'k':Number(n.toFixed(1)).toString();}
function graph21(selector,rows,series,line=false){
 const el=$(selector);if(!rows.length){el.innerHTML='<p>No data in this period.</p>';return;}
 const integer=series.every(s=>/orders|returned|cancelled|partial|messages/i.test(s.key)),values=rows.flatMap(r=>series.map(s=>r[s.key]).filter(v=>v!==null&&v!==undefined&&Number.isFinite(Number(v)))),rawHi=Math.max(1,...values),rawLo=Math.min(0,...values),stepY=integer?Math.max(1,Math.ceil((rawHi-rawLo)/4)):(rawHi-rawLo)/4,lo=integer?Math.floor(rawLo/stepY)*stepY:rawLo,hi=integer?lo+stepY*4:rawHi;
 const w=620,h=250,left=55,right=15,top=26,bottom=42,scale=v=>top+(hi-v)/(hi-lo)*(h-top-bottom),zero=scale(0),step=(w-left-right)/rows.length;
 let svg='';for(let i=0;i<=4;i++){const v=lo+(hi-lo)*i/4,y=scale(v);svg+=`<line x1="${left}" x2="${w-right}" y1="${y}" y2="${y}"/><text x="${left-8}" y="${y+4}" text-anchor="end">${e(integer?String(Math.round(v)):shortNumber(v))}</text>`;}
 series.forEach((s,j)=>{let points=[];const flush=()=>{if(points.length)svg+=`<polyline points="${points.join(' ')}" fill="none" stroke="${tones[s.color]}" stroke-width="2.2" ${s.dashed?'stroke-dasharray="6 4"':''}/>`;points=[];};rows.forEach((r,i)=>{const value=r[s.key];if(value===null||value===undefined){flush();return;}const x=left+(i+.5)*step,y=scale(value),tip=e((r.range||r.name)+' · '+s.label+': '+number(value)),color=value<0?tones.red:tones[s.color];if(line){points.push(x+','+y);svg+=`<circle cx="${x}" cy="${y}" r="3" style="fill:${color}"><title>${tip}</title></circle>`;}else{const bw=step*.75/series.length;svg+=`<rect x="${x-step*.375+j*bw}" y="${Math.min(y,zero)}" width="${Math.max(.4,bw-1)}" height="${Math.abs(y-zero)}" rx="2" style="fill:${color}"><title>${tip}</title></rect>`;}if(rows.length<=31&&!s.dashed)svg+=`<text x="${line?x:x-step*.375+(j+.5)*step*.75/series.length}" y="${value<0?y+13:y-7}" text-anchor="middle">${e(integer?String(value):shortNumber(value))}</text>`;});if(line)flush();});
 rows.forEach((r,i)=>{if(i%Math.max(1,Math.ceil(rows.length/10))===0){const label=r.label||(/^\d{4}-\d{2}$/.test(r.name)?monthLabel(r.name):r.name.slice(5)||r.name);svg+=`<text x="${left+(i+.5)*step}" y="${h-15}" text-anchor="middle">${e(label)}</text>`;}});
 el.innerHTML=`<div class="legend">${series.map(s=>`<span><i style="background:${tones[s.color]}"></i>${e(s.label)}</span>`).join('')}</div><svg viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}" role="img" aria-label="${e(series.map(s=>s.label).join(', '))}">${svg}</svg>`;
}
async function loadGuidance(){const r=await api('guidance',dashboardFilter());S.guidance=r;$('#content').innerHTML='<div class="card"><h2>Suggestions</h2><p>Rules based on your saved data. Review the evidence before acting.</p><div id="suggestionsArea"></div></div>';renderSuggestions();}

function renderSettings(){const s=S.settings;$('#content').innerHTML=`<form id="simpleSettings" class="card"><h2>Settings</h2><div class="formgrid">${field('businessName','Business name',s.businessName)}${field('deadDays','Dead stock after days',s.deadDays||60,'number','min="1"')}${field('sfKey','Steadfast API key (blank keeps saved)','','password')}${field('sfSecret','Steadfast secret (blank keeps saved)','','password')}${field('metaAccount','Meta USD ad account',s.metaAccount)}${field('metaVersion','Supported Graph API version from Meta app',s.metaVersion)}${field('metaToken','Meta token with ads_read (blank keeps saved)','','password')}${field('usdRate','BDT per USD',s.usdRate,'number','min="0.01" step="any"')}${field('vatRate','Ad VAT %',s.vatRate,'number','min="0" max="100" step="any"')}<label>Logo<input id="logoFile" type="file" accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp"></label></div><p>Steadfast credentials: ${s.hasSteadfast?'Saved':'Not saved'} · Meta token: ${s.hasMeta?'Saved':'Not saved'}. Sync runs only when requested.</p><button class="primary">Save settings</button></form>`;$('#simpleSettings').onsubmit=async ev=>{ev.preventDefault();const p=Object.fromEntries(new FormData(ev.target)),b=ev.submitter;b.disabled=true;try{if($('#logoFile').files[0])p.logo=await resizeImage($('#logoFile').files[0],160,.65);S.settings=await api('saveSettings',p);branding();toast('Settings saved');}finally{b.disabled=false;}};}

Count available units excluding reserved parcels.</p>${table(['Product','#Available','#Stock value','Last sale',''],rows.map(p=>[p.name,p.qty,p.components?'Shared components':money(p.value),p.lastSale||'—',raw(p.components?btn('Combo','combo',p.id):btn('Count','stock',p.id))]))}</div>`;$('#simpleBuy').onclick=purchaseModal;$('#simpleDamage').onclick=damageModal;}

Bank receipt does not create profit.</p>`,'Save',async p=>{await api('saveCash',{...p,requestId:S.cashId});close();loadFinance();});S.cashId=crypto.randomUUID();}
function purchaseModal(){const id=crypto.randomUUID();modal('Stock purchase',`<div class="formgrid">${field('date','Purchase and receipt date',S.today,'date','required')}${field('supplier','Supplier')}${field('reference','Reference')}${select('sku','Product',S.catalog.filter(x=>!x.components),'',true)}${field('qty','Quantity','','number','min="0.01" step="any" required')}${field('cost','Unit purchase cost','','number','min="0" step="any" required')}${check('includedInCount','These units are already included in my physical count',false)}${field('note','Note')}</div><p>Records a fully paid purchase and stock receipt once. Bank decreases; profit changes when goods are sold.</p>`,'Save purchase',async(p,f)=>{await api('savePurchase',{...p,requestId:id,receivedDate:p.date,paid:Number(p.qty)*Number(p.cost),includedInCount:f.elements.namedItem('includedInCount').checked,lines:[{sku:p.sku,qty:p.qty,cost:p.cost}]});close();await refreshCatalog();loadFinance();});const f=$('#modalForm');f.querySelector('[name=sku]').onchange=()=>{f.querySelector('[name=cost]').value=S.catalog.find(x=>x.id===f.querySelector('[name=sku]').value)?.cost??'';};}
function openOrder(order={}){
 openOrderLegacy(order);const grid=$('#modalForm .formgrid');grid.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',field('deliveredAt','Verified delivery date (blank if unknown)',(order.deliveredAt||'').slice(0,10),'date'));
 // Keep primary inputs visible; optional financial and return details remain available.
 const form=$('#modalForm');const optional=['thana','paymentStatus','shippingCharge','codCharge','returnCharge','finalAmount','amountVerified','itemsVerified','dispatched','adAttributed'];
 const details=document.createElement('details');details.className='optional-fields';details.innerHTML='<summary>More details / courier charges / partial delivery</summary><div class="formgrid"></div>';
 const dest=details.querySelector('.formgrid');for(const name of optional){const input=form.querySelector('[name="'+name+'"]');if(input)dest.appendChild(input.closest('label'));}grid.after(details);const firstReturn=Array.from(form.querySelectorAll('h3')).find(x=>x.textContent.startsWith('Partial:'));if(firstReturn){const box=document.createElement('details');box.className='optional-fields';box.innerHTML='<summary>Returned items / stock received back</summary>';firstReturn.before(box);let node=firstReturn;while(node&&!node.classList?.contains('modalfoot')&&!node.classList?.contains('actions')&&!node.textContent.startsWith('Courier:')){const next=node.nextSibling;box.append(node);node=next;}}
}
async function loadAds(){const r=await api('ads');S.ads=r;$('#content').innerHTML=`<div class="card"><h2>Ad spend & orders</h2><div class="actions"><label>From<input id="adStart" type="date" value="${e(S.adStart||S.today.slice(0,7)+'-01')}"></label><label>To<input id="adEnd" type="date" value="${e(S.adEnd||S.today)}"></label><button id="adApply">Apply</button><button id="adSync">Sync Meta</button><button id="adManual">Daily entry</button></div><p id="adNotice">Comparison only: total orders may include organic sales and delayed conversions. Missing messaging metrics are not zero.</p></div><div class="grid"><div class="card"><h3>Daily ad expense vs orders created</h3><div id="adSpendGraph"></div></div><div class="card"><h3>Ad conversations vs orders created</h3><div id="adMessageGraph"></div></div></div><div id="adRows" class="card"></div>`;$('#adApply').onclick=()=>{S.adStart=$('#adStart').value;S.adEnd=$('#adEnd').value;renderAds();};$('#adManual').onclick=()=>adModal();$('#adSync').onclick=async()=>{const b=$('#adSync');b.disabled=true;try{const out=await api('syncAds',{start:$('#adStart').value,end:$('#adEnd').value});S.ads=out.view;$('#adNotice').textContent=out.results.map(x=>x.date+': '+(x.saved?'saved':x.error)).join(' · ')||'No report rows returned; no zero amounts inferred.';renderAds();}finally{b.disabled=false;}};if(S.dashboardSeedDirty){S.dashboardSeed=await api('dashboardSeed');S.dashboardSeedDirty=false;}renderAds();}
function adModal(){modal('Daily ad entry',field('date','Date',S.today,'date','required')+field('usd','Spend USD (before VAT)','','number','min="0" step="any" required')+field('messages','Ad conversations (blank if unknown)','','number','min="0" step="1"')+field('rate','BDT per USD',S.settings.usdRate,'number','min="0.01" step="any" required')+field('vat','VAT %',S.settings.vatRate??15,'number','min="0" max="100" step="any" required')+'<p>Existing dates keep their original rate and VAT. Ads expenses for the same coverage must be reconciled before saving.</p>','Save',async p=>{S.ads=await api('saveAd',p);close();loadAds();});}
function renderAds(){const start=$('#adStart').value,end=$('#adEnd').value;if(!start||!end||end<start){toast('Choose valid dates');return;}const rows=localDates(start,end).map(date=>{const x=S.ads.rows.find(x=>x.date===date&&x.account===S.settings.metaAccount),orders=S.dashboardSeed.orders.filter(o=>o.createdDate===date).length;return {name:date,range:date,spend:x?.amount??null,messages:x?.messages??null,orders};});
 dualGraph('#adSpendGraph',rows);graph21('#adMessageGraph',rows,[{key:'messages',label:'Ad conversations',color:'green'},{key:'orders',label:'All orders created',color:'blue'}]);$('#adRows').innerHTML=table(['Date','#USD','#Rate','#VAT %','#Expense BDT','#Conversations'],S.ads.rows.filter(x=>x.date>=start&&x.date<=end).map(x=>[x.date,x.usd,x.rate,x.vat,money(x.amount),x.messages??'Unknown']));}
function dualGraph(sel,rows){const w=620,h=250,l=58,r=45,t=22,b=38,maxMoney=Math.max(1,...rows.map(x=>x.spend||0)),maxOrders=Math.max(4,...rows.map(x=>x.orders)),countMax=Math.ceil(maxOrders/4)*4,step=(w-l-r)/rows.length;let svg='',points=[];for(let i=0;i<=4;i++){const y=h-b-(h-t-b)*i/4;svg+=`<line x1="${l}" x2="${w-r}" y1="${y}" y2="${y}"/><text x="${l-6}" y="${y+4}" text-anchor="end">${shortNumber(maxMoney*i/4)}</text><text x="${w-r+7}" y="${y+4}">${countMax*i/4}</text>`;}rows.forEach((x,i)=>{const cx=l+(i+.5)*step,cy=h-b-x.orders/countMax*(h-t-b);points.push(cx+','+cy);if(x.spend!==null){const y=h-b-x.spend/maxMoney*(h-t-b);svg+=`<rect x="${cx-step*.3}" y="${y}" width="${step*.6}" height="${h-b-y}" style="fill:#00b89c"><title>${e(x.name)} · VAT-inclusive expense ${money(x.spend)}</title></rect>`;}svg+=`<circle cx="${cx}" cy="${cy}" r="3" style="fill:#397cea"><title>${e(x.name)} · All orders ${x.orders}</title></circle>`;if(i%Math.max(1,Math.ceil(rows.length/8))===0)svg+=`<text x="${cx}" y="${h-12}" text-anchor="middle">${x.name.slice(5)}</text>`;});$(sel).innerHTML=`<p class="muted">Left: expense BDT (columns) · Right: all orders (line)</p><svg viewBox="0 0 ${w} ${h}" role="img" aria-label="Daily ad expense and orders">${svg}<polyline points="${points.join(' ')}" fill="none" stroke="#397cea" stroke-width="2"/></svg>`;}
function renderHelp(){$('#content').innerHTML=`<div class="card"><h2>Quick help</h2><p><b>Import:</b> upload courier export, check the preview and confirm. Existing product assignments stay.</p><p><b>Dates:</b> delivered sales use verified delivery date; orders received use creation date. Missing delivery dates are flagged, not guessed.</p><p><b>Stock:</b> use Inventory or Stock Entry in the same spreadsheet. Enter available physical count and date; this replaces the baseline, not adds to it.</p><p><b>Money:</b> purchases reduce bank when paid; product costs reduce profit when sold. Damage is non-cash loss. Reinvestment is an ordinary business purchase, not new cash income.</p><p><b>Refresh:</b> checks Sheet data. Automatic checks run every five minutes while visible and defer during form entry. Courier and Meta calls run only on their buttons.</p><p><b>Meta:</b> configure an ads_read token, supported API version, exchange rate and VAT. Daily comparisons require account timezone Asia/Dhaka. No campaign editing is performed.</p><p><b>Closing:</b> verify all delivery dates, costs, charges and actual expenses; close the month. Backup and reopening with reason remain available.</p></div>`;}

Status updates do not supply delivery dates or revised charges.</p><div id="courierResult"></div></div>`;$('#statusBatch').onclick=async()=>{const b=$('#statusBatch');b.disabled=true;try{const r=await api('updateStatuses');$('#courierResult').textContent=r.checked+' checked · '+r.failed+' failed · '+r.remaining+' remaining. Import a recent export for amounts and delivery dates.';}finally{b.disabled=false;}};$('#checkBalance').onclick=async()=>{const b=$('#checkBalance');b.disabled=true;try{const r=await api('refreshBalance');$('#courierResult').textContent='Courier balance: '+money(r.amount)+' · '+new Date(r.updated).toLocaleString();}finally{b.disabled=false;}};}

function renderPerformance(d){
 const metrics=[{id:'sales',name:'Delivered sales'},{id:'orders',name:'Orders'},{id:'gross',name:'Gross profit'},{id:'profit',name:'Net profit / estimate'},{id:'returned',name:'Returns'},{id:'partial',name:'Partial deliveries'}];
 for(const kind of ['district','product']){const select=$('#'+kind+'Metric'),extra=kind==='product'?[{id:'qty',name:'Sold quantity'},{id:'stock',name:'Current stock'},{id:'stockValue',name:'Current stock value'}]:[];select.innerHTML=opts(metrics.concat(extra),S[kind+'Metric']||'sales');const draw=()=>{const key=select.value;S[kind+'Metric']=key;const rows=(kind==='district'?d.districts:d.products).map(x=>{const stock=d.inventory.rows.find(p=>p.id===x.id);return {...x,label:x.name,range:x.name,profit:x.net??x.projectedNet,stock:stock?.qty??null,stockValue:stock?.components?null:stock?.value??null};}).sort((a,b)=>(b[key]??-Infinity)-(a[key]??-Infinity)).slice(0,10);graph21('#'+kind+'Graph',rows,[{key,label:metrics.concat(extra).find(x=>x.id===key).name,color:['returned','partial'].includes(key)?'orange':key==='profit'?'green':'blue'}]);};select.onchange=draw;draw();}
}
