  if(d.incomplete)add('missing-profit','High','Complete actual profit inputs',`${d.incomplete} selected finalized orders have missing or conflicting profit inputs.`,'Known-profit subtotals can be misleading until missing orders are completed.','Assign products/costs, confirm charges and verify partial collection.','Orders');
  if(d.historyIncomplete)add('history-missing','Medium','Historical estimate is incomplete',`${d.historyIncomplete} historical finalized orders lack required amounts or charges.`,'Missing monetary fields are not assumed to be zero.','Use the courier export or verified manual details to complete them.','Orders');
  const noLines=selected.filter(o=>o.date>=V2.cutoff&&!(o.lines||[]).length).length;if(noLines)add('assign','Medium','Assign products to imported orders',`${noLines} selected September-onward orders have no product lines.`,'Product/category filters cannot include unidentified items.','Add product or combo quantities without changing verified courier order totals.','Orders');
  d.expenses.rows.filter(x=>x.actual>x.projection&&x.projection>0).forEach(x=>add('budget:'+x.month+':'+x.category,'Medium','Review an expense above budget',`${x.category} in ${x.month}: actual ${fmt(x.actual)}, accrued budget ${fmt(x.projection)}.`,'A one-time purchase or recurring increase may explain the difference.','Check coverage dates and review whether the higher cost was planned.','Expenses'));
  if(!d.ad.verified)add('ad-tracking','Low','Advertising performance needs attribution','No usable tracked-sales comparison is available for this selection.','Total business sales must not be assumed to come from ads.','Set the expense marketplace and mark orders as ad-attributed only when verified.','Expenses');
  else d.ad.rows.filter(x=>x.cost>x.sales).forEach(x=>add('ad-spend:'+x.name,'High','Ad spend exceeds tracked sales',`${x.name}: ad cost ${fmt(x.cost)}, attributed sales ${fmt(x.sales)}.`,'This comparison may also be affected by missing attribution or conversion delays.','Verify attribution and period coverage before changing the campaign budget.','Expenses'));
  if(d.cash.courier>0)add('courier-ready','Low','Courier funds are available to request',`The last verified Steadfast balance is ${fmt(d.cash.courier)}.`,'This is requestable courier money, not cash already in the bank.','Refresh the balance and consider a payment request if upcoming bills need funding.','Purchases & Bank');
  if(d.cash.pending>0)add('transfer-pending','Medium','Confirm pending courier transfers',`${fmt(d.cash.pending)} is recorded as requested but not received.`,'Requested funds must not be counted again as a new income or bank receipt.','Match the payment reference and confirm the actual bank receipt.','Purchases & Bank');
  if(d.cash.bank===null)add('opening-bank','Medium','Enter the opening bank balance','Bank tracking has no opening balance/date yet.','Historical sales cannot determine how much money is currently in your bank.','Enter a verified opening balance, then record subsequent cash movements.','Purchases & Bank');
  if(d.cash.bank!==null&&d.cash.upcomingTotal>d.cash.bank)add('bills','High','Upcoming bills exceed bank funds',`Outstanding bills total ${fmt(d.cash.upcomingTotal)}; recorded bank balance is ${fmt(d.cash.bank)}.`,'Stock purchases, salaries and other due payments may compete for the same funds.','Review due dates and courier funds before committing to another purchase.','Purchases & Bank');
  if(d.cash.bank!==null&&d.cash.bank<s.cashReserve)add('reserve','High','Bank balance is below your reserve',`Recorded bank balance ${fmt(d.cash.bank)} is below the saved reserve of ${fmt(s.cashReserve)}.`,'A new paid purchase could leave too little money for essential expenses.','Review immediate payments and consider a smaller purchase.','Purchases & Bank');
  if(d.cash.untrackedExpenses)add('legacy-payments','Medium','Review payment tracking for old expenses',`${d.cash.untrackedExpenses} expense records do not yet have payment tracking.`,'Opening cash should already include old payments; adding them again would double-count.','Verify which bills remain unpaid and enter only new payments after your opening balance.','Expenses');
  if(d.cash.purchases.some(x=>!x.receivedDate))add('purchase-receipt','Medium','Confirm purchased stock receipts','Some purchase records are still awaiting stock receipt.','A paid purchase does not mean goods are physically available to sell.','Record receipt on arrival; do not count these units as available before then.','Purchases & Bank');
  const hour=Number(Utilities.formatDate(new Date(),V2.tz,'H')),due=hour>=s.reminderHour;
  if(due&&!checks.some(c=>c.id==='orders:'+today))add('orders:'+today,'High',"Confirm today's order update",'Today’s order data has not been marked complete.','An empty list could mean no orders or an unfinished update.','Import/sync and review today’s orders, or confirm No orders today.','Orders','orders');
  const coveredAds=read_('Expenses').some(x=>!x.void&&/^(ad|ads|advertising)$/i.test(x.category)&&(x.periodStart||x.date)<=today&&(x.periodEnd||x.date)>=today&&x.coverageComplete!==false);
  if(due&&!coveredAds&&!checks.some(c=>c.id==='ads:'+today))add('ads:'+today,'Medium',"Confirm today's ad cost",'No complete ad-cost entry or zero-spend confirmation covers today.','Estimated profit will continue to use applicable projections.','Enter actual ad cost or confirm no ad spending today.','Expenses','ads');
  if(due&&!checks.some(c=>c.kind==='bank'&&c.date>=addDays_(today,-6)))add('bank:'+today,'Medium','Reconcile the bank balance','The app balance has not been marked reconciled during the last seven days.','Fees, deposits or payments outside the app can make its balance differ from the bank.','Compare with your bank and record missing transactions before confirming.','Purchases & Bank','bank');
  const earliest=orders.filter(o=>o.date>=V2.cutoff).map(o=>o.date).sort()[0]||V2.cutoff;
  monthsIn_(earliest,today).filter(m=>monthEnd_(m)<=today&&!isClosed_(m+'-01')).forEach(m=>add('close:'+m,'High','Close the month: '+m,`The last day of ${m} has arrived/passed and its accounts remain open.`,'Actual net profit stays blank until order outcomes and actual expenses are complete.','Review missing inputs and use Close Month; bank settlement is not required.','Month Closing'));
  if(d.orders<min)add('sample','Low','Use caution with this small sample',`Only ${d.orders} orders match the current selection.`,'A few orders can produce large percentages without showing a stable pattern.','Widen the time range before making pricing, advertising or district restrictions.');
  const compare=d.growth.previous;if(d.growth.sales===null)add('growth-base','Low','Growth has no valid comparison base',`The comparison period ${compare.start} to ${compare.end} has zero recorded sales.`,'Percentage growth from zero is undefined.','Compare absolute sales amounts or select a period with a usable baseline.');
  return items.sort((a,b)=>['High','Medium','Low'].indexOf(a.priority)-['High','Medium','Low'].indexOf(b.priority));
}


/** 2.2: local-first views, explicit imports, dated sales, stock edits and optional read-only ads. */
function touch_(){props_().setProperty('SHD_REV',uid_());}
function revision_(){return {revision:props_().getProperty('SHD_REV')||'',today:today_()};}
function basePut_(n,x){const r=basePutLegacy_(n,x);if(!['Log','Stage'].includes(n))touch_();return r;}
function writeAll_(n,x){const r=writeAllLegacy_(n,x);if(!['Log','Stage'].includes(n))touch_();return r;}
function deliveryDay_(o){
  const v=str_(o.deliveredAt);if(!v)return '';try{if(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(v)){if(/(?:Z|[+-]\d{2}:?\d{2})$/.test(v))return Utilities.formatDate(new Date(v),V2.tz,'yyyy-MM-dd');return day_(v.slice(0,10));}}catch(e){}return '';
}
function eventDate_(o){return ['Delivered','Partial'].includes(o.status)?deliveryDay_(o):o.date;}
function importRecord_(r){const o=importRecord_Legacy(r);o.deliveredAt=str_(r['Delivery Time']||r['Delivered At']||r['Delivery Date']);o.returnCharge=num_(r['Return Charge'])??0;return o;}
function importMerged_(old,n){const o=importMerged_Legacy(old,n);for(const k of ['total','cod','shippingCharge','codCharge'])if(num_(n[k])===null&&num_(old[k])!==null)o[k]=old[k];if(!n.deliveredAt)o.deliveredAt=old.deliveredAt||'';if(!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(n.raw||{},'Return Charge'))o.returnCharge=old.returnCharge??n.returnCharge;return o;}
function saveOrder_(p){
 const old=read_('Orders').find(o=>o.id===p.id);const dt=p.deliveredAt===undefined?old?.deliveredAt||'':str_(p.deliveredAt);
 if(dt){const day=day_(dt);if(day>today_()||day<p.date)throw Error('Delivery date must be between order date and today');guardPeriod_(day,day);}
 const r=saveOrder_Legacy(p);r.deliveredAt=dt;put_('Orders',r);return r;
}
function analytics_(p,all,s){
 const orders=all||read_('Orders'),event=orders.filter(o=>eventDate_(o)).map(o=>({...o,originalDate:o.originalDate||o.date,date:eventDate_(o)}));
 const d=analytics_Legacy(p,event,s);includeCrossCutoffEstimates_(d);if(d.incomplete||d.historyIncomplete){d.displayNet=null;d.displayMargin=null;d.projectedNet=null;if(d.incomplete)d.gross=null;}
 d.months.forEach(m=>{if(m.incomplete||m.historyIncomplete){m.projectedNet=null;m.net=null;}});return d;
}
function dashboardSeed_(){const d=dashboardSeed_Legacy(),all=read_('Orders');const by=new Map(all.map(o=>[o.id,o]));d.orders.forEach(o=>{const raw=by.get(o.id);o.createdDate=o.date;o.eventDate=eventDate_(raw);o.deliveredAt=deliveryDay_(raw);o.cod=num_(raw.cod);o.total=num_(raw.total);});d.revision=revision_().revision;d.targets=read_('Settings').filter(x=>x.id.startsWith('target:'));return d;}
function bootstrap_(){return {version:V2.version,today:today_(),settings:getSettings_(),catalog:catalog_(),combos:read_('Combos'),dashboardSeed:dashboardSeed_(),migrated:!!props_().getProperty('SHD_HISTORY_IMPORTED'),revision:revision_().revision};}
function monthReview_(p){const r=monthReview_Legacy(p),start=p.month+'-01';const all=read_('Orders');const relevant=all.filter(o=>((eventDate_(o)>=start&&eventDate_(o)<=r.end)||(['Delivered','Partial'].includes(o.status)&&!deliveryDay_(o)&&o.date<=r.end))&&o.status!=='Draft');r.incomplete=relevant.filter(o=>!finance_(o,15).known||o.approvalConflict||!eventDate_(o)).map(o=>({id:o.id,name:o.name,status:o.status,reason:!eventDate_(o)?'Delivery date missing':finance_(o,15).reason||'Approval conflict'}));r.canClose=today_()>=r.end&&!r.incomplete.length;return r;}
function getSettings_(){const s=getSettings_Legacy();for(const k of ['geminiModel','aiEnabled','aiLimit','aiDataMode','hasGemini','suggestionLimit'])delete s[k];return {...s,metaAccount:s.metaAccount||'1430584315368882',metaVersion:s.metaVersion||'',usdRate:s.usdRate??'',vatRate:s.vatRate??15,hasMeta:!!props_().getProperty('SHD_META_TOKEN')};}
function saveSettings_(p){
 const s=settings_();for(const k of ['businessName','metaAccount','metaVersion'])if(p[k]!==undefined)s[k]=str_(p[k]);
 if(s.metaAccount&&!/^\d+$/.test(s.metaAccount))throw Error('Meta account ID must contain digits');
 if(s.metaVersion&&!/^v\d+\.0$/.test(s.metaVersion))throw Error('Use the supported Graph API version shown in your Meta app, for example v23.0');
 for(const k of ['usdRate','vatRate','deadDays'])if(p[k]!==undefined&&p[k]!=='')s[k]=amount_(p[k],k);
 if(s.vatRate>100)throw Error('VAT rate cannot exceed 100');if(s.deadDays<1)throw Error('Dead stock days must be positive');
 if(p.logo!==undefined){if(p.logo&&!/^data:image\/(png|jpeg|webp);base64,[A-Za-z0-9+/=]+$/.test(p.logo))throw Error('Use PNG/JPEG/WebP logo');if(p.logo.length>28000)throw Error('Logo too large');s.logo=p.logo;}
 for(const [k,v] of [['sfKey','SHD_SF_KEY'],['sfSecret','SHD_SF_SECRET'],['metaToken','SHD_META_TOKEN']])if(str_(p[k]))props_().setProperty(v,str_(p[k]).replace(/\s/g,''));
 s.aiEnabled=false;s.aiDataMode='off';s.syncEnabled=false;s.profitRate=15;put_('Settings',s);removeCourierSchedule_();return getSettings_();
}
function saveTarget_(p){const month=day_(p.month+'-01').slice(0,7);put_('Settings',{id:'target:'+month,month,amount:amount_(p.amount,'Monthly sales target')});return dashboardSeed_();}
function setupSimple_(){
 const db=db_();let tab=db.getSheetByName('Stock Entry');if(!tab){tab=db.insertSheet('Stock Entry');tab.getRange(1,1,1,6).setValues([['Product ID','Product','Counted available','Count date','Note','Result']]);tab.setFrozenRows(1);tab.hideColumns(1);}
 mirrorStock_();const triggers=ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers();if(!triggers.some(t=>t.getHandlerFunction()==='stockSheetEdited'))ScriptApp.newTrigger('stockSheetEdited').forSpreadsheet(db).onEdit().create();touch_();
}
function stockSheetEdited(e){if(!e||!e.range)return;const n=e.range.getSheet().getName();if(n==='Stock Entry'||n==='Products'||n.startsWith('SHD_'))touch_();}
function stockFingerprint_(row){return JSON.stringify([str_(row[0]),row[2],sheetDay_(row[3]),str_(row[4])]);}
function sheetDay_(v){return v instanceof Date?Utilities.formatDate(v,V2.tz,'yyyy-MM-dd'):str_(v);}
function mirrorStock_(){
 const tab=db_().getSheetByName('Stock Entry');if(!tab)return;
 const rows=tab.getLastRow()>1?tab.getRange(2,1,tab.getLastRow()-1,6).getValues():[],checks=read_('Checks'),view=stockView_();let changed=false;
 for(const p of view.products){let i=rows.findIndex(x=>str_(x[0])===p.id);const old=i>=0?rows[i]:null,ci=checks.findIndex(c=>c.id==='stock-sheet:'+p.id),check=checks[ci];if(old&&check&&check.fingerprint!==stockFingerprint_(old))continue;
 const row=[p.id,safeCell_(p.name),p.qty,today_(),'',p.verified?'Current available':'Provisional — enter physical count'];if(old&&JSON.stringify(old)===JSON.stringify(row))continue;
 if(i<0)rows.push(row);else rows[i]=row;const record={id:'stock-sheet:'+p.id,fingerprint:stockFingerprint_(row)};if(ci<0)checks.push(record);else checks[ci]=record;changed=true;
 }
 if(changed&&rows.length){tab.getRange(2,1,rows.length,6).setValues(rows);writeAll_('Checks',checks);}
}
function applyStockEntries_(){
 const tab=db_().getSheetByName('Stock Entry');if(!tab||tab.getLastRow()<2)return;
 const rows=tab.getRange(2,1,tab.getLastRow()-1,6).getValues(),checks=read_('Checks');let changed=false;
 for(let i=0;i<rows.length;i++){const row=rows[i],id=str_(row[0]);if(!id)continue;const fp=stockFingerprint_(row),prev=checks.find(c=>c.id==='stock-sheet:'+id);if(prev?.fingerprint===fp)continue;
 try{saveStock_Legacy({sku:id,mode:'baseline',qty:row[2],date:sheetDay_(row[3]),note:str_(row[4]),requestId:'sheet:'+uid_()});basePut_('Checks',{id:'stock-sheet:'+id,fingerprint:fp});tab.getRange(i+2,6).setValue('Saved '+now_());changed=true;}catch(err){tab.getRange(i+2,6).setValue('Not saved: '+err.message);}
 }
 if(changed)touch_();
}
function saveStock_(p){applyStockEntries_();const r=saveStock_Legacy(p);mirrorStock_();return r;}
function saveDamage_(p){
 const id='damage:'+str_(p.requestId);if(!p.requestId)throw Error('Damage request ID required');const date=day_(p.date);guardPeriod_(date,date);if(date>today_())throw Error('Damage cannot be future dated');
 const product=products_().find(x=>x.id===p.sku);if(!product||num_(product.cost)===null)throw Error('Select a physical product with purchase cost');
 const qty=amount_(p.qty,'Damaged quantity');if(qty<=0)throw Error('Quantity must be positive');const prior=read_('Expenses').find(x=>x.id===id);const stock=read_('Stock').find(x=>x.id===p.sku);if(!stock)throw Error('Set available stock first');
 const moved=(stock.movements||[]).some(m=>m.id===id);if(!moved&&stockView_().products.find(x=>x.id===p.sku).qty<qty)throw Error('Insufficient available stock');if(date<stock.date)throw Error('Damage predates stock baseline');
 if(prior&&(prior.damageSku!==p.sku||prior.damageQty!==qty||prior.date!==date))throw Error('This damage request was already used');
 if(!prior)put_('Expenses',{id,date,periodStart:date,periodEnd:date,category:'Damage',amount:qty*product.cost,damageSku:p.sku,damageQty:qty,nonCash:true,coverageComplete:true,payments:[],note:str_(p.note)});
 if(!moved){stock.movements=(stock.movements||[]).concat({id,date,qty:-qty,note:'Damage: '+str_(p.note)});put_('Stock',stock);}
 mirrorStock_();return {stock:stockView_(),expense:read_('Expenses').find(x=>x.id===id)};
}
function saveExpense_(p){const old=read_('Expenses').find(x=>x.id===p.id);if(old?.nonCash||old?.metaManaged)throw Error('Use the original Damage or Ads entry to prevent double counting');if(/^(ads?|advertising)$/i.test(str_(p.category)))p.category='Ads';return saveExpense_Legacy(p);}
function guidanceView_(p){
 const d=dashboard_(p,true),items=[];const add=(title,what,why,next,page)=>items.push({title,what,why,next,page});
 const missingDate=read_('Orders').filter(o=>['Delivered','Partial'].includes(o.status)&&!deliveryDay_(o));
 if(missingDate.length)add('Delivery dates are missing',missingDate.length+' delivered/partial orders have no verified delivery date.','They cannot be placed in the correct sales day or month.','Import the delivery-date column or enter the verified date in the order.','Orders');
 if(d.incomplete)add('Complete profit inputs',d.incomplete+' finalized orders have incomplete costs, charges or approval information.','A complete profit figure cannot be calculated from those records.','Assign products, verify courier charges and review partial collections.','Orders');
 if(d.inventory.low)add('Review low stock',d.inventory.low+' products/combos are at or below the reorder point.','Availability may limit new orders.','Check physical quantities and buy only the items you need.','Inventory');
 if(d.inventory.dead)add('Review slow stock',d.inventory.dead+' items hold '+d.inventory.deadValue+' BDT without recent recorded sales.','Cash remains tied up in this stock.','Check demand and consider a suitable bundle or promotion.','Inventory');
 if(d.problemRate>10)add('Review returns and cancellations',d.problemRate.toFixed(1)+'% of finalized selected orders were returned/cancelled.','The count alone does not identify the cause.','Review addresses, customer confirmation and product issues before changing your process.','Orders');
 if(d.growth.sales!==null&&d.growth.sales<0)add('Delivered sales are lower',Math.abs(d.growth.sales).toFixed(1)+'% below the comparison period.','Missing dates or late delivery can affect this comparison.','Check data completeness and compare orders received alongside delivered sales.','Dashboard');
 if(d.cash.bank===null)add('Enter opening bank balance','Bank balance is not initialized.','Courier sales do not tell us your current bank funds.','Enter a verified bank balance and its starting date.','Purchases & Bank');
 if(!d.cash.balanceUpdated)add('Refresh courier balance','No courier balance has been fetched.','Missing is not zero.','Use Check balance in Courier when you need the latest amount.','Courier');
 const prev=addDays_(today_().slice(0,7)+'-01',-1).slice(0,7);if(prev>='2026-09'&&!isClosed_(prev+'-01'))add('Review the previous month',prev+' is not closed.','Closing confirms reconciled actual expenses and delivery records.','Complete the month review and close when all required data is entered.','Month Closing');
 return {filter:d.filter,suggestions:items,generatedAt:now_()};
}
function adView_(){return {rows:read_('AdDaily').sort((a,b)=>b.date.localeCompare(a.date)),settings:getSettings_()};}
function saveAd_(p){
 const date=day_(p.date);guardPeriod_(date,date);if(date>today_())throw Error('Ad spend cannot be future dated');const s=getSettings_(),account=str_(p.account||s.metaAccount);if(!/^\d+$/.test(account))throw Error('Ad account required');
 const id=account+':'+date,old=read_('AdDaily').find(x=>x.id===id),usd=amount_(p.usd,'USD spend'),rate=old?old.rate:amount_(p.rate??s.usdRate,'USD to BDT rate'),vat=old?old.vat:amount_(p.vat??s.vatRate,'VAT rate');if(rate<=0||vat>100)throw Error('Check exchange/VAT rate');
 const expenseId='meta:'+id,others=read_('Expenses').filter(x=>x.id!==expenseId&&!x.void&&/^(ads?|advertising)$/i.test(x.category)&&(x.periodStart||x.date)<=date&&(x.periodEnd||x.date)>=date);
 if(others.length)throw Error('An Ads expense already covers '+date+'. Reconcile it before importing Meta spend to avoid double counting.');
 const messages=p.messages===null||p.messages===undefined||p.messages===''?null:amount_(p.messages,'Conversations');if(messages!==null&&!Number.isInteger(messages))throw Error('Conversation count must be a whole number');
 const row={id,account,date,usd,rate,vat,amount:Math.round(usd*rate*(1+vat/100)*100)/100,messages,origin:p.origin||'Manual',updated:now_()};
 const expense=read_('Expenses').find(x=>x.id===expenseId);if((expense?.payments||[]).reduce((a,x)=>a+x.amount,0)>row.amount)throw Error('Updated spend is below recorded payments; reconcile first');
 put_('Expenses',{id:expenseId,date,periodStart:date,periodEnd:date,category:'Ads',marketplace:'Facebook',amount:row.amount,coverageComplete:true,payments:expense?.payments||[],metaManaged:true,note:'USD '+usd+' × '+rate+' + '+vat+'% VAT'});put_('AdDaily',row);return adView_();
}
function metaGet_(path,params){
 const s=getSettings_(),token=props_().getProperty('SHD_META_TOKEN');if(!token||!s.metaVersion)throw Error('Save Meta token and a supported Graph API version in Settings');
 const url='https://graph.facebook.com/'+s.metaVersion+'/'+path+'?'+Object.entries(params||{}).map(([k,v])=>encodeURIComponent(k)+'='+encodeURIComponent(v)).join('&');
 const r=UrlFetchApp.fetch(url,{headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+token},muteHttpExceptions:true});let j;try{j=JSON.parse(r.getContentText());}catch(e){throw Error('Meta returned an invalid response');}if(r.getResponseCode()>=300||j.error)throw Error('Meta request failed (code '+(j.error?.code||r.getResponseCode())+'). Check token, ads_read permission and API version.');return j;
}
function syncAds_(p){
 const start=day_(p.start),end=day_(p.end);if(end<start||daysBetween_(start,end)>31||end>today_())throw Error('Sync a past/current range of up to 31 days');guardPeriod_(start,end);
 const s=getSettings_();if(!(Number(s.usdRate)>0))throw Error('Set USD to BDT rate first');const account=metaGet_('act_'+s.metaAccount,{fields:'currency,timezone_name'});if(account.currency!=='USD')throw Error('This integration expects a USD account');if(account.timezone_name!==V2.tz)throw Error('Meta account timezone is '+account.timezone_name+'. Use daily manual entries aligned to Dhaka time; automatic sync is blocked to avoid misleading comparisons.');
 const collected=[];let after='';for(let page=0;page<20;page++){const params={fields:'date_start,date_stop,spend,actions',time_increment:1,level:'account',time_range:JSON.stringify({since:start,until:end}),limit:100};if(after)params.after=after;const r=metaGet_('act_'+s.metaAccount+'/insights',params);if(!Array.isArray(r.data))throw Error('Meta insights missing data');collected.push(...r.data);if(!r.paging?.next){after='';break;}after=r.paging?.cursors?.after;if(!after)throw Error('Unsupported Meta pagination');}if(after)throw Error('Meta response exceeded safe page limit; narrow date range');
 const rows=collected.map(x=>{if(x.date_start!==x.date_stop||x.date_start<start||x.date_start>end)throw Error('Unexpected Meta report dates');const action=(x.actions||[]).find(a=>a.action_type==='onsite_conversion.messaging_conversation_started_7d');return {date:x.date_start,usd:x.spend,messages:action?Number(action.value):null,origin:'Meta API'};});
 // Only explicit API rows are imported; absent days and missing message metrics are not guessed zero.
 const results=[];for(const row of rows){try{saveAd_(row);results.push({date:row.date,saved:true});}catch(e){results.push({date:row.date,saved:false,error:e.message});}}return {results,view:adView_(),note:'VAT included. Missing conversation metrics stay unknown. This is comparison, not order attribution.'};
}
function savePurchase_(p){
 // Initialize receipt IDs before adding a new same-day purchase to avoid absorbing it into a prior count.
 for(const b of read_('Stock'))if(!Array.isArray(b.purchaseIds)){b.purchaseIds=read_('Purchases').filter(x=>x.receivedDate&&x.receivedDate<=b.date).map(x=>x.id);put_('Stock',b);}
 const r=savePurchase_Legacy(p);mirrorStock_();return r;
}
function extraCourier_(p){if(p.kind!=='balance')throw Error('Use the Steadfast portal for this operation');return refreshBalance_();}

function saveBudget_(p){if(/^(ads?|advertising)$/i.test(str_(p.category)))p.category='Ads';return saveBudget_Legacy(p);}
function expenseTotals_(expenses,budgets,start,end,today){const canonical=x=>({...x,category:/^(ads?|advertising)$/i.test(str_(x.category))?'Ads':x.category});return expenseTotals_Legacy(expenses.map(canonical),budgets.map(canonical),start,end,today);}

function includeCrossCutoffEstimates_(d){
 const cross=d.months.filter(m=>!m.historical&&m.estimate!==0),extra=cross.reduce((a,m)=>a+m.estimate,0);
 if(cross.length){d.displayNet+=extra;d.projectedNet=d.projectedNet===null?null:d.projectedNet+extra;d.net=null;d.displayNetLabel='Includes historical estimates';d.displayMargin=d.sales?d.displayNet/d.sales*100:null;cross.forEach(m=>{m.projectedNet+=m.estimate;m.net=null;});}
}

function importCommit_(p){
 const existing=read_('Orders'),maps=importMatchMaps_(existing);
 for(const n of read_('Stage')){const hit=findImportMatch_(n,maps);if(hit.problem)throw Error('Resolve import problems: '+hit.problem);const next=hit.order?importMerged_(hit.order,n):n;if(hit.order&&!importDiff_(hit.order,next))continue;
 for(const o of [hit.order,next].filter(Boolean)){guardPeriod_(o.date,o.date);const delivery=deliveryDay_(o);if(delivery)guardPeriod_(delivery,delivery);}}
 return importCommitBase_(p);
}



/** Domain logic. Money is BDT; date boundaries are Asia/Dhaka. */
function status_(s){const k=str_(s).toLowerCase().replace(/[ _-]+/g,' ');const map={'delivered':'Delivered','partially delivered':'Partial','partial delivered':'Partial','partial':'Partial','cancelled':'Cancelled','returned':'Returned','pending':'Pending','hold':'Hold','in review':'In Review','no defined':'Unknown','unknown':'Unknown','draft':'Draft','confirmed':'Confirmed','shipped':'Shipped','delivered approval pending':'Delivered Approval Pending','partial delivered approval pending':'Partial Approval Pending','cancelled approval pending':'Cancelled Approval Pending','unknown approval pending':'Unknown'};return map[k]||'Unknown';}
/** Deterministic address hints; ambiguity stays blank and is not represented as verified geography. */
function districtHint_(address){const aliases='Dhaka:ঢাকা|Gazipur:গাজীপুর|Narayanganj:নারায়ণগঞ্জ,নারায়ণগঞ্জ|Narsingdi:নরসিংদী|Manikganj:মানিকগঞ্জ|Munshiganj:মুন্সিগঞ্জ|Tangail:টাঙ্গাইল|Kishoreganj:কিশোরগঞ্জ|Faridpur:ফরিদপুর|Gopalganj:গোপালগঞ্জ|Madaripur:মাদারীপুর|Rajbari:রাজবাড়ী,রাজবাড়ী|Shariatpur:শরীয়তপুর,শরীয়তপুর|Chattogram:চট্টগ্রাম,Chittagong|Cox’s Bazar:কক্সবাজার,Coxs Bazar,Cox Bazar|Cumilla:কুমিল্লা,Comilla|Brahmanbaria:ব্রাহ্মণবাড়িয়া,ব্রাহ্মণবাড়িয়া|Chandpur:চাঁদপুর|Feni:ফেনী|Noakhali:নোয়াখালী,নোয়াখালী|Lakshmipur:লক্ষ্মীপুর|Rangamati:রাঙ্গামাটি|Khagrachhari:খাগড়াছড়ি,খাগড়াছড়ি|Bandarban:বান্দরবান|Rajshahi:রাজশাহী|Bogura:বগুড়া,বগুড়া,Bogra|Pabna:পাবনা|Sirajganj:সিরাজগঞ্জ|Naogaon:নওগাঁ|Natore:নাটোর|Joypurhat:জয়পুরহাট,জয়পুরহাট|Chapainawabganj:চাঁপাইনবাবগঞ্জ|Khulna:খুলনা|Jashore:যশোর,Jessore|Satkhira:সাতক্ষীরা|Bagerhat:বাগেরহাট|Kushtia:কুষ্টিয়া,কুষ্টিয়া|Jhenaidah:ঝিনাইদহ|Magura:মাগুরা|Narail:নড়াইল,নড়াইল|Chuadanga:চুয়াডাঙ্গা,চুয়াডাঙ্গা|Meherpur:মেহেরপুর|Barishal:বরিশাল,Barisal|Patuakhali:পটুয়াখালী,পটুয়াখালী|Bhola:ভোলা|Pirojpur:পিরোজপুর|Barguna:বরগুনা|Jhalokathi:ঝালকাঠি|Sylhet:সিলেট|Moulvibazar:মৌলভীবাজার|Habiganj:হবিগঞ্জ|Sunamganj:সুনামগঞ্জ|Rangpur:রংপুর|Dinajpur:দিনাজপুর|Kurigram:কুড়িগ্রাম,কুড়িগ্রাম|Gaibandha:গাইবান্ধা|Nilphamari:নীলফামারী|Lalmonirhat:লালমনিরহাট|Panchagarh:পঞ্চগড়,পঞ্চগড়|Thakurgaon:ঠাকুরগাঁও|Mymensingh:ময়মনসিংহ,ময়মনসিংহ|Jamalpur:জামালপুর|Sherpur:শেরপুর|Netrokona:নেত্রকোনা';const a=str_(address).toLowerCase();const candidates=[];aliases.split('|').forEach(entry=>{const [name,other]=entry.split(':');const terms=[name].concat(other.split(','));if(terms.some(term=>{const t=term.toLowerCase();return /[a-z]/.test(t)?new RegExp('(^|[^a-z])'+t.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&')+'([^a-z]|$)').test(a):a.includes(t);}))candidates.push(name);});return candidates.length===1?candidates[0]:'';}

function attachCourier_(p){const o=read_('Orders').find(x=>x.id===p.id);if(!o)throw Error('Order not found');const cid=str_(p.cid),tracking=str_(p.tracking);if(!/^\d+$/.test(cid)&&!tracking)throw Error('Consignment ID or tracking code required');if(!p.confirm)throw Error('Confirm this is the exact saved order');if(read_('Orders').some(x=>x.id!==o.id&&((cid&&x.cid===cid)||(tracking&&x.tracking===tracking))))throw Error('Already linked to another order');const response=sf_(cid?'/status_by_cid/'+encodeURIComponent(cid):'/status_by_trackingcode/'+encodeURIComponent(tracking));if(!response.delivery_status)throw Error('Courier did not verify the identifier');o.cid=cid;o.tracking=tracking;o.status=status_(response.delivery_status);o.bookingState='booked';o.lastSync=now_();o.revision++;put_('Orders',o);return o;}
function final_(o){return ['Delivered','Partial','Cancelled','Returned'].includes(o.status);}
function components_(lines){const out={};(lines||[]).forEach(l=>(l.components||[{sku:l.sku,qty:1}]).forEach(c=>{out[c.sku]=(out[c.sku]||0)+c.qty*l.qty;}));return out;}
function used_(o){if(o.date<V2.cutoff||o.status==='Draft'||['Unknown','In Review'].includes(o.status)&&!o.stockReserved)return {};const lines=o.lines||[];const all=components_(lines);if(o.status==='Cancelled'&&!o.dispatched)return {};const restored=o.stockReturns||{};Object.keys(all).forEach(k=>all[k]=Math.max(0,all[k]-(restored[k]||0)));return all;}
function stocksBase_(products,orders,records){const out={};products.forEach(p=>{const b=records.find(x=>x.id===p.id);const base=b||{qty:p.legacyStock,date:today_(),included:{}};let q=base.qty+(base.movements||[]).reduce((a,m)=>a+m.qty,0);orders.forEach(o=>{const u=used_(o)[p.id]||0,inc=(base.included||{})[o.id];if(inc===null){q+=(o.stockReturns||{})[p.id]||0;}else if(inc!==undefined)q-=u-inc;else if(o.date>=base.date)q-=u;});out[p.id]={qty:q,date:base.date,verified:!!b&&!b.provisional,base:base.qty};});return out;}
function stockView_(){const p=products_(),o=read_('Orders'),s=stocks_(p,o,read_('Stock'));return {products:p.map(x=>Object.assign(x,s[x.id])),records:read_('Stock')};}
function saveStockBase_(p){const sku=str_(p.sku);if(!products_().some(x=>x.id===sku))throw Error('Unknown SKU');const records=read_('Stock');let b=records.find(x=>x.id===sku);if(p.mode==='baseline'){const date=day_(p.date);if(date>today_())throw Error('Stock count cannot be future dated');const included={};read_('Orders').filter(o=>o.date>=V2.cutoff&&o.date<=date).forEach(o=>{included[o.id]=o.imported&&!o.lines.length?null:used_(o)[sku]||0;});b={id:sku,date,qty:amount_(p.qty,'Stock'),included,movements:[],updated:now_()};}else{if(!b)throw Error('Set dated counted available stock first');const date=day_(p.date);if(date<b.date)throw Error('Movement predates current stock count');let q=amount_(p.qty,'Quantity');if(p.mode==='out')q=-q;b.movements=(b.movements||[]).concat({id:p.requestId||uid_(),date,qty:q,note:str_(p.note)}).filter((x,i,a)=>a.findIndex(y=>y.id===x.id)===i);if(stocks_(products_(),read_('Orders'),records.map(x=>x.id===sku?b:x))[sku].qty<0)throw Error('Insufficient stock');}put_('Stock',b);log_('Stock',sku+' '+p.mode);return stockView_();}
function saveProductBase_(p){const name=str_(p.name);if(!name)throw Error('Product name required');const s=db_().getSheetByName('Products'),a=s.getDataRange().getValues(),h=a[0],idx=h.indexOf('SKU');let id=str_(p.id)||'SKU-'+uid_().slice(0,8);let i=a.findIndex((r,j)=>j>0&&str_(r[idx])===id);if(i<0&&p.edit)throw Error('Product not found');const r=i<0?h.map(()=> ''):a[i];const v={'SKU':id,'Product Name':name,'Category':str_(p.category)||'Other','Cost Price':amount_(p.cost,'Cost'),'Sales Price':amount_(p.price,'Price'),'Reorder Point':amount_(p.reorder||0,'Reorder point'),'Status':p.active===false?'Inactive':'Active'};Object.keys(v).forEach(k=>{if(h.indexOf(k)>=0)r[h.indexOf(k)]=safeCell_(v[k]);});s.getRange(i<0?a.length+1:i+1,1,1,h.length).setValues([r]);if(requestReads_)delete requestReads_.baseProducts;return products_();}

/** Steadfast export import. CSV prepared from original XLSX retains all 18 fields. */
function importRecord_Legacy(r){const cid=str_(r['Order ID']),tracking=str_(r['Tracking Code']);if(!cid&&!tracking)throw Error('Consignment ID and tracking code both missing');const date=day_(r['Created At']);const delivered=status_(r['Delivery Status']),approval=status_(r['Approval Status']);return {id:cid?'SF-'+cid:'SF-T-'+tracking,cid,tracking,invoice:str_(r.Invoice),date,name:str_(r['Recipient Name'])||'Guest',phone:phone_(r['Recipient Phone']),address:str_(r['Recipient Address']),district:str_(r.District)||districtHint_(r['Recipient Address']),districtInferred:!str_(r.District),thana:str_(r.Thana),total:num_(r['COD Amount']),cod:num_(r['COD Amount']),shippingCharge:num_(r['Shipping Charge']),codCharge:num_(r['COD Charge']),returnCharge:0,status:delivered,approval:str_(r['Approval Status']),approvalConflict:delivered!==approval,courierPayment:str_(r['Payment Status']),source:'Old Data',paymentStatus:'Unknown',paymentMethod:'Cash on Delivery',note:str_(r.Note),createdAt:str_(r['Created At']),deliveredAt:str_(r['Delivery Time']),approvedAt:str_(r['Approved At']),lines:[],stockReturns:{},stockReserved:true,dispatched:['Delivered','Partial','Returned'].includes(delivered),revision:1,imported:true,amountVerified:false,itemsVerified:false,raw:r};}
function importMatchMaps_(existing){const byCid={},byTracking={},byId={};existing.forEach(o=>{if(o.cid)byCid[o.cid]=o;if(o.tracking)byTracking[o.tracking]=o;byId[o.id]=o;});return {byCid,byTracking,byId};}
function findImportMatch_(n,maps){const a=n.cid?maps.byCid[n.cid]:null,b=n.tracking?maps.byTracking[n.tracking]:null,c=maps.byId[n.id]||null;const found=[a,b,c].filter(Boolean),ids=[...new Set(found.map(x=>x.id))];if(ids.length>1)return {problem:'Consignment ID / tracking code match different saved orders.'};return {order:found[0]||null};}
function importMerged_Legacy(old,n){
  const rawDate=Date.parse(n.raw?.['Exported At']||''),synced=Date.parse(old.lastSync||''),keepStatus=!!old.lastSync&&(!rawDate||rawDate<synced),previousStatus=old.status;
  const preserve={id:old.id,lines:old.lines||[],stockReturns:old.stockReturns||{},district:old.district||n.district,thana:old.thana||n.thana,amountVerified:!!old.amountVerified,financialReturns:old.financialReturns||{},itemsVerified:!!old.itemsVerified,finalAmount:old.finalAmount,lastSync:old.lastSync,bookingState:old.bookingState,source:old.source,customerType:old.customerType,customerTypeManual:old.customerTypeManual,adAttributed:old.adAttributed,dispatched:old.dispatched||n.dispatched,requestId:old.requestId,createdBy:old.createdBy};
  const out=Object.assign({},old,n,preserve);return out;
}
function importDiff_(old,next){const keys=['cid','tracking','invoice','date','name','phone','address','district','thana','total','cod','shippingCharge','codCharge','returnCharge','status','approval','approvalConflict','courierPayment','note','createdAt','deliveredAt','approvedAt','dispatched'];return keys.some(k=>JSON.stringify(old[k]??null)!==JSON.stringify(next[k]??null));}
function importPreview_(p){
  let rows;if(p.csv){if(str_(p.csv).length>6000000)throw Error('CSV exceeds 6 MB. Split the file.');const parsed=Utilities.parseCsv(p.csv.replace(/^\uFEFF/,''));const h=parsed.shift().map(str_);const needed=['Created At','Order ID','Tracking Code','Recipient Name','COD Amount','Delivery Status'];needed.forEach(k=>{if(!h.includes(k))throw Error('Missing column: '+k);});rows=parsed.filter(r=>r.some(Boolean)).map(r=>Object.fromEntries(h.map((k,i)=>[k,r[i]===undefined?'':r[i]])));}else{rows=p.rows;}
  if(!Array.isArray(rows)||!rows.length||rows.length>5000)throw Error('Import 1–5000 rows per batch');
  const errors=[],seen=new Set(),trackingSeen=new Set(),stage=[];let duplicates=0;rows.forEach((r,i)=>{try{const o=importRecord_(r);if(seen.has(o.id)||(o.tracking&&trackingSeen.has(o.tracking))){duplicates++;return;}seen.add(o.id);if(o.tracking)trackingSeen.add(o.tracking);stage.push(o);}catch(e){errors.push({row:i+2,error:e.message});}});
  const existing=read_('Orders'),maps=importMatchMaps_(existing),problems=[];let added=0,updated=0,unchanged=0;stage.forEach((n,i)=>{const hit=findImportMatch_(n,maps);if(hit.problem){problems.push({row:i+2,error:hit.problem});return;}if(!hit.order){added++;return;}const next=importMerged_(hit.order,n);if(importDiff_(hit.order,next))updated++;else unchanged++;});
  writeAll_('Stage',stage);const token=uid_();props_().setProperty('SHD_IMPORT_TOKEN',token);props_().setProperty('SHD_IMPORT_ERRORS',String(errors.length));props_().setProperty('SHD_IMPORT_PROBLEMS',String(problems.length));const status={};stage.forEach(o=>status[o.status]=(status[o.status]||0)+1);
  return {token,count:stage.length,duplicates,errors,problems,newCount:added,updatedCount:updated,unchangedCount:unchanged,status,start:stage.map(o=>o.date).sort()[0],end:stage.map(o=>o.date).sort().pop(),sample:stage.slice(0,5),columns:Object.keys(rows[0])};
}
function importCommitBase_(p){
  if(p.token!==props_().getProperty('SHD_IMPORT_TOKEN'))throw Error('Preview expired. Preview again.');
  if(Number(props_().getProperty('SHD_IMPORT_ERRORS'))>0||Number(props_().getProperty('SHD_IMPORT_PROBLEMS'))>0)throw Error('Resolve import problems before confirming.');
  const stage=read_('Stage');if(!stage.length)throw Error('Preview import first');
  const existing=read_('Orders'),maps=importMatchMaps_(existing);let added=0,updated=0,unchanged=0;
  stage.forEach(n=>{const hit=findImportMatch_(n,maps);if(hit.problem)throw Error(hit.problem);if(hit.order){const next=importMerged_(hit.order,n);if(importDiff_(hit.order,next)){next.revision=(hit.order.revision||0)+1;Object.assign(hit.order,next);updated++;}else unchanged++;}else{existing.push(n);maps.byId[n.id]=n;if(n.cid)maps.byCid[n.cid]=n;if(n.tracking)maps.byTracking[n.tracking]=n;added++;}});
  writeAll_('Orders',existing);if(read_('Orders').length!==existing.length)throw Error('Import verification failed. No existing orders were intentionally cleared.');
  props_().setProperty('SHD_HISTORY_IMPORTED',props_().getProperty('SHD_HISTORY_IMPORTED')||now_());props_().deleteProperty('SHD_IMPORT_TOKEN');props_().deleteProperty('SHD_IMPORT_ERRORS');props_().deleteProperty('SHD_IMPORT_PROBLEMS');log_('Import',added+' new, '+updated+' updated, '+unchanged+' unchanged; safe merge');
  return {added,updated,unchanged,total:existing.length,message:'Import complete. Product/combo assignments and manual business fields were preserved.'};
}

function getSettings_Legacy(){const s=settings_();return Object.assign({},s,{hasGemini:!!props_().getProperty('SHD_GEMINI'),hasSteadfast:!!props_().getProperty('SHD_SF_KEY')&&!!props_().getProperty('SHD_SF_SECRET'),lastSync:props_().getProperty('SHD_LAST_SYNC')||'',backup:props_().getProperty('SHD_BACKUP_URL')||''});}
function saveSettingsBase_(p){const s=settings_();s.businessName=str_(p.businessName)||s.businessName;s.profitRate=V2.historicalRate;s.aiEnabled=!!p.aiEnabled;s.aiLimit=Math.min(1000,Math.max(1,amount_(p.aiLimit||40,'AI daily limit')));s.geminiModel=str_(p.geminiModel)||s.geminiModel;if(!/^gemini-[a-zA-Z0-9.-]+$/.test(s.geminiModel))throw Error('Invalid model name');s.syncEnabled=false;s.syncMinutes=15;if(p.logo!==undefined){if(p.logo&&!/^data:image\/(png|jpeg|webp);base64,[A-Za-z0-9+/=]+$/.test(p.logo))throw Error('PNG/JPEG/WebP logo required');if(p.logo.length>28000)throw Error('Logo too large. Use resized upload.');s.logo=p.logo;}[['geminiKey','SHD_GEMINI'],['sfKey','SHD_SF_KEY'],['sfSecret','SHD_SF_SECRET']].forEach(([k,key])=>{if(str_(p[k]))props_().setProperty(key,str_(p[k]).replace(/\s/g,''));});put_('Settings',s);removeCourierSchedule_();return getSettings_();}
function sf_(path,method,body){const key=props_().getProperty('SHD_SF_KEY'),secret=props_().getProperty('SHD_SF_SECRET');if(!key||!secret)throw Error('Save Steadfast keys in Settings');const options={method:method||'get',headers:{'Api-Key':key,'Secret-Key':secret},muteHttpExceptions:true,contentType:'application/json'};if(body)options.payload=JSON.stringify(body);const r=UrlFetchApp.fetch('https://portal.packzy.com/api/v1'+path,options);if(r.getResponseCode()>=300)throw Error('Steadfast HTTP '+r.getResponseCode()+'. Check credentials/quota and retry status lookup.');let j;try{j=JSON.parse(r.getContentText());}catch(e){throw Error('Steadfast response not JSON');}if(j.status&&Number(j.status)>=400)throw Error('Steadfast request rejected ('+j.status+'). Check booking fields.');return j;}
function book_(p){const o=read_('Orders').find(o=>o.id===p.id);if(!o)throw Error('Order missing');if(o.cid||o.tracking)return o;if(!(o.lines||[]).length)throw Error('Select products before booking this order');if(!getSettings_().hasSteadfast)throw Error('Save Steadfast API credentials in Settings');if(!/^01\d{9}$/.test(o.phone))throw Error('Steadfast needs an 11-digit Bangladesh phone');if(!o.address||o.address.length>250||o.name.length>100)throw Error('Address required, max 250 characters; name max 100');if(o.bookingState==='uncertain'){if(!p.noParcelConfirmed)throw Error('Previous booking result unknown. Check Steadfast invoice '+o.invoice+' before retry.');o.bookingState='retry-confirmed';}if(!['Draft','Confirmed','Pending','In Review'].includes(o.status))throw Error('Only open, unshipped orders can be booked');if(num_(o.cod)===null)throw Error('Enter COD amount before booking');const proposed={...o,status:'Confirmed',stockReserved:true};const ps=products_(),all=read_('Orders'),records=read_('Stock');const before=stocks_(ps,all,records),after=stocks_(ps,all.map(x=>x.id===o.id?proposed:x),records);ps.forEach(x=>{if(after[x.id].qty<0&&after[x.id].qty<before[x.id].qty)throw Error('Insufficient stock: '+x.name);});o.stockReserved=true;o.status='Confirmed';o.bookingState='uncertain';o.revision++;put_('Orders',o);try{const r=sf_('/create_order','post',{invoice:o.invoice,recipient_name:o.name,recipient_phone:o.phone,recipient_address:o.address,cod_amount:o.cod,note:o.note});if(!r.consignment||!r.consignment.consignment_id)throw Error('No consignment ID returned');o.cid=str_(r.consignment.consignment_id);o.tracking=str_(r.consignment.tracking_code);o.status=status_(r.consignment.status);o.stockReserved=true;o.bookingState='booked';o.lastSync=now_();o.revision++;put_('Orders',o);log_('Booking',o.id+' booked');return o;}catch(e){log_('Booking',o.id+' result uncertain; verify invoice before retry');throw e;}}
function updateStatus_(p){
  const id=str_(p.id),orders=read_('Orders'),o=orders.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!o)throw Error('Order not found');if(isClosed_(o.date))throw Error('This order is in a closed month. Reopen the month before updating status.');if(!o.cid&&!o.tracking)throw Error('Consignment ID or tracking code missing. Link the parcel first.');
  try{const r=sf_(o.cid?'/status_by_cid/'+encodeURIComponent(o.cid):'/status_by_trackingcode/'+encodeURIComponent(o.tracking));if(!r.delivery_status)throw Error('Delivery status absent');const before=o.status;o.status=status_(r.delivery_status);o.rawStatus=r.delivery_status;o.lastSync=now_();o.syncError='';o.revision=(o.revision||0)+1;if(['Delivered','Partial','Shipped','Returned'].includes(o.status))o.dispatched=true;put_('Orders',o);props_().setProperty('SHD_LAST_SYNC',o.lastSync);log_('Status update',o.id+' '+before+' -> '+o.status);return {order:o,previousStatus:before,status:o.status,lastUpdated:o.lastSync,message:'Status updated. Courier charges and partial collected amounts were not changed.'};}
  catch(e){o.lastAttempt=now_();o.syncError=e.message;o.revision=(o.revision||0)+1;put_('Orders',o);throw e;}
}
function updateStatuses_(p){
  const orders=read_('Orders'),active=orders.filter(o=>(o.cid||o.tracking)&&(!final_(o)||o.approvalConflict||p.id===o.id)&&!isClosed_(o.date)).sort((a,b)=>(a.lastSync||'').localeCompare(b.lastSync||''));let checked=0,failed=0;
  for(const o of active.slice(0,20)){try{const r=sf_(o.cid?'/status_by_cid/'+encodeURIComponent(o.cid):'/status_by_trackingcode/'+encodeURIComponent(o.tracking));if(!r.delivery_status)throw Error('Delivery status absent');const before=o.status;o.status=status_(r.delivery_status);o.rawStatus=r.delivery_status;o.lastSync=now_();o.revision=(o.revision||0)+1;o.syncError='';if(['Delivered','Partial','Shipped','Returned'].includes(o.status))o.dispatched=true;put_('Orders',o);checked++;if(before!==o.status)log_('Status update',o.id+' '+before+' -> '+o.status);}catch(e){o.lastAttempt=now_();o.syncError=e.message;o.revision=(o.revision||0)+1;put_('Orders',o);failed++;if(/HTTP (401|403|429)/.test(e.message))break;}}
  if(checked)props_().setProperty('SHD_LAST_SYNC',now_());return {checked,failed,remaining:Math.max(0,active.length-checked-failed),lastUpdated:props_().getProperty('SHD_LAST_SYNC')||'',message:'Status only. Courier charges and verified partial collected amounts still require import/manual verification when absent.'};
}
function syncBase_(p){return updateStatuses_(p||{});}
function extraCourierBase_(p){const paths={balance:'/get_balance',payments:'/payments',returns:'/get_return_requests',police:'/police_stations'};if(p.kind==='payment'){if(!/^\d+$/.test(str_(p.id)))throw Error('Payment ID required');return sf_('/payments/'+p.id);}if(p.kind==='requestReturn'){const o=read_('Orders').find(o=>o.id===p.id);if(!o||!o.cid)throw Error('Consignment ID required');if(!p.confirm)throw Error('Confirm return request');return sf_('/create_return_request','post',{consignment_id:o.cid,reason:str_(p.reason)});}if(!paths[p.kind])throw Error('Unknown courier report');return sf_(paths[p.kind]);}
function saveCombo_(p){const all=read_('Combos'),products=products_();const id=str_(p.id)||'COMBO-'+uid_().slice(0,8);if(products.some(x=>x.id===id))throw Error('ID conflicts with product');if(!str_(p.name))throw Error('Combo name required');const c=(p.components||[]).map(x=>{if(!products.some(y=>y.id===x.sku))throw Error('Select component product');const qty=amount_(x.qty,'Component qty');if(!qty)throw Error('Component qty must be positive');return {sku:x.sku,qty};});if(!c.length)throw Error('Add at least one component');if(new Set(c.map(x=>x.sku)).size!==c.length)throw Error('Combine repeated components into one quantity');put_('Combos',{id,name:str_(p.name),price:amount_(p.price,'Combo price'),components:c,active:p.active!==false});return read_('Combos');}
function catalog_(){const ps=products_(),st=stocks_(ps,read_('Orders'),read_('Stock'));return ps.map(p=>Object.assign(p,{stock:st[p.id].qty})).concat(read_('Combos').map(c=>Object.assign({},c,{category:'Combo',cost:c.components.some(x=>!ps.find(p=>p.id===x.sku)||ps.find(p=>p.id===x.sku).cost===null)?null:c.components.reduce((a,x)=>a+ps.find(p=>p.id===x.sku).cost*x.qty,0),stock:Math.min(...c.components.map(x=>Math.floor((st[x.sku]||{qty:0}).qty/x.qty)))})));}
function prepareLines_(input,old){const cat=catalog_();if(!Array.isArray(input)||input.length>100)throw Error('Invalid order items');if(new Set(input.map(l=>l.sku)).size!==input.length)throw Error('Combine repeated products into one line');return input.map(l=>{const c=cat.find(x=>x.id===l.sku);if(!c)throw Error('Unknown product/combo: '+l.sku);const prior=(old||[]).find(x=>x.sku===l.sku);const qty=amount_(l.qty,'Quantity');if(!qty)throw Error('Quantity must be positive');const cost=prior&&prior.cost!==null?prior.cost:l.cost!==undefined&&l.cost!==''?amount_(l.cost,'Unit cost'):c.cost;const ps=products_();const returned=amount_(l.returned||0,'Returned line qty');if(returned>qty)throw Error('Returned line quantity exceeds sold quantity');return {sku:c.id,name:c.name,category:c.category,qty,price:amount_(l.price,'Price'),cost,returned,components:prior?prior.components:(c.components||[{sku:c.id,qty:1}]).map(x=>({sku:x.sku,qty:x.qty,cost:c.components?(ps.find(p=>p.id===x.sku)?.cost??null):cost}))};});}
function saveOrderBase_(p){const orders=read_('Orders'),old=orders.find(o=>o.id===p.id);if(!old&&orders.some(o=>o.requestId&&o.requestId===p.requestId))return orders.find(o=>o.requestId===p.requestId);if(old&&Number(p.revision)!==old.revision)throw Error('Order changed. Reload before saving.');if(old&&old.bookingState==='uncertain')throw Error('Resolve courier booking before editing');const o=Object.assign({},old||{id:uid_(),invoice:'SHD-'+uid_().slice(0,18),createdAt:now_(),stockReturns:{},revision:0});['name','address','district','thana','note','source','paymentStatus','paymentMethod'].forEach(k=>o[k]=str_(p[k]));o.phone=phone_(p.phone);o.date=day_(p.date);o.status=status_(p.status);if(old&&old.dispatched&&o.status==='Draft')throw Error('Dispatched orders cannot revert to Draft');if(!o.name)throw Error('Customer name required');o.lines=prepareLines_(p.lines,old&&old.lines);if(old&&Object.values(old.stockReturns||{}).some(q=>q>0)&&JSON.stringify(o.lines.map(l=>({...l,returned:0})))!==JSON.stringify(old.lines.map(l=>({...l,returned:0}))))throw Error('Returned orders cannot change item lines. Create an adjustment order.');o.total=o.imported&&num_(p.total)===null?null:amount_(p.total,'Order total');o.cod=o.imported&&num_(p.cod)===null?null:amount_(p.cod===undefined?p.total:p.cod,'COD amount');['shippingCharge','codCharge'].forEach(k=>o[k]=p[k]===''||p[k]===null?null:amount_(p[k],k));o.returnCharge=amount_(p.returnCharge||0,'Return charge');o.amountVerified=!!p.amountVerified;o.itemsVerified=!!p.itemsVerified;o.finalAmount=o.amountVerified?amount_(p.finalAmount,'Final collected amount'):null;o.adAttributed=!!p.adAttributed;o.dispatched=!!(o.dispatched||p.dispatched||['Shipped','Delivered','Partial','Returned'].includes(o.status));o.districtInferred=false;o.stockReserved=!['Draft','Unknown'].includes(o.status);o.requestId=o.requestId||p.requestId;o.revision++;o.updatedAt=now_();if(o.status==='Draft')o.stockReserved=false;
  if(p.financialReturns){const cap=components_(o.lines);o.financialReturns={};Object.keys(p.financialReturns).forEach(k=>{const n=amount_(p.financialReturns[k],'Financial return qty');if(n>(cap[k]||0))throw Error('Return exceeds order component quantity');if(n)o.financialReturns[k]=n;});}if(p.returns){const cap=components_(o.lines);Object.keys(p.returns).forEach(k=>{const n=amount_(p.returns[k],'Returned stock');if(n>(cap[k]||0))throw Error('Return exceeds shipped quantity');if(n<(o.stockReturns[k]||0))throw Error('Recorded physical return cannot decrease');});o.stockReturns=Object.assign({},o.stockReturns,p.returns);}
  const next=orders.filter(x=>x.id!==o.id).concat(o),ps=products_(),records=read_('Stock'),before=stocks_(ps,orders,records),after=stocks_(ps,next,records);ps.forEach(x=>{if(after[x.id].qty<0&&after[x.id].qty<before[x.id].qty)throw Error('Insufficient stock: '+x.name+'. Update dated available stock first.');});put_('Orders',o);return o;}
function customers_(){const map={},rate=settings_().profitRate;read_('Orders').forEach(o=>{const id=o.phone?'PHONE-'+o.phone:'GUEST-'+o.id;const c=map[id]||(map[id]={id,name:o.name,phone:o.phone,district:o.district,orders:0,delivered:0,sales:0,lastDate:o.date});c.orders++;const f=finance_(o,rate);if(f.sales!==null)c.sales+=f.sales;if(o.status==='Delivered')c.delivered++;if(o.date>=c.lastDate){c.name=o.name;c.lastDate=o.date;} });return Object.values(map).sort((a,b)=>b.orders-a.orders);}
function orderList_(p){const all=read_('Orders'),types=customerTypes_(all);let rows=all.filter(o=>(!p.start||o.date>=p.start)&&(!p.end||o.date<=p.end)&&(!p.status||o.status===p.status)&&(!p.pendingProducts||!o.lines.length)&&(!p.query||JSON.stringify([o.name,o.phone,o.id,o.cid,o.tracking,o.district,o.invoice]).toLowerCase().includes(str_(p.query).toLowerCase()))).sort((a,b)=>b.date.localeCompare(a.date));const rate=settings_().profitRate,total=rows.length;return {total,rows:rows.slice(p.offset||0,(p.offset||0)+50).map(o=>Object.assign({},o,{customerType:types[o.id],finance:finance_(o,rate)}))};}

/** v2.1 accounting extensions. Existing product, order and import storage is retained. */
function settings_(){return Object.assign({deadDays:60,leadDays:7,stockTargetDays:30,reminderHour:20,minSample:10,salesTarget:0,cashReserve:0,suggestionLimit:3,aiDataMode:'off'},baseSettings_());}
function upgrade21_(){
  if(props_().getProperty('SHD_UPGRADE_21'))return;
  const orders=read_('Orders');orders.forEach(o=>{if(o.source==='Steadfast import')o.source='Old Data';});
  writeAll_('Orders',orders);props_().setProperty('SHD_UPGRADE_21',now_());
}
function upgrade214_(){
  if(props_().getProperty('SHD_UPGRADE_214'))return;
  const s=settings_();s.profitRate=V2.historicalRate;s.syncEnabled=false;put_('Settings',s);removeCourierSchedule_();props_().setProperty('SHD_UPGRADE_214',now_());
}
function products_(){const meta=read_('ProductMeta'),raw=requestReads_&&requestReads_.baseProducts||baseProducts_();if(requestReads_)requestReads_.baseProducts=raw;return raw.map(p=>Object.assign({},p,meta.find(m=>m.id===p.id)||{}));}
function catalogView_(){return {catalog:catalog_(),combos:read_('Combos'),settings:getSettings_()};}
function monthEnd_(m){day_(m+'-01');return new Date(Date.UTC(Number(m.slice(0,4)),Number(m.slice(5,7)),0)).toISOString().slice(0,10);}
function addDays_(s,n){return new Date(Date.parse(s+'T00:00:00Z')+n*86400000).toISOString().slice(0,10);}
function daysBetween_(a,b){return Math.round((Date.parse(b)-Date.parse(a))/86400000)+1;}
function dates_(start,end){const a=[];for(let d=start;d<=end;d=addDays_(d,1)){a.push(d);if(a.length>5000)throw Error('Select a period shorter than 5000 days');}return a;}
function monthsIn_(start,end){return [...new Set(dates_(start,end).map(d=>d.slice(0,7)))];}
function isClosed_(date){return read_('Months').some(m=>m.id===str_(date).slice(0,7)&&m.closed);}
function guardPeriod_(start,end){if(read_('Months').some(m=>m.closed&&m.id>=start.slice(0,7)&&m.id<=end.slice(0,7)))throw Error('This period contains a closed month. Reopen it from Month Closing before editing.');}
function recordPeriod_(x){return [x.periodStart||x.start||x.date,x.periodEnd||x.end||x.date||today_()];}
function put_(table,item){
  if(['Orders','Expenses','Budgets'].includes(table)){
    const old=read_(table).find(x=>x.id===item.id);
    for(const x of [old,item].filter(Boolean)){const [a,b]=recordPeriod_(x);if(a)guardPeriod_(a,b||today_());if(table==='Orders'&&deliveryDay_(x))guardPeriod_(deliveryDay_(x),deliveryDay_(x));}
  }
  return basePut_(table,item);
}
function audit_(type,id,detail){log_(type,id+' '+detail);}
function saveSettings_Legacy(p){
  const extra={};for(const [k,lo,hi] of [['deadDays',1,730],['leadDays',0,365],['stockTargetDays',1,730],['reminderHour',0,23],['minSample',1,1000],['suggestionLimit',0,30],['salesTarget',0,1e10],['cashReserve',0,1e10]]){
    if(p[k]!==undefined){const n=amount_(p[k],k);if(n<lo||n>hi)throw Error(k+' outside allowed range');extra[k]=n;}
  }
  if(p.aiDataMode!==undefined){if(!['off','summary','protected'].includes(p.aiDataMode))throw Error('Invalid AI data mode');extra.aiDataMode=p.aiDataMode;}
  saveSettingsBase_(p);put_('Settings',Object.assign(settings_(),extra));return getSettings_();
}
function saveProduct_(p){const meta={id:p.id,leadDays:amount_(p.leadDays===undefined?settings_().leadDays:p.leadDays,'Lead days'),created:now_()};const before=products_().map(x=>x.id);const result=saveProductBase_(p);meta.id=p.id||result.find(x=>!before.includes(x.id)).id;const old=read_('ProductMeta').find(x=>x.id===meta.id);put_('ProductMeta',Object.assign({},old,meta,{created:old?.created||meta.created}));return products_();}
function saveOrder_Legacy(p){
  guardPeriod_(day_(p.date),p.date);const old=read_('Orders').find(o=>o.id===p.id);if(old)guardPeriod_(old.date,old.date);
  if(p.date>today_())throw Error('Order date cannot be in the future');
  if(Object.values(p.financialReturns||{}).some(q=>Number(q)>0)&&(p.lines||[]).some(l=>Number(l.returned)>0))throw Error('Use either whole-line returns or component returns, not both for the same partial order.');
  if(!old&&status_(p.status)!=='Draft'&&!(p.lines||[]).length)throw Error('Select at least one product before confirming an order');
  const saved=saveOrderBase_(p);
  saved.customerTypeManual=!!p.customerTypeManual;
  saved.customerType=saved.customerTypeManual?(p.customerType==='Repeat'?'Repeat':'New'):customerType_({phone:saved.phone,id:saved.id,date:saved.date,createdAt:saved.createdAt}).type;
  saved.source=saved.source==='Steadfast import'?'Old Data':saved.source||'Other';put_('Orders',saved);audit_('Order',saved.id,'revision '+saved.revision);return saved;
}
function customerTypes_(orders){
  const types={},seen=new Set();orders.slice().sort((a,b)=>a.date.localeCompare(b.date)||(a.createdAt||'').localeCompare(b.createdAt||'')||a.id.localeCompare(b.id)).forEach(o=>{
    const ph=phone_(o.phone);types[o.id]=o.customerTypeManual?o.customerType:ph?(seen.has(ph)?'Repeat':'New'):(o.imported?'Unknown':'New');if(ph)seen.add(ph);
  });return types;
}
function customerType_(p){const ph=phone_(p.phone);if(!ph)return {type:'New',matched:false};const match=read_('Orders').some(o=>o.id!==p.id&&phone_(o.phone)===ph&&(o.date<p.date||(o.date===p.date&&(o.createdAt||'')<(p.createdAt||now_()))));return {type:match?'Repeat':'New',matched:match};}
function importCommit_Legacy(p){const existing=read_('Orders');read_('Stage').forEach(o=>{guardPeriod_(o.date,o.date);const old=existing.find(x=>x.id===o.id||o.cid&&x.cid===o.cid||o.tracking&&x.tracking===o.tracking);if(old)guardPeriod_(old.date,old.date);});return importCommitBase_(p);}
function sync_(p){const r=syncBase_(p||{});r.message+=' Closed months are frozen; reopen to update their statuses.';return r;}
function removeCourierSchedule_(){ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers().filter(t=>t.getHandlerFunction()==='scheduledSync').forEach(t=>ScriptApp.deleteTrigger(t));}
function setCourierSync_(p){const s=settings_();s.syncEnabled=false;put_('Settings',s);removeCourierSchedule_();return getSettings_();}
function refreshCourier_(){const result=updateStatuses_({});return Object.assign(result,{settings:getSettings_()});}

function extraCourier_Legacy(p){return p.kind==='balance'?refreshBalance_({}):extraCourierBase_(p);}
function refreshBalance_(){const r=sf_('/get_balance'),balance=num_(r.current_balance);if(balance===null)throw Error('Courier did not return a numeric current_balance');const x={id:'courier-balance',amount:balance,updated:now_()};put_('Checks',x);return x;}

/** Purchase receipts are added to stock once; a later physical baseline absorbs prior receipts. */
function stocks_(products,orders,records){
  const out=stocksBase_(products,orders,records),purchases=read_('Purchases').filter(x=>!x.void&&x.receivedDate&&x.receivedDate<=today_());
  products.forEach(p=>{const base=records.find(x=>x.id===p.id);purchases.forEach(b=>{const included=base&&Array.isArray(base.purchaseIds)?base.purchaseIds.includes(b.id):base&&b.receivedDate<=base.date;
    if(!included&&(base?b.receivedDate>=base.date:true))out[p.id].qty+=(b.lines||[]).filter(l=>l.sku===p.id).reduce((a,l)=>a+l.qty,0);
  });});return out;
}
function saveStock_Legacy(p){
  const id=p.requestId||uid_(),checks=read_('Checks');if(checks.some(c=>c.id==='stock-op:'+id))return stockView_();
  const before=read_('Stock').find(x=>x.id===p.sku);if(p.mode==='baseline'&&before&&(before.movements||[]).some(m=>m.date>p.date))throw Error('Count date predates recorded movements. Use a current physical count.');
  saveStockBase_(p);if(p.mode==='baseline'){const b=read_('Stock').find(x=>x.id===p.sku);b.purchaseIds=read_('Purchases').filter(x=>!x.void&&x.receivedDate&&x.receivedDate<=p.date).map(x=>x.id);put_('Stock',b);}
  put_('Checks',{id:'stock-op:'+id,date:today_()});return stockView_();
}
function savePurchase_Legacy(p){
  const all=read_('Purchases'),id=p.id||p.requestId||uid_();if(all.some(x=>x.id===id))return cashView_();
  const date=day_(p.date);if(date>today_())throw Error('Purchase date cannot be future');const receivedDate=p.receivedDate?day_(p.receivedDate):'';
  if(receivedDate&&receivedDate>today_())throw Error('Confirm receipt only after goods arrive');
  const ps=products_();if(!Array.isArray(p.lines)||!p.lines.length)throw Error('Add purchase items');
  const lines=p.lines.map(l=>{if(!ps.some(x=>x.id===l.sku))throw Error('Select a valid product');const qty=amount_(l.qty,'Quantity');if(qty<=0)throw Error('Quantity must be positive');return {sku:l.sku,name:ps.find(x=>x.id===l.sku).name,qty,cost:amount_(l.cost,'Unit purchase cost')};});
  if(new Set(lines.map(l=>l.sku)).size!==lines.length)throw Error('Combine repeated product rows');
  if(receivedDate){for(const l of lines){const b=read_('Stock').find(x=>x.id===l.sku);if(b&&receivedDate<b.date&&!p.includedInCount)throw Error('Receipt is on/before your stock count. Confirm these units are already included, or use the actual later receipt date.');}}
  const total=lines.reduce((a,l)=>a+l.qty*l.cost,0),paid=amount_(p.paid||0,'Paid amount');if(paid>total)throw Error('Paid exceeds purchase total');
  const record={id,date,receivedDate,supplier:str_(p.supplier),reference:str_(p.reference),dueDate:p.dueDate?day_(p.dueDate):'',lines,total,note:str_(p.note),payments:paid?[{id:id+':initial',date,amount:paid,account:'Bank'}]:[],created:now_()};
  if(record.reference&&all.some(x=>x.reference===record.reference&&x.supplier===record.supplier))throw Error('Supplier purchase reference already recorded');
  put_('Purchases',record);
  if(receivedDate&&p.includedInCount){const records=read_('Stock');for(const l of lines){const b=records.find(x=>x.id===l.sku);if(b&&receivedDate<=b.date){b.purchaseIds=[...new Set((b.purchaseIds||all.filter(x=>x.receivedDate&&x.receivedDate<=b.date).map(x=>x.id)).concat(id))];put_('Stock',b);}}}
  audit_('Purchase',id,'recorded; stock receipt '+(receivedDate||'not yet'));return cashView_();
}
function receivePurchase_(p){const b=read_('Purchases').find(x=>x.id===p.id);if(!b)throw Error('Purchase not found');if(b.receivedDate)return cashView_();b.receivedDate=day_(p.date);if(b.receivedDate>today_())throw Error('Future receipt not allowed');if(read_('Stock').some(s=>b.lines.some(l=>l.sku===s.id)&&b.receivedDate<=s.date))throw Error('Receipt predates physical baseline; record a current count to reconcile first.');put_('Purchases',b);return cashView_();}

/** Expense coverage replaces the matching day/category projection, not the whole month. */
function expenseTotals_Legacy(expenses,budgets,start,end,today){
  if(end<start)return {rows:[],actual:0,projection:0,used:0,daily:[]};
  const map={};function cell(d,c){const key=d+'|'+c.trim().toLowerCase();return map[key]||(map[key]={date:d,category:c,actual:0,projection:0,covered:false});}
  const last=end<today?end:today;
  budgets.filter(b=>b.active).forEach(b=>{const from=[start,b.start].sort().pop(),to=[last,b.end||last].sort()[0];dates_(from,to).forEach(d=>{cell(d,b.category).projection+=b.frequency==='daily'?b.amount:b.amount/Number(monthEnd_(d.slice(0,7)).slice(8));});});
  expenses.filter(x=>!x.void).forEach(x=>{const a=x.periodStart||x.date,b=x.periodEnd||x.date,n=daysBetween_(a,b);if(b<start||a>last)return;dates_(a>start?a:start,b<last?b:last).forEach(d=>{const v=cell(d,x.category);v.actual+=Number(x.amount)/n;if(x.coverageComplete!==false)v.covered=true;});});
  const daily=Object.values(map).map(x=>({...x,used:x.actual+(x.covered?0:x.projection)}));const groups={};daily.forEach(x=>{const key=x.date.slice(0,7)+'|'+x.category.toLowerCase();const g=groups[key]||(groups[key]={month:x.date.slice(0,7),category:x.category,actual:0,projection:0,used:0});for(const k of ['actual','projection','used'])g[k]+=x[k];});
  const rows=Object.values(groups);return {rows,daily,actual:rows.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.actual,0),projection:rows.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.projection,0),used:rows.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.used,0)};
}
function expenseView_(){return {expenses:read_('Expenses'),budgets:read_('Budgets')};}
function saveExpense_Legacy(p){
  const id=p.id||p.requestId||uid_(),old=read_('Expenses').find(x=>x.id===id);if(!p.id&&old)return expenseView_();
  const date=day_(p.date),periodStart=day_(p.periodStart||date),periodEnd=day_(p.periodEnd||date);if(periodEnd<periodStart)throw Error('Expense coverage end precedes start');
  if(date>today_())throw Error('Record actual expense when incurred; use a projection for future costs');
  const amount=amount_(p.amount,'Expense'),paid=amount_(p.paid||0,'Paid');if(paid>amount)throw Error('Paid exceeds expense');
  if(paid&&day_(p.paidDate||date)>today_())throw Error('Payment date cannot be in the future');
  const paidBeforeOpening=amount_(p.paidBeforeOpening??old?.paidBeforeOpening??0,'Already paid before opening');const payments=old?.payments||[];if(paidBeforeOpening>amount)throw Error('Prior payment exceeds expense');if(paidBeforeOpening>0&&!read_('Ledger').some(x=>x.kind==='opening'))throw Error('Enter opening balance before recording a payment already included in it');if(old&&payments.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.amount,0)+paidBeforeOpening>amount)throw Error('Expense cannot be less than payments already recorded');
  if(p.void&&(payments.length||paidBeforeOpening))throw Error('Record/refund payments before voiding a paid expense; use a correction entry instead');
  put_('Expenses',{id,date,periodStart,periodEnd,category:str_(p.category)||'Other',marketplace:str_(p.marketplace),amount,coverageComplete:p.coverageComplete!==false,note:str_(p.note),void:!!p.void,dueDate:p.dueDate?day_(p.dueDate):'',paidBeforeOpening,payments:old?(!Array.isArray(old.payments)&&!p.confirmPaymentStatus?undefined:payments):paid?[{id:id+':initial',date:day_(p.paidDate||date),amount:paid,account:'Bank'}]:[]});
  audit_('Expense',id,old?'edited':'created');return expenseView_();
}
function saveBudget_Legacy(p){const start=day_(p.start),end=p.end?day_(p.end):'';if(end&&end<start)throw Error('End precedes start');put_('Budgets',{id:p.id||p.requestId||uid_(),category:str_(p.category)||'Other',frequency:p.frequency==='daily'?'daily':'monthly',amount:amount_(p.amount,'Projection'),start,end,active:p.active!==false});return expenseView_();}
function payBill_(p){
  const table=p.kind==='purchase'?'Purchases':'Expenses',x=read_(table).find(b=>b.id===p.id);if(!x||x.void)throw Error('Bill not found');if(x.nonCash)throw Error('Non-cash damage cannot be paid as a bill');
  const id=p.requestId||uid_();if(!Array.isArray(x.payments))throw Error('Confirm the prior payment status in Expenses first');x.payments=x.payments||[];if(x.payments.some(y=>y.id===id))return cashView_();
  const date=day_(p.date),amount=amount_(p.amount,'Payment');if(date>today_())throw Error('Payment must be received/paid before recording');
  if(date<x.date)throw Error('Payment predates bill; record a separate advance first');
  if(amount<=0||x.payments.reduce((a,y)=>a+y.amount,0)+(x.paidBeforeOpening||0)+amount>(x.total??x.amount)+0.005)throw Error('Payment exceeds outstanding bill');
  x.payments.push({id,date,amount,account:'Bank',reference:str_(p.reference)});
  // Paying a closed-month bill changes cash only, not that month's expense recognition.
  basePut_(table,x);audit_('Payment',id,'paid '+table+' '+x.id);return cashView_();
}
function saveCash_(p){
  const a=read_('Ledger'),id=p.id||p.requestId||uid_();if(a.some(x=>x.id===id))return cashView_();const kind=p.kind;
  if(!['opening','investment','withdrawal','deposit','bankFee','transferPending','transferReceived','adjustmentIn','adjustmentOut','customerReceipt'].includes(kind))throw Error('Select cash transaction type');
  const date=day_(p.date),amount=amount_(p.amount,'Amount');if(date>today_())throw Error('Future bank entries are not allowed');
  if(kind==='opening'&&a.some(x=>x.kind==='opening'))throw Error('Opening balance already saved. Use a dated reconciliation adjustment.');
  const reference=str_(p.reference);if(kind.startsWith('transfer')&&!reference)throw Error('Courier payment reference required');
  if(reference&&a.some(x=>x.reference===reference&&x.kind===kind))throw Error('This payment reference is already recorded');
  if(kind==='transferReceived'){const pending=a.find(x=>x.kind==='transferPending'&&x.reference===reference);if(pending&&Math.abs(pending.amount-amount)>0.005)throw Error('Received amount differs from pending transfer. Reconcile with the courier before recording.');}
  if(!['opening','transferPending','transferReceived','investment','withdrawal'].includes(kind)&&!str_(p.note))throw Error('Describe this bank adjustment/receipt; it does not create sales or profit.');
  if(kind==='bankFee')throw Error('Record bank fees through Expenses so cash and profit both update once.');
  const sign=['withdrawal','adjustmentOut'].includes(kind)?-1:kind==='transferPending'?0:1;
  put_('Ledger',{id,date,kind,amount,signed:amount*sign,reference,note:str_(p.note),created:now_()});if(kind.startsWith('transfer')){const b=read_('Checks').find(x=>x.id==='courier-balance');if(b)put_('Checks',{...b,needsRefresh:true});}audit_('Cash',id,kind);return cashView_();
}
function cashView_(){
  const ledger=read_('Ledger'),expenses=read_('Expenses').filter(x=>!x.void&&!x.nonCash),purchases=read_('Purchases').filter(x=>!x.void),opening=ledger.find(x=>x.kind==='opening');
  const bills=expenses.map(x=>({...x,total:x.amount,kind:'expense'})).concat(purchases.map(x=>({...x,kind:'purchase'})));
  const payments=bills.flatMap(x=>(x.payments||[]).map(y=>({...y,bill:x.id,category:x.category||'Stock purchase'})));
  const bank=opening?ledger.filter(x=>x.date>=opening.date&&x.date<=today_()).reduce((s,x)=>s+x.signed,0)-payments.filter(x=>x.date>=opening.date&&x.date<=today_()).reduce((s,x)=>s+x.amount,0):null;
  const pending=ledger.filter(x=>x.kind==='transferPending'&&!ledger.some(y=>y.kind==='transferReceived'&&y.reference===x.reference)).reduce((s,x)=>s+x.amount,0);
  const balance=read_('Checks').find(x=>x.id==='courier-balance');const upcoming=bills.filter(x=>Array.isArray(x.payments)).map(x=>({...x,outstanding:Math.max(0,x.total-(x.paidBeforeOpening||0)-(x.payments||[]).reduce((a,y)=>a+y.amount,0))})).filter(x=>x.outstanding>0.005);
  return {bank,courier:balance?.needsRefresh?null:balance?.amount??null,balanceUpdated:balance?.updated||'',balanceNeedsRefresh:!!balance?.needsRefresh,available:bank!==null&&balance&&!balance.needsRefresh?bank+balance.amount:null,pending,upcoming,upcomingTotal:upcoming.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.outstanding,0),ledger:ledger.slice().reverse(),payments,purchases,openingDate:opening?.date||'',updated:now_(),untrackedExpenses:expenses.filter(x=>!Array.isArray(x.payments)).length};
}

/** Management analytics use order creation-date cohorts and verified courier outcomes. */
function lineFacts_(o){
  const totalComponents=components_(o.lines),part=o.status==='Partial',sold=['Delivered','Partial'].includes(o.status),hasParts=part&&Object.values(o.financialReturns||{}).some(q=>q>0);
  return (o.lines||[]).map(l=>{
    let returned=part?(l.returned||0):o.status==='Returned'?l.qty:0,qty=sold?l.qty-returned:0,cost=num_(l.cost),retCost=0;
    if(hasParts){(l.components||[]).forEach(c=>{const r=(o.financialReturns[c.sku]||0)*(c.qty*l.qty/(totalComponents[c.sku]||1));if(r&&num_(c.cost)===null)cost=null;retCost+=r*(num_(c.cost)||0);});returned=null;}
    const cogs=!sold?0:cost===null||part&&!o.itemsVerified?null:hasParts?Math.max(0,l.qty*cost-retCost):qty*cost;
    const weight=sold?hasParts?(cost>0?l.price*l.qty*Math.max(0,1-retCost/(cost*l.qty)):l.price*l.qty):l.price*qty:l.price*l.qty;
    return {sku:l.sku,name:l.name,category:l.category||'Other',qty,returned,originalQty:l.qty,cogs,weight:Math.max(0,weight||0)};
  });
}
function finance_(o,rate){
  const sold=['Delivered','Partial'].includes(o.status),sales=!sold?0:o.status==='Partial'?((o.amountVerified)?num_(o.finalAmount):null):num_(o.total);
  const fees=[o.shippingCharge,o.codCharge,o.returnCharge],fee=fees.some(x=>num_(x)===null)?null:fees.reduce((a,x)=>a+Number(x),0),facts=lineFacts_(o);
  const missing=!facts.length||facts.some(x=>x.cogs===null),cogs=sold?(missing?null:facts.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.cogs,0)):0,historical=(o.originalDate||o.date)<V2.cutoff;
  const known=final_(o)&&sales!==null&&fee!==null&&(historical||cogs!==null),gross=sales!==null&&cogs!==null?sales-cogs:null,contribution=gross!==null&&fee!==null?gross-fee:null;
  const estimate=historical&&known?(((sold?sales:0)-fee)*V2.historicalRate/100):null;
  return {sales,fee,cogs,gross,contribution,estimate,historical,known,missingProducts:missing,reason:!final_(o)?'Not final':sales===null?'Collected amount missing/unverified':fee===null?'Courier charge missing':!historical&&cogs===null?'Product/cost verification pending':''};
}
function partsFor_(o,s){
  const f=finance_(o,s.profitRate),facts=lineFacts_(o),weight=facts.reduce((a,l)=>a+l.weight,0),fallback=facts.reduce((a,l)=>a+l.originalQty,0);
  return facts.map(l=>{const share=weight?l.weight/weight:fallback?l.originalQty/fallback:0;return {...l,share,sales:f.sales===null?null:f.sales*share,fees:f.fee===null?null:f.fee*share,gross:f.sales===null||l.cogs===null?null:f.sales*share-l.cogs,estimate:f.estimate===null?null:f.estimate*share};});
}
function cleanFilter_(p){
  const start=day_(p.start||today_().slice(0,7)+'-01'),end=day_(p.end||today_());if(end<start)throw Error('End precedes start');if(daysBetween_(start,end)>5000)throw Error('Select up to 5000 days');
  return {start,end,district:str_(p.district),category:str_(p.category),product:str_(p.product),marketplace:str_(p.marketplace),customerType:str_(p.customerType),query:str_(p.query).toLowerCase()};
}
function source_(o){return o.source==='Steadfast import'?'Old Data':o.source||'Unknown';}
function matchOrder_(o,p,t){return (!p.district||(o.district||'Unknown')===p.district)&&(!p.marketplace||source_(o)===p.marketplace)&&(!p.customerType||t[o.id]===p.customerType)&&(!p.query||[o.name,o.phone,o.invoice,o.cid,o.tracking,o.id].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(p.query));}
function matchLine_(l,p){return (!p.product||l.sku===p.product)&&(!p.category||l.category===p.category);}
function groupStats_(name){return {name,orders:0,sales:0,qty:0,returnQty:0,returnQtyUnknown:false,fees:0,gross:0,contribution:0,estimate:0,incomplete:0,delivered:0,partial:0,returned:0,cancelled:0,pending:0,finalized:0,repeat:0,identified:0,soldOrders:0,partialUnverified:0,conflicts:0,historyIncomplete:0};}
function analytics_Legacy(p,all,s){
  p=cleanFilter_(p);all=all||read_('Orders');s=s||settings_();const types=customerTypes_(all),filteredLines=!!(p.product||p.category),segmented=filteredLines||!!(p.district||p.marketplace||p.customerType||p.query);
  const d=groupStats_('Total'),maps={days:{},months:{},districts:{},products:{},statuses:{},marketplaces:{}},globalByMonth={};
  const ex=expenseTotals_(read_('Expenses'),read_('Budgets'),p.start<V2.cutoff?V2.cutoff:p.start,p.end,today_()),closed=read_('Months'),monthExpenses={};
  ex.rows.forEach(x=>{const m=monthExpenses[x.month]||(monthExpenses[x.month]={actual:0,used:0});m.actual+=x.actual;m.used+=closed.some(c=>c.id===x.month&&c.closed)?x.actual:x.used;});
  function group(k,name){return maps[k][name]||(maps[k][name]=groupStats_(name));}
  function add(g,o,parts){
    const f=finance_(o,s.profitRate),ratio=parts?parts.reduce((a,l)=>a+l.share,0):1;g.orders++;g.actualTracking=g.actualTracking||(o.originalDate||o.date)>=V2.cutoff;g.customers=g.customers||{};const ph=phone_(o.phone);if(ph){g.customers[ph]=g.customers[ph]||false;if(types[o.id]==='Repeat')g.customers[ph]=true;}if(final_(o))g.finalized++;
    g[{Delivered:'delivered',Partial:'partial',Returned:'returned',Cancelled:'cancelled'}[o.status]||'pending']++;
    if(['New','Repeat'].includes(types[o.id])){g.identified++;if(types[o.id]==='Repeat')g.repeat++;}
    if(['Delivered','Partial'].includes(o.status)&&f.sales!==null)g.soldOrders++;
    if(o.status==='Partial'&&(!o.amountVerified||!o.itemsVerified))g.partialUnverified++;if(o.approvalConflict)g.conflicts++;
    if(f.historical&&final_(o)&&!f.known)g.historyIncomplete++;
    g.sales+=(f.sales||0)*ratio;g.modernSales=(g.modernSales||0)+(f.historical?0:(f.sales||0)*ratio);g.fees+=(f.fee||0)*ratio;
    const facts=parts||partsFor_(o,s);g.qty+=facts.reduce((a,l)=>a+l.qty,0);facts.forEach(l=>{g.returnQty+=l.returned||0;if(l.returned===null)g.returnQtyUnknown=true;});
    if((o.originalDate||o.date)>=V2.cutoff&&final_(o)){
      const known=f.sales!==null&&f.fee!==null&&(parts?parts.length&&parts.every(l=>l.cogs!==null):f.cogs!==null);
      if(!known||o.approvalConflict)g.incomplete++;else{const gross=parts?parts.reduce((a,l)=>a+l.gross,0):f.gross;g.gross+=gross;g.contribution+=gross-f.fee*ratio;}
    }
    g.estimate+=(f.estimate||0)*ratio;
  }
  all.filter(o=>o.date>=p.start&&o.date<=p.end).forEach(o=>{
    const f=finance_(o,s.profitRate),m=o.date.slice(0,7);if((o.originalDate||o.date)>=V2.cutoff)globalByMonth[m]=(globalByMonth[m]||0)+(f.sales||0);
    if(!matchOrder_(o,p,types))return;const parts=partsFor_(o,s).filter(l=>matchLine_(l,p));if(filteredLines&&!parts.length)return;
    const slice=filteredLines?parts:null;add(d,o,slice);for(const [k,n] of [['days',o.date],['months',m],['statuses',o.status]])add(group(k,n),o,slice);
    for(const [k,n,items] of [['districts',o.district||'Unknown',slice],['marketplaces',source_(o),slice]].concat(parts.map(l=>['products',l.sku,[l]]))){
      const g=group(k,n);add(g,o,items);g.byMonth=g.byMonth||{};const gm=g.byMonth[m]||(g.byMonth[m]=groupStats_(m));add(gm,o,items);
    }
  });
  dates_(p.start,p.end).forEach(dt=>group('days',dt));monthsIn_(p.start,p.end).forEach(m=>group('months',m));
  const months=Object.values(maps.months).sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name));let allocatedActual=0,allocatedUsed=0;
  function metric(g){g.returnRate=g.finalized?g.returned/g.finalized*100:null;g.cancelRate=g.finalized?g.cancelled/g.finalized*100:null;g.problemRate=g.finalized?(g.returned+g.cancelled)/g.finalized*100:null;g.partialRate=g.finalized?g.partial/g.finalized*100:null;g.aov=g.soldOrders?g.sales/g.soldOrders:null;g.identifiedCustomers=Object.keys(g.customers||{}).length;g.repeatCustomers=Object.values(g.customers||{}).filter(Boolean).length;g.repeatRate=g.identifiedCustomers?g.repeatCustomers/g.identifiedCustomers*100:null;return g;}
  function allocated(gm,m,isSegment){const e=monthExpenses[m]||{actual:0,used:0},share=isSegment?(globalByMonth[m]>0?(gm.modernSales||0)/globalByMonth[m]:0):1;return {actual:e.actual*share,used:e.used*share};}
  months.forEach(m=>{const a=allocated(m,m.name,segmented),historical=m.name<V2.cutoff.slice(0,7),isClosed=closed.some(x=>x.id===m.name&&x.closed);Object.assign(m,{historical,closed:isClosed,actualExpense:a.actual,estimatedExpense:a.used,net:historical||!isClosed||m.incomplete?null:m.contribution-a.actual,projectedNet:historical?null:m.contribution-a.used});allocatedActual+=a.actual;allocatedUsed+=a.used;metric(m);});
  d.net=months.some(m=>m.historical||!m.closed||m.incomplete)?null:d.contribution-allocatedActual;
  d.projectedNet=months.some(m=>!m.historical)?d.contribution-allocatedUsed:null;
  const actualSales=months.filter(m=>!m.historical).reduce((a,m)=>a+m.sales,0);
