  d.margin=actualSales&&d.net!==null?d.net/actualSales*100:null;d.estimatedMargin=actualSales&&d.projectedNet!==null?d.projectedNet/actualSales*100:null;
  const modernMonths=months.filter(m=>!m.historical),historyMonths=months.filter(m=>m.historical),modernComplete=modernMonths.length>0&&modernMonths.every(m=>m.closed&&!m.incomplete),modernActual=modernComplete?modernMonths.reduce((a,m)=>a+m.contribution-m.actualExpense,0):null,modernEstimated=modernMonths.length?modernMonths.reduce((a,m)=>a+m.contribution-m.estimatedExpense,0):0,historyEstimate=historyMonths.reduce((a,m)=>a+m.estimate,0);
  d.displayNet=historyMonths.length?(historyEstimate+(modernComplete?modernActual:modernEstimated)):(modernComplete?modernActual:modernEstimated);
  d.displayNetLabel=historyMonths.length?(modernMonths.length?'Includes historical estimates':'Historical data estimated at '+V2.historicalRate+'%'):(modernComplete?'Actual':'Estimated');
  d.displayMargin=d.sales?d.displayNet/d.sales*100:null;d.historicalRate=V2.historicalRate;d.modernComplete=modernComplete;
  for(const k of ['districts','products','marketplaces'])Object.values(maps[k]).forEach(g=>{let act=0,est=0;Object.entries(g.byMonth||{}).forEach(([m,gm])=>{const a=allocated(gm,m,true);act+=a.actual;est+=a.used;});g.net=d.net===null||g.incomplete?null:g.contribution-act;g.projectedNet=g.actualTracking?g.contribution-est:null;if(!g.actualTracking)g.gross=null;metric(g);delete g.byMonth;});
  Object.assign(d,{filter:p,months,expenses:{...ex,allocatedActual,allocatedUsed},segmented,actualIncomplete:d.incomplete,allocation:'Operating expenses allocated monthly by recognized sales share across the entire business. Zero-sales segments receive no allocation; unallocated costs remain in business totals.'});
  for(const k of ['days','districts','products','statuses','marketplaces'])d[k]=Object.values(maps[k]).map(metric).sort(k==='days'?(a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name):(a,b)=>b.sales-a.sales);
  const cat=catalog_();d.products.forEach(g=>{g.id=g.name;g.name=cat.find(c=>c.id===g.id)?.name||all.flatMap(o=>o.lines||[]).find(l=>l.sku===g.id)?.name||'Archived product';});metric(d);if(!months.some(m=>!m.historical))d.gross=null;for(const g of [d,...Object.values(maps).flatMap(m=>Object.values(m))])delete g.customers;return d;
}
function inventoryMetrics_(p,orders){
  const s=settings_(),cat=catalog_(),st=stockView_().products,start=addDays_(today_(),-29),usage={},last={};
  orders.filter(o=>o.date>=V2.cutoff&&o.date<=today_()&&['Delivered','Partial'].includes(o.status)&&!(o.status==='Partial'&&!o.itemsVerified)).forEach(o=>{
    const parts=partsFor_(o,s);parts.forEach(l=>{if(l.qty>0){last[l.sku]=!last[l.sku]||last[l.sku]<o.date?o.date:last[l.sku];if(o.date>=start)usage[l.sku]=(usage[l.sku]||0)+l.qty;}});
    // Physical product demand also includes components sold in combos.
    (o.lines||[]).filter(l=>(l.components||[]).some(c=>c.sku!==l.sku)).forEach(l=>{(l.components||[]).forEach(c=>{
      const qty=c.qty*(l.qty-(o.status==='Partial'?l.returned||0:0)),cap=components_(o.lines)[c.sku]||1,ret=o.status==='Partial'?(o.financialReturns?.[c.sku]||0)*c.qty*l.qty/cap:0,net=Math.max(0,qty-ret);
      if(net){last[c.sku]=!last[c.sku]||last[c.sku]<o.date?o.date:last[c.sku];if(o.date>=start)usage[c.sku]=(usage[c.sku]||0)+net;}
    });});
  });
  const firstDate=orders.filter(o=>o.date>=V2.cutoff).map(o=>o.date).sort()[0]||today_();
  const rows=cat.filter(x=>x.active&&(!p.product||x.id===p.product)&&(!p.category||x.category===p.category)).map(x=>{
    const base=st.find(z=>z.id===x.id),lead=x.leadDays??s.leadDays,qty=x.stock,cost=num_(x.cost),rate=(usage[x.id]||0)/Math.max(1,Math.min(30,daysBetween_(firstDate,today_()))),coverage=rate?qty/rate:null;
    const observedFrom=x.created?x.created.slice(0,10):firstDate,noSaleSince=last[x.id]||observedFrom,dead=qty>0&&daysBetween_(noSaleSince,today_())>s.deadDays;
    return {...x,qty,value:cost===null?null:qty*cost,coverage,lead,low:qty<=(x.reorder||0),dead,lastSale:last[x.id]||'',reorderNow:qty<=(x.reorder||0)||coverage!==null&&coverage<=lead,recommended:rate?Math.max(0,Math.ceil(rate*(lead+s.stockTargetDays)-qty)):null,verified:base?base.verified:(x.components||[]).every(c=>st.find(z=>z.id===c.sku)?.verified)};
  });
  const physical=rows.filter(x=>!x.components);return {rows,totalItems:physical.length,comboCount:rows.length-physical.length,totalQty:physical.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.qty,0),value:physical.some(x=>x.value===null)?null:physical.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.value,0),low:rows.filter(x=>x.low).length,dead:physical.filter(x=>x.dead).length,deadValue:physical.filter(x=>x.dead).reduce((a,x)=>a+(x.value||0),0),unverified:rows.filter(x=>!x.verified).length,componentValueOnly:!!(p.product&&rows.some(x=>x.components)),scope:'Current inventory: Product/Category apply. Dates, district, marketplace, customer and search do not change inventory.'};
}
function previousPeriod_(p){const m=p.start.slice(0,7);if(p.start===m+'-01'&&p.end.slice(0,7)===m){const prev=addDays_(p.start,-1).slice(0,7),end=prev+'-'+p.end.slice(8);return {...p,start:prev+'-01',end:end>monthEnd_(prev)?monthEnd_(prev):end};}const end=addDays_(p.start,-1);return {...p,start:addDays_(end,-daysBetween_(p.start,p.end)+1),end};}
function growth_(a,b){return b===0?null:(a-b)/Math.abs(b)*100;}
function dashboard_(p,forGuidance){
  p=cleanFilter_(p);const all=read_('Orders'),s=settings_(),d=analytics_(p,all,s),prev=analytics_(previousPeriod_(p),all,s);d.growth={sales:growth_(d.sales,prev.sales),net:d.net===null||prev.net===null?null:growth_(d.net,prev.net),previous:previousPeriod_(p)};
  d.inventory=inventoryMetrics_(p,all);d.products.forEach(x=>{const inv=d.inventory.rows.find(y=>y.id===x.id);if(inv)Object.assign(x,{stock:inv.qty,stockValue:inv.value,coverage:inv.coverage,reorder:inv.reorderNow});});
  d.cash=cashView_();d.options={districts:[...new Set(all.map(o=>o.district||'Unknown'))].sort(),categories:[...new Set(catalog_().map(x=>x.category))].sort(),marketplaces:[...new Set(['Facebook','Website','WhatsApp','Other','Old Data'].concat(all.map(source_)))].sort(),months:[...new Set(all.map(o=>o.date.slice(0,7)).concat(today_().slice(0,7)))].sort().reverse()};
  d.ad=adMetrics_(p,all);
  if(!forGuidance){const c=d.cash;d.cash=Object.fromEntries(['bank','courier','balanceUpdated','balanceNeedsRefresh','available','pending','upcomingTotal','openingDate','updated','untrackedExpenses'].map(k=>[k,c[k]]));d.expenses.daily=[];d.days=d.days.map(x=>({name:x.name,orders:x.orders,sales:x.sales}));d.statuses=d.statuses.map(x=>({name:x.name,orders:x.orders}));}
  return d;
}
function dashboardSeed_Legacy(){
  const all=read_('Orders'),s=settings_(),types=customerTypes_(all),catalog=catalog_(),cash=cashView_(),inventory=inventoryMetrics_({},all),expense=expenseTotals_(read_('Expenses'),read_('Budgets'),V2.cutoff,today_(),today_());
  const orders=all.map(o=>{const f=finance_(o,s.profitRate),parts=partsFor_(o,s);return {id:o.id,date:o.date,name:o.name,phone:o.phone,district:o.district||'Unknown',invoice:o.invoice||'',cid:o.cid||'',tracking:o.tracking||'',status:o.status,approvalConflict:!!o.approvalConflict,source:source_(o),customerType:types[o.id]||'Unknown',historical:f.historical,sales:f.sales,fee:f.fee,cogs:f.cogs,gross:f.gross,contribution:f.contribution,estimate:f.estimate,known:f.known,partialVerified:o.status!=='Partial'||(!!o.amountVerified&&!!o.itemsVerified),parts:parts.map(l=>({sku:l.sku,name:l.name,category:l.category||'Other',qty:l.qty,returned:l.returned,cogs:l.cogs,share:l.share,sales:l.sales,fees:l.fees,gross:l.gross,estimate:l.estimate}))};});
  const options={districts:[...new Set(all.map(o=>o.district||'Unknown'))].sort(),categories:[...new Set(catalog.map(x=>x.category))].sort(),marketplaces:[...new Set(['Facebook','Website','WhatsApp','Other','Old Data'].concat(all.map(source_)))].sort(),months:[...new Set(all.map(o=>o.date.slice(0,7)).concat(today_().slice(0,7)))].sort().reverse()};
  const cashSafe=Object.fromEntries(['bank','courier','balanceUpdated','balanceNeedsRefresh','available','pending','upcomingTotal','openingDate','updated','untrackedExpenses'].map(k=>[k,cash[k]]));
  return {generatedAt:now_(),today:today_(),cutoff:V2.cutoff,historicalRate:V2.historicalRate,orders,expenseDaily:expense.daily,closedMonths:read_('Months').map(m=>({id:m.id,closed:!!m.closed})),inventory,cash:cashSafe,options,catalog:catalog.map(x=>({id:x.id,name:x.name,category:x.category,active:x.active,components:x.components||null}))};
}

function adMetrics_(p,orders){
  if(p.product||p.category||p.district||p.customerType||p.query)return {rows:[],verified:false,note:'Advertising attribution is available for date/marketplace selections only.'};
  const rows=read_('Expenses').filter(e=>!e.void&&/^(ad|ads|advertising)$/i.test(e.category)&&e.marketplace&&e.date>=p.start&&e.date<=p.end&&(!p.marketplace||e.marketplace===p.marketplace)),by={};
  rows.forEach(e=>{const x=by[e.marketplace]||(by[e.marketplace]={name:e.marketplace,cost:0,sales:0});x.cost+=e.amount;});
  const attributed=orders.filter(o=>o.date>=p.start&&o.date<=p.end&&o.adAttributed===true&&(!p.marketplace||source_(o)===p.marketplace));attributed.forEach(o=>{const g=by[source_(o)];if(g)g.sales+=finance_(o,settings_().profitRate).sales||0;});
  return {rows:Object.values(by),verified:attributed.length>0,note:'Only orders explicitly marked ad-attributed are included. Revenue/ad cost is not proof of incremental advertising profit.'};
}
function monthReview_Legacy(p){
  const month=str_(p.month),start=day_(month+'-01'),end=monthEnd_(month);if(start<V2.cutoff)throw Error('Before September 2026 is historical estimate only.');
  const orders=read_('Orders').filter(o=>o.date>=start&&o.date<=end&&o.status!=='Draft'),incomplete=orders.filter(o=>!final_(o)||!finance_(o,settings_().profitRate).known||o.approvalConflict);
  return {month,end,canClose:today_()>=end&&incomplete.length===0,openTooEarly:today_()<end,incomplete:incomplete.map(o=>({id:o.id,name:o.name,status:o.status,reason:finance_(o,settings_().profitRate).reason||'Approval conflict'})),expenses:expenseTotals_(read_('Expenses'),read_('Budgets'),start,end,today_()),existing:read_('Months').find(m=>m.id===month)||null,summary:analytics_({start,end})};
}
function closeMonth_(p){const r=monthReview_(p);if(r.existing?.closed)return r;if(!r.canClose)throw Error(r.openTooEarly?'Close on or after the last day of the month.':'Resolve open orders, missing costs/charges, partial verification and approval conflicts first.');if(!p.confirm)throw Error('Confirm all actual expenses are complete, including zero-expense categories. Bank settlement is not required.');const backup=DriveApp.getFileById(db_().getId()).makeCopy('Shoukhin month close '+r.month+' '+Date.now()).getUrl();put_('Months',{id:r.month,closed:true,closedAt:now_(),confirmedExpenses:true,gross:r.summary.gross,contribution:r.summary.contribution,expenses:r.expenses.actual,net:r.summary.contribution-r.expenses.actual,backup});audit_('Month close',r.month,'finalized');return monthReview_(p);}
function reopenMonth_(p){const m=read_('Months').find(x=>x.id===p.month);if(!m)throw Error('Month not found');if(!str_(p.reason))throw Error('Reopening reason required');m.closed=false;m.reopenedAt=now_();m.reason=str_(p.reason);put_('Months',m);audit_('Month reopen',m.id,m.reason);return monthReview_(p);}
function bootstrap_Legacy(){const p={start:today_().slice(0,7)+'-01',end:today_()};return {version:V2.version,today:today_(),settings:getSettings_(),catalog:catalog_(),combos:read_('Combos'),dashboard:dashboard_(p),dashboardSeed:dashboardSeed_(),migrated:!!props_().getProperty('SHD_HISTORY_IMPORTED')};}

/** Deterministic reminders run without an AI service. No external notification is sent. */
function hash_(x){let h=2166136261;for(let i=0;i<x.length;i++){h^=x.charCodeAt(i);h=Math.imul(h,16777619);}return (h>>>0).toString(16);}
function taskView_(){return read_('Tasks').sort((a,b)=>(a.status==='Done')-(b.status==='Done')||(a.dueDate||'9999').localeCompare(b.dueDate||'9999'));}
function saveTask_(p){
  const id=p.id||p.requestId||uid_(),old=read_('Tasks').find(t=>t.id===id);if(!str_(p.title))throw Error('Task title required');
  if(!['To-do','In Progress','Done'].includes(p.status||'To-do'))throw Error('Invalid task status');
  put_('Tasks',{id,title:str_(p.title).slice(0,250),note:str_(p.note).slice(0,5000),dueDate:p.dueDate?day_(p.dueDate):'',priority:['High','Medium','Low'].includes(p.priority)?p.priority:'Medium',status:p.status||'To-do',source:p.source==='AI'?'AI':p.source==='Reminder'?'Reminder':old?.source||'My Task',created:old?.created||now_(),updated:now_()});return taskView_();
}
function saveCheck_(p){
  const kinds=['orders','ads','bank','snooze'];if(!kinds.includes(p.kind))throw Error('Invalid confirmation');
  const date=day_(p.date||today_());if(p.kind!=='snooze'&&date>today_())throw Error('Cannot confirm future data');
  const id=p.kind==='snooze'?'snooze:'+str_(p.rule):p.kind+':'+date;
  if(p.kind==='ads'){
    const ads=x=>/^(ads?|advertising)$/i.test(str_(x.category));
    if(read_('Expenses').some(x=>!x.void&&ads(x)&&(x.periodStart||x.date)<=date&&(x.periodEnd||x.date)>=date&&Number(x.amount)>0))throw Error('Ad expense already covers this date. Review Expenses instead of confirming zero.');
    const categories=[...new Set(read_('Budgets').filter(x=>x.active&&ads(x)&&x.start<=date&&(!x.end||x.end>=date)).map(x=>x.category))];
    (categories.length?categories:['Ads']).forEach(category=>saveExpense_({id:'zero-ads:'+date+':'+category.toLowerCase(),date,category,amount:0,coverageComplete:true,note:'Confirmed no ad spend on this date'}));
  }
  put_('Checks',{id,kind:p.kind,date,note:str_(p.note),updated:now_()});return {saved:true};
}
function ruleSuggestions_(d){
  const s=settings_(),items=[],orders=read_('Orders'),checks=read_('Checks'),today=today_(),p=d.filter;
  function add(id,priority,title,what,why,next,action='Dashboard',check=''){
    if(checks.some(c=>c.id==='snooze:'+id&&c.date>today))return;
    items.push({id,priority,title,what,why,next,action,check,source:'Rule',updated:today});
  }
  const fmt=n=>'BDT '+Math.round(n||0).toLocaleString('en-US'),percent=n=>(n||0).toFixed(1)+'%',i=d.inventory,min=s.minSample;
  if(d.growth.sales!==null&&d.growth.sales<0)add('sales-fall','High','Sales are falling',`Sales changed by ${percent(d.growth.sales)} versus ${d.growth.previous.start} to ${d.growth.previous.end}.`,'Lower sales may be caused by fewer orders, smaller baskets or returns.','Compare daily orders and average order value before changing offers.');
  if(d.aov!==null&&d.aov<500&&d.soldOrders>=min)add('basket','Medium','Review average order value',`Average recognized order value is ${fmt(d.aov)} across ${d.soldOrders} sold orders.`,'Small baskets can leave little profit after delivery and packing.','Test a relevant add-on or combo and compare its margin, not sales alone.','Products & Combos');
  const month=analytics_({start:today.slice(0,7)+'-01',end:today}),prev=analytics_(previousPeriod_({start:today.slice(0,7)+'-01',end:today}));
  if(month.orders>prev.orders&&month.sales<=prev.sales&&prev.orders>=min)add('orders-not-sales','Medium','Orders rose without sales growth',`${month.orders} orders versus ${prev.orders}, but recognized sales did not increase.`,'More orders do not always mean more revenue.','Review pending/returned orders and basket values before increasing promotion.');
  if(month.aov&&prev.aov&&month.aov>prev.aov*1.1&&month.soldOrders>=min)add('aov-up','Low','Customers are spending more per order',`Average order value rose from ${fmt(prev.aov)} to ${fmt(month.aov)} in comparable month-to-date periods.`,'Successful combos or higher-value items may be contributing.','Check the product table and keep profitable offers visible.');
  if(d.products.length&&d.sales>0){const top=d.products.slice().sort((a,b)=>b.sales-a.sales)[0];if(top.sales/d.sales>.6)add('concentration','Medium','Sales depend on one product',`${top.name} contributes ${percent(top.sales/d.sales*100)} of selected sales.`,'A stockout or demand change could affect much of your revenue.','Protect its supply and test other products with healthy margins.','Products & Combos');}
  if(s.salesTarget&&today.slice(8)!=='01'&&month.sales/s.salesTarget<Number(today.slice(8))/Number(monthEnd_(today.slice(0,7)).slice(8)))add('target','Medium','Monthly sales target needs attention',`${fmt(month.sales)} of your ${fmt(s.salesTarget)} monthly target is recorded.`,'The current pace is below the evenly distributed target.','Check the remaining gap and focus on available products with positive contribution.');
  const weekday={};d.days.forEach(x=>{const day=new Date(x.name+'T00:00:00Z').getUTCDay();const g=weekday[day]||(weekday[day]={orders:0,days:0});g.orders+=x.orders;g.days++;});
  const best=Object.keys(weekday).sort((a,b)=>weekday[b].orders/weekday[b].days-weekday[a].orders/weekday[a].days)[0];if(best!==undefined&&weekday[best].days>=4&&d.orders>=min*4)add('weekday','Low','Prepare for your busier weekday',`${['Sunday','Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday'][best]} has the highest average order count in this selection.`,'Order preparation capacity can be planned around repeated demand.','Prepare stock and packing ahead; this pattern is descriptive, not a guarantee.');
  if(month.sales>prev.sales&&month.estimatedMargin!==null&&prev.estimatedMargin!==null&&month.estimatedMargin<prev.estimatedMargin)add('margin-fall','High','Sales grew but estimated margin fell',`Estimated margin is ${percent(month.estimatedMargin)}, previously ${percent(prev.estimatedMargin)}.`,'Costs or discounts may be absorbing the extra sales; both periods must have complete inputs for a fair comparison.','Review product costs, courier fees and expense coverage.','Expenses');
  if(d.projectedNet!==null&&d.projectedNet<0)add('loss','High','Estimated net profit is negative',`Estimated net is ${fmt(d.projectedNet)} for the selected actual-tracking period.`,'Known revenue is below known direct costs and accrued operating expenses.','Complete missing inputs first, then review the largest avoidable expenses.','Expenses');
  d.products.filter(x=>x.sales>0&&x.orders>=min).slice(0,15).forEach(x=>{
    if(x.gross!==null&&x.gross/x.sales<.15)add('weak-margin:'+x.id,'High','Review a product with a weak gross margin',`${x.name}: sales ${fmt(x.sales)}, known gross profit ${fmt(x.gross)}.`,'High sales can still leave little margin; incomplete costs must be resolved.','Check supplier price and discounts before spending more to promote it.','Products & Combos');
    if(x.gross/x.sales>.3&&x.qty<d.qty*.1)add('promote:'+x.id,'Low','Test a profitable product offer',`${x.name} has a relatively strong known gross margin but a small share of units sold.`,'A modest promotion may expand profitable sales if demand exists.','Run a small test and compare added contribution with the promotion cost.','Products & Combos');
    if(x.net!==null&&x.gross>0&&x.net<0)add('allocation:'+x.id,'Medium','Review allocated product expenses',`${x.name} has positive gross profit but negative net after expense allocation.`,'The net figure includes a monthly share of operating expenses, not only direct costs.','Review the disclosed allocation basis before discontinuing the product.');
  });
  i.rows.forEach(x=>{
    if(x.qty===0)add('empty:'+x.id,'High','A product is out of stock',`${x.name} currently has no available units.`,'New commitments may not be fulfillable until stock arrives.','Check replenishment timing before accepting orders or promoting it.','Inventory');
    else if(x.reorderNow)add('reorder:'+x.id,'High','Reorder stock soon',`${x.name}: ${x.qty} available; estimated coverage ${x.coverage===null?'not known':x.coverage.toFixed(1)+' days'}; supplier lead time ${x.lead} days.`,'Recent demand may use the remaining units before replenishment arrives.','Check the physical count and supplier timing, then order '+(x.recommended===null?'an appropriate quantity.':x.recommended+' units as a starting estimate.'),'Purchases & Bank');
    if(x.coverage!==null&&x.coverage>s.stockTargetDays*2)add('excess:'+x.id,'Medium','Review excess stock',`${x.name} covers about ${x.coverage.toFixed(0)} days at the recent sales pace.`,'Long stock coverage ties up cash and may increase unsold inventory.','Consider a smaller next purchase and avoid replenishing automatically.','Purchases & Bank');
    if(x.dead)add('dead:'+x.id,'Medium','Money is tied up in dead stock',`${x.name} has ${x.qty} units worth ${fmt(x.value)} and no recognized demand within ${s.deadDays} days.`,'Cash remains tied up while the product is not moving.','Inspect condition and test a clearance offer or suitable combo before buying more.','Products & Combos');
    if(!x.verified)add('count:'+x.id,'Medium','Confirm the physical stock count',`${x.name} still uses a provisional or incomplete count.`,'Reorder and stock-value suggestions are only as reliable as the baseline.','Count available stock excluding reserved parcels and enter the as-of date.','Inventory');
    if(x.components&&x.qty<=s.leadDays)add('combo:'+x.id,'Medium','A component limits combo availability',`${x.name} can currently be assembled ${x.qty} times.`,'Combo capacity uses the smallest component stock divided by the quantity required per combo.','Check the combo definition and replenish the limiting component.','Products & Combos');
  });
  const previousProducts=analytics_(previousPeriod_(p)).products;
  d.products.forEach(x=>{const old=previousProducts.find(y=>y.id===x.id);if(!old||old.qty<min)return;if(x.qty>old.qty*1.3)add('demand-up:'+x.id,'Low','Product demand is growing',`${x.name} sold ${x.qty} units, previously ${old.qty} in the comparison period.`,'A sustained increase may require earlier replenishment.','Check stock coverage and the length of both periods before increasing purchases.','Inventory');else if(x.qty<old.qty*.7&&i.rows.find(y=>y.id===x.id)?.qty>0)add('demand-down:'+x.id,'Medium','Product demand is slowing',`${x.name} sold ${x.qty} units, previously ${old.qty}; stock is currently available.`,'Slower movement could create excess stock.','Review price, promotion and customer feedback before buying more.','Products & Combos');});
  if(d.finalized>=min&&d.returnRate>10)add('returns','High','Review the return rate',`${d.returned} of ${d.finalized} finalized parcels were returned (${percent(d.returnRate)}).`,'Returns can consume courier fees and delay stock availability.','Inspect recorded reasons and distinguish delivery problems from product issues.','Orders');
  if(d.finalized>=min&&d.partialRate>10)add('partials','Medium','Partial deliveries need attention',`${d.partial} finalized orders were partial (${percent(d.partialRate)}).`,'Repeated partial acceptance may indicate unclear offers or product expectations.','Review which items were declined and confirm the collected amounts.','Orders');
  if(d.partialUnverified)add('partial-data','High','Verify partial delivery details',`${d.partialUnverified} selected partial orders have incomplete amount or item verification.`,'Their complete sales or product profit cannot be calculated yet.','Open each partial order and confirm the final collected amount and returned items.','Orders');
  if(d.conflicts)add('status-conflict','High','Resolve courier status conflicts',`${d.conflicts} selected orders have conflicting delivery/approval information.`,'A provisional delivery must not be treated as an approved settlement.','Refresh status and check the exact parcel in Steadfast.','Courier');
  const customerTypes=customerTypes_(orders),selected=orders.filter(o=>o.date>=p.start&&o.date<=p.end&&matchOrder_(o,p,customerTypes));
  const stale=selected.filter(o=>!final_(o)&&o.status!=='Draft'&&daysBetween_(o.date,today)>7);
  if(stale.length)add('stale-parcels','High','Follow up on aging open orders',`${stale.length} selected orders have remained open for more than seven days.`,'An old status may mean delivery is delayed or a sync is missing.','Refresh courier status, then follow up on unresolved parcels.','Courier');
  const approval=selected.filter(o=>o.status.includes('Approval Pending')).length;if(approval)add('approval','Medium','Delivery approval is still pending',`${approval} selected parcels are waiting for courier approval.`,'These statuses are not final delivered/returned outcomes.','Check again after approval and keep pending money outside available funds.','Courier');
  const unreceived=selected.filter(o=>['Returned','Partial'].includes(o.status)&&o.dispatched&&Object.values(components_(o.lines)).reduce((a,v)=>a+v,0)>0&&!Object.values(o.stockReturns||{}).some(v=>v>0));
  if(unreceived.length)add('return-receipt','Medium','Check physical return receipts',`${unreceived.length} return/partial orders have no physical stock receipt recorded.`,'Courier status alone does not confirm that sellable items reached your store.','Inspect the actual returned items before recording cumulative received quantities.','Orders');
  for(const [kind,list] of [['district',d.districts],['marketplace',d.marketplaces],['product',d.products]])list.filter(x=>x.finalized>=min&&x.returnRate>d.returnRate+10).forEach(x=>add('return-'+kind+':'+(x.id||x.name),'High','Review returns for this '+kind,`${x.name}: ${percent(x.returnRate)} returned across ${x.finalized} finalized orders; selected average ${percent(d.returnRate)}.`,'This difference is large enough to investigate, but it does not identify the cause.','Review return reasons and avoid broad restrictions based on a small sample.','Orders'));
  if(d.repeatRate!==null&&d.identifiedCustomers>=min)add('retention','Low','Review repeat customer activity',`${percent(d.repeatRate)} of identified unique customers in this selection are returning customers.`,'Matching uses available phone history and any explicit New/Repeat override.','Review what repeat customers buy and consider a relevant follow-up offer.','Customers');
  if(month.repeatRate!==null&&prev.repeatRate!==null&&month.identifiedCustomers>=min&&month.repeatRate<prev.repeatRate)add('repeat-fall','Medium','Repeat customer share is falling',`Repeat share is ${percent(month.repeatRate)}, compared with ${percent(prev.repeatRate)} previously.`,'Lower repeat activity may reflect customer satisfaction or a normal repurchase cycle.','Check product feedback and purchase frequency before planning follow-ups.','Customers');
  if(d.marketplaces.length>1&&d.net!==null){const best=d.marketplaces.filter(x=>x.sales>0&&x.orders>=min).sort((a,b)=>b.net/b.sales-a.net/a.sales)[0];if(best)add('market-margin','Low','Review a stronger marketplace',`${best.name} has the highest net margin among sufficiently sampled selected marketplaces.`,'The result includes allocated operating expenses and depends on source accuracy.','Compare capacity and attributable ad costs before increasing effort there.');}
  if(selected.some(o=>source_(o)==='Old Data'))add('old-source','Low','Old Data has no verified original marketplace','Imported history is grouped under Old Data.','Courier exports do not necessarily identify Facebook, Website or WhatsApp as the original source.','Keep it separate unless you can verify the source; do not guess.');
  if(d.incomplete)add('missing-profit','High','Complete actual profit inputs',`${d.incomplete} selected finalized orders have missing or conflicting profit inputs.`,'Known-profit subtotals can be misleading until missing orders are completed.','Assign products/costs, confirm charges and verify partial collection.','Orders');
  if(d.historyIncomplete)add('history-missing','Medium','Historical estimate is incomplete',`${d.historyIncomplete} historical finalized orders lack required amounts or charges.`,'Missing monetary fields are not assumed to be zero.','Use the courier export or verified manual details to complete them.','Orders');
  const noLines=selected.filter(o=>o.date>=V2.cutoff&&!(o.lines||[]).length).length;if(noLines)add('assign','Medium','Assign products to imported orders',`${noLines} selected September-onward orders have no product lines.`,'Product/category filters cannot include unidentified items.','Add product or combo quantities without changing verified courier order totals.','Orders');
  d.expenses.rows.filter(x=>x.actual>x.projection&&x.projection>0).forEach(x=>add('budget:'+x.month+':'+x.category,'Medium','Review an expense above budget',`${x.category} in ${x.month}: actual ${fmt(x.actual)}, accrued budget ${fmt(x.projection)}.`,'A one-time purchase or recurring increase may explain the difference.','Check coverage dates and review whether the higher cost was planned.','Expenses'));
  if(!d.ad.verified)add('ad-tracking','Low','Advertising performance needs attribution','No usable tracked-sales comparison is available for this selection.','Total business sales must not be assumed to come from ads.','Set the expense marketplace and mark orders as ad-attributed only when verified.','Expenses');
  else d.ad.rows.filter(x=>x.cost>x.sales).forEach(x=>add('ad-spend:'+x.name,'High','Ad spend exceeds tracked sales',`${x.name}: ad cost ${fmt(x.cost)}, attributed sales ${fmt(x.sales)}.`,'This comparison may also be affected by missing attribution or conversion delays.','Verify attribution and period coverage before changing the campaign budget.','Expenses'));
  if(d.cash.courier>0)add('courier-ready','Low','Courier funds are available to request',`The last verified Steadfast balance is ${fmt(d.cash.courier)}.`,'This is requestable courier money, not cash already in the bank.','Refresh the balance and consider a payment request if upcoming bills need funding.','Purchases & Bank');
  if(d.cash.pending>0)add('transfer-pending','Medium','Confirm pending courier transfers',`${fmt(d.cash.pending)} is recorded as requested but not received.`,'Requested funds must not be counted again as a new income or bank receipt.','Match the payment reference and confirm the actual bank receipt.','Purchases & Bank');
  if(d.cash.bank===null)add('opening-bank','Medium','Enter the opening bank balance','Bank tracking has no opening balance/date yet.','Historical sales cannot determine how much money is currently in your bank.','Enter a verified opening balance, then record subsequent cash movements.','Purchases & Bank');
  if(d.cash.bank!==null&&d.cash.upcomingTotal>d.cash.bank)add('bills','High','Upcoming bills exceed bank funds',`Outstanding bills total ${fmt(d.cash.upcomingTotal)}; recorded bank balance is ${fmt(d.cash.bank)}.`,'Stock purchases, salaries and other due payments may compete for the same funds.','Review due dates and courier funds before committing to another purchase.','Purchases & Bank');
  if(d.cash.bank!==null&&d.cash.bank<s.cashReserve)add('reserve','High','Bank balance is below your reserve',`Recorded bank balance ${fmt(d.cash.bank)} is below the saved reserve of ${fmt(s.cashReserve)}.`,'A new paid purchase could leave too little money for essential expenses.','Review immediate payments and consider a smaller purchase.','Purchases & Bank');
  if(d.cash.untrackedExpenses)add('legacy-payments','Medium','Review payment tracking for old expenses',`${d.cash.untrackedExpenses} expense records do not yet have payment tracking.`,'Opening cash should already include old payments; adding them again would double-count.','Verify which bills remain unpaid and enter only new payments after your opening balance.','Expenses');
  if(d.cash.purchases.some(x=>!x.receivedDate))add('purchase-receipt','Medium','Confirm purchased stock receipts','Some purchase records are still awaiting stock receipt.','A paid purchase does not mean goods are physically available to sell.','Record receipt on arrival; do not count these units as available before then.','Purchases & Bank');
  const hour=Number(Utilities.formatDate(new Date(),V2.tz,'H')),due=hour>=s.reminderHour;
  if(due&&!checks.some(c=>c.id==='orders:'+today))add('orders:'+today,'High',"Confirm today's order update",'Today’s order data has not been marked complete.','An empty list could mean no orders or an unfinished update.','Import/sync and review today’s orders, or confirm No orders today.','Orders','orders');
  const coveredAds=read_('Expenses').some(x=>!x.void&&/^(ad|ads|advertising)$/i.test(x.category)&&(x.periodStart||x.date)<=today&&(x.periodEnd||x.date)>=today&&x.coverageComplete!==false);
  if(due&&!coveredAds&&!checks.some(c=>c.id==='ads:'+today))add('ads:'+today,'Medium',"Confirm today's ad cost",'No complete ad-cost entry or zero-spend confirmation covers today.','Estimated profit will continue to use applicable projections.','Enter actual ad cost or confirm no ad spending today.','Expenses','ads');
  if(due&&!checks.some(c=>c.kind==='bank'&&c.date>=addDays_(today,-6)))add('bank:'+today,'Medium','Reconcile the bank balance','The app balance has not been marked reconciled during the last seven days.','Fees, deposits or payments outside the app can make its balance differ from the bank.','Compare with your bank and record missing transactions before confirming.','Purchases & Bank','bank');
  const earliest=orders.filter(o=>o.date>=V2.cutoff).map(o=>o.date).sort()[0]||V2.cutoff;
  monthsIn_(earliest,today).filter(m=>monthEnd_(m)<=today&&!isClosed_(m+'-01')).forEach(m=>add('close:'+m,'High','Close the month: '+m,`The last day of ${m} has arrived/passed and its accounts remain open.`,'Actual net profit stays blank until order outcomes and actual expenses are complete.','Review missing inputs and use Close Month; bank settlement is not required.','Month Closing'));
  if(d.orders<min)add('sample','Low','Use caution with this small sample',`Only ${d.orders} orders match the current selection.`,'A few orders can produce large percentages without showing a stable pattern.','Widen the time range before making pricing, advertising or district restrictions.');
  const compare=d.growth.previous;if(d.growth.sales===null)add('growth-base','Low','Growth has no valid comparison base',`The comparison period ${compare.start} to ${compare.end} has zero recorded sales.`,'Percentage growth from zero is undefined.','Compare absolute sales amounts or select a period with a usable baseline.');
  return items.sort((a,b)=>['High','Medium','Low'].indexOf(a.priority)-['High','Medium','Low'].indexOf(b.priority));
}


/** 2.2: local-first views, explicit imports, dated sales, stock edits and optional read-only ads. */
function touch_(){props_().setProperty('SHD_REV',uid_());}
function revision_(){return {revision:props_().getProperty('SHD_REV')||'',today:today_()};}
function basePut_(n,x){const r=basePutLegacy_(n,x);if(!['Log','Stage'].includes(n))touch_();return r;}
function writeAll_(n,x){const r=writeAllLegacy_(n,x);if(!['Log','Stage'].includes(n))touch_();return r;}
function deliveryDay_(o){
  const v=str_(o.deliveredAt);if(!v)return '';try{if(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(v)){if(/(?:Z|[+-]\d{2}:?\d{2})$/.test(v))return Utilities.formatDate(new Date(v),V2.tz,'yyyy-MM-dd');return day_(v.slice(0,10));}}catch(e){}return '';
}
function eventDate_(o){return ['Delivered','Partial'].includes(o.status)?deliveryDay_(o):o.date;}
function importRecord_(r){const o=importRecord_Legacy(r);o.deliveredAt=str_(r['Delivery Time']||r['Delivered At']||r['Delivery Date']);o.returnCharge=num_(r['Return Charge'])??0;return o;}
function importMerged_(old,n){const o=importMerged_Legacy(old,n);for(const k of ['total','cod','shippingCharge','codCharge'])if(num_(n[k])===null&&num_(old[k])!==null)o[k]=old[k];if(!n.deliveredAt)o.deliveredAt=old.deliveredAt||'';if(!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(n.raw||{},'Return Charge'))o.returnCharge=old.returnCharge??n.returnCharge;return o;}
function saveOrder_(p){
 const old=read_('Orders').find(o=>o.id===p.id);const dt=p.deliveredAt===undefined?old?.deliveredAt||'':str_(p.deliveredAt);
 if(dt){const day=day_(dt);if(day>today_()||day<p.date)throw Error('Delivery date must be between order date and today');guardPeriod_(day,day);}
 const r=saveOrder_Legacy(p);r.deliveredAt=dt;put_('Orders',r);return r;
}
function analytics_(p,all,s){
 const orders=all||read_('Orders'),event=orders.filter(o=>eventDate_(o)).map(o=>({...o,originalDate:o.originalDate||o.date,date:eventDate_(o)}));
 const d=analytics_Legacy(p,event,s);includeCrossCutoffEstimates_(d);if(d.incomplete||d.historyIncomplete){d.displayNet=null;d.displayMargin=null;d.projectedNet=null;if(d.incomplete)d.gross=null;}
 d.months.forEach(m=>{if(m.incomplete||m.historyIncomplete){m.projectedNet=null;m.net=null;}});return d;
}
function dashboardSeed_(){const d=dashboardSeed_Legacy(),all=read_('Orders');const by=new Map(all.map(o=>[o.id,o]));d.orders.forEach(o=>{const raw=by.get(o.id);o.createdDate=o.date;o.eventDate=eventDate_(raw);o.deliveredAt=deliveryDay_(raw);o.cod=num_(raw.cod);o.total=num_(raw.total);});d.revision=revision_().revision;d.targets=read_('Settings').filter(x=>x.id.startsWith('target:'));return d;}
function bootstrap_(){return {version:V2.version,today:today_(),settings:getSettings_(),catalog:catalog_(),combos:read_('Combos'),dashboardSeed:dashboardSeed_(),migrated:!!props_().getProperty('SHD_HISTORY_IMPORTED'),revision:revision_().revision};}
function monthReview_(p){const r=monthReview_Legacy(p),start=p.month+'-01';const all=read_('Orders');const relevant=all.filter(o=>((eventDate_(o)>=start&&eventDate_(o)<=r.end)||(['Delivered','Partial'].includes(o.status)&&!deliveryDay_(o)&&o.date<=r.end))&&o.status!=='Draft');r.incomplete=relevant.filter(o=>!finance_(o,15).known||o.approvalConflict||!eventDate_(o)).map(o=>({id:o.id,name:o.name,status:o.status,reason:!eventDate_(o)?'Delivery date missing':finance_(o,15).reason||'Approval conflict'}));r.canClose=today_()>=r.end&&!r.incomplete.length;return r;}
function getSettings_(){const s=getSettings_Legacy();for(const k of ['geminiModel','aiEnabled','aiLimit','aiDataMode','hasGemini','suggestionLimit'])delete s[k];return {...s,metaAccount:s.metaAccount||'1430584315368882',metaVersion:s.metaVersion||'',usdRate:s.usdRate??'',vatRate:s.vatRate??15,hasMeta:!!props_().getProperty('SHD_META_TOKEN')};}
function saveSettings_(p){
 const s=settings_();for(const k of ['businessName','metaAccount','metaVersion'])if(p[k]!==undefined)s[k]=str_(p[k]);
 if(s.metaAccount&&!/^\d+$/.test(s.metaAccount))throw Error('Meta account ID must contain digits');
 if(s.metaVersion&&!/^v\d+\.0$/.test(s.metaVersion))throw Error('Use the supported Graph API version shown in your Meta app, for example v23.0');
 for(const k of ['usdRate','vatRate','deadDays'])if(p[k]!==undefined&&p[k]!=='')s[k]=amount_(p[k],k);
 if(s.vatRate>100)throw Error('VAT rate cannot exceed 100');if(s.deadDays<1)throw Error('Dead stock days must be positive');
 if(p.logo!==undefined){if(p.logo&&!/^data:image\/(png|jpeg|webp);base64,[A-Za-z0-9+/=]+$/.test(p.logo))throw Error('Use PNG/JPEG/WebP logo');if(p.logo.length>28000)throw Error('Logo too large');s.logo=p.logo;}
 for(const [k,v] of [['sfKey','SHD_SF_KEY'],['sfSecret','SHD_SF_SECRET'],['metaToken','SHD_META_TOKEN']])if(str_(p[k]))props_().setProperty(v,str_(p[k]).replace(/\s/g,''));
 s.aiEnabled=false;s.aiDataMode='off';s.syncEnabled=false;s.profitRate=15;put_('Settings',s);removeCourierSchedule_();return getSettings_();
}
function saveTarget_(p){const month=day_(p.month+'-01').slice(0,7);put_('Settings',{id:'target:'+month,month,amount:amount_(p.amount,'Monthly sales target')});return dashboardSeed_();}
function setupSimple_(){
 const db=db_();let tab=db.getSheetByName('Stock Entry');if(!tab){tab=db.insertSheet('Stock Entry');tab.getRange(1,1,1,6).setValues([['Product ID','Product','Counted available','Count date','Note','Result']]);tab.setFrozenRows(1);tab.hideColumns(1);}
 mirrorStock_();const triggers=ScriptApp.getProjectTriggers();if(!triggers.some(t=>t.getHandlerFunction()==='stockSheetEdited'))ScriptApp.newTrigger('stockSheetEdited').forSpreadsheet(db).onEdit().create();touch_();
}
function stockSheetEdited(e){if(!e||!e.range)return;const n=e.range.getSheet().getName();if(n==='Stock Entry'||n==='Products'||n.startsWith('SHD_'))touch_();}
function stockFingerprint_(row){return JSON.stringify([str_(row[0]),row[2],sheetDay_(row[3]),str_(row[4])]);}
function sheetDay_(v){return v instanceof Date?Utilities.formatDate(v,V2.tz,'yyyy-MM-dd'):str_(v);}
function mirrorStock_(){
 const tab=db_().getSheetByName('Stock Entry');if(!tab)return;
 const rows=tab.getLastRow()>1?tab.getRange(2,1,tab.getLastRow()-1,6).getValues():[],checks=read_('Checks'),view=stockView_();let changed=false;
 for(const p of view.products){let i=rows.findIndex(x=>str_(x[0])===p.id);const old=i>=0?rows[i]:null,ci=checks.findIndex(c=>c.id==='stock-sheet:'+p.id),check=checks[ci];if(old&&check&&check.fingerprint!==stockFingerprint_(old))continue;
 const row=[p.id,safeCell_(p.name),p.qty,today_(),'',p.verified?'Current available':'Provisional — enter physical count'];if(old&&JSON.stringify(old)===JSON.stringify(row))continue;
 if(i<0)rows.push(row);else rows[i]=row;const record={id:'stock-sheet:'+p.id,fingerprint:stockFingerprint_(row)};if(ci<0)checks.push(record);else checks[ci]=record;changed=true;
 }
 if(changed&&rows.length){tab.getRange(2,1,rows.length,6).setValues(rows);writeAll_('Checks',checks);}
}
function applyStockEntries_(){
 const tab=db_().getSheetByName('Stock Entry');if(!tab||tab.getLastRow()<2)return;
 const rows=tab.getRange(2,1,tab.getLastRow()-1,6).getValues(),checks=read_('Checks');let changed=false;
 for(let i=0;i<rows.length;i++){const row=rows[i],id=str_(row[0]);if(!id)continue;const fp=stockFingerprint_(row),prev=checks.find(c=>c.id==='stock-sheet:'+id);if(prev?.fingerprint===fp)continue;
 try{saveStock_Legacy({sku:id,mode:'baseline',qty:row[2],date:sheetDay_(row[3]),note:str_(row[4]),requestId:'sheet:'+uid_()});basePut_('Checks',{id:'stock-sheet:'+id,fingerprint:fp});tab.getRange(i+2,6).setValue('Saved '+now_());changed=true;}catch(err){tab.getRange(i+2,6).setValue('Not saved: '+err.message);}
 }
 if(changed)touch_();
}
function saveStock_(p){applyStockEntries_();const r=saveStock_Legacy(p);mirrorStock_();return r;}
function saveDamage_(p){
 const id='damage:'+str_(p.requestId);if(!p.requestId)throw Error('Damage request ID required');const date=day_(p.date);guardPeriod_(date,date);if(date>today_())throw Error('Damage cannot be future dated');
 const product=products_().find(x=>x.id===p.sku);if(!product||num_(product.cost)===null)throw Error('Select a physical product with purchase cost');
 const qty=amount_(p.qty,'Damaged quantity');if(qty<=0)throw Error('Quantity must be positive');const prior=read_('Expenses').find(x=>x.id===id);const stock=read_('Stock').find(x=>x.id===p.sku);if(!stock)throw Error('Set available stock first');
 const moved=(stock.movements||[]).some(m=>m.id===id);if(!moved&&stockView_().products.find(x=>x.id===p.sku).qty<qty)throw Error('Insufficient available stock');if(date<stock.date)throw Error('Damage predates stock baseline');
 if(prior&&(prior.damageSku!==p.sku||prior.damageQty!==qty||prior.date!==date))throw Error('This damage request was already used');
 if(!prior)put_('Expenses',{id,date,periodStart:date,periodEnd:date,category:'Damage',amount:qty*product.cost,damageSku:p.sku,damageQty:qty,nonCash:true,coverageComplete:true,payments:[],note:str_(p.note)});
 if(!moved){stock.movements=(stock.movements||[]).concat({id,date,qty:-qty,note:'Damage: '+str_(p.note)});put_('Stock',stock);}
 mirrorStock_();return {stock:stockView_(),expense:read_('Expenses').find(x=>x.id===id)};
}
function saveExpense_(p){const old=read_('Expenses').find(x=>x.id===p.id);if(old?.nonCash||old?.metaManaged)throw Error('Use the original Damage or Ads entry to prevent double counting');if(/^(ads?|advertising)$/i.test(str_(p.category)))p.category='Ads';return saveExpense_Legacy(p);}
function guidanceView_(p){
 const d=dashboard_(p,true),items=[];const add=(title,what,why,next,page)=>items.push({title,what,why,next,page});
 const missingDate=read_('Orders').filter(o=>['Delivered','Partial'].includes(o.status)&&!deliveryDay_(o));
 if(missingDate.length)add('Delivery dates are missing',missingDate.length+' delivered/partial orders have no verified delivery date.','They cannot be placed in the correct sales day or month.','Import the delivery-date column or enter the verified date in the order.','Orders');
 if(d.incomplete)add('Complete profit inputs',d.incomplete+' finalized orders have incomplete costs, charges or approval information.','A complete profit figure cannot be calculated from those records.','Assign products, verify courier charges and review partial collections.','Orders');
 if(d.inventory.low)add('Review low stock',d.inventory.low+' products/combos are at or below the reorder point.','Availability may limit new orders.','Check physical quantities and buy only the items you need.','Inventory');
 if(d.inventory.dead)add('Review slow stock',d.inventory.dead+' items hold '+d.inventory.deadValue+' BDT without recent recorded sales.','Cash remains tied up in this stock.','Check demand and consider a suitable bundle or promotion.','Inventory');
 if(d.problemRate>10)add('Review returns and cancellations',d.problemRate.toFixed(1)+'% of finalized selected orders were returned/cancelled.','The count alone does not identify the cause.','Review addresses, customer confirmation and product issues before changing your process.','Orders');
 if(d.growth.sales!==null&&d.growth.sales<0)add('Delivered sales are lower',Math.abs(d.growth.sales).toFixed(1)+'% below the comparison period.','Missing dates or late delivery can affect this comparison.','Check data completeness and compare orders received alongside delivered sales.','Dashboard');
 if(d.cash.bank===null)add('Enter opening bank balance','Bank balance is not initialized.','Courier sales do not tell us your current bank funds.','Enter a verified bank balance and its starting date.','Purchases & Bank');
 if(!d.cash.balanceUpdated)add('Refresh courier balance','No courier balance has been fetched.','Missing is not zero.','Use Check balance in Courier when you need the latest amount.','Courier');
 const prev=addDays_(today_().slice(0,7)+'-01',-1).slice(0,7);if(prev>='2026-09'&&!isClosed_(prev+'-01'))add('Review the previous month',prev+' is not closed.','Closing confirms reconciled actual expenses and delivery records.','Complete the month review and close when all required data is entered.','Month Closing');
 return {filter:d.filter,suggestions:items,generatedAt:now_()};
}
function adView_(){return {rows:read_('AdDaily').sort((a,b)=>b.date.localeCompare(a.date)),settings:getSettings_()};}
function saveAd_(p){
 const date=day_(p.date);guardPeriod_(date,date);if(date>today_())throw Error('Ad spend cannot be future dated');const s=getSettings_(),account=str_(p.account||s.metaAccount);if(!/^\d+$/.test(account))throw Error('Ad account required');
 const id=account+':'+date,old=read_('AdDaily').find(x=>x.id===id),usd=amount_(p.usd,'USD spend'),rate=old?old.rate:amount_(p.rate??s.usdRate,'USD to BDT rate'),vat=old?old.vat:amount_(p.vat??s.vatRate,'VAT rate');if(rate<=0||vat>100)throw Error('Check exchange/VAT rate');
 const expenseId='meta:'+id,others=read_('Expenses').filter(x=>x.id!==expenseId&&!x.void&&/^(ads?|advertising)$/i.test(x.category)&&(x.periodStart||x.date)<=date&&(x.periodEnd||x.date)>=date);
 if(others.length)throw Error('An Ads expense already covers '+date+'. Reconcile it before importing Meta spend to avoid double counting.');
 const messages=p.messages===null||p.messages===undefined||p.messages===''?null:amount_(p.messages,'Conversations');if(messages!==null&&!Number.isInteger(messages))throw Error('Conversation count must be a whole number');
 const row={id,account,date,usd,rate,vat,amount:Math.round(usd*rate*(1+vat/100)*100)/100,messages,origin:p.origin||'Manual',updated:now_()};
 const expense=read_('Expenses').find(x=>x.id===expenseId);if((expense?.payments||[]).reduce((a,x)=>a+x.amount,0)>row.amount)throw Error('Updated spend is below recorded payments; reconcile first');
 put_('Expenses',{id:expenseId,date,periodStart:date,periodEnd:date,category:'Ads',marketplace:'Facebook',amount:row.amount,coverageComplete:true,payments:expense?.payments||[],metaManaged:true,note:'USD '+usd+' × '+rate+' + '+vat+'% VAT'});put_('AdDaily',row);return adView_();
}
function metaGet_(path,params){
 const s=getSettings_(),token=props_().getProperty('SHD_META_TOKEN');if(!token||!s.metaVersion)throw Error('Save Meta token and a supported Graph API version in Settings');
 const url='https://graph.facebook.com/'+s.metaVersion+'/'+path+'?'+Object.entries(params||{}).map(([k,v])=>encodeURIComponent(k)+'='+encodeURIComponent(v)).join('&');
 const r=UrlFetchApp.fetch(url,{headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+token},muteHttpExceptions:true});let j;try{j=JSON.parse(r.getContentText());}catch(e){throw Error('Meta returned an invalid response');}if(r.getResponseCode()>=300||j.error)throw Error('Meta request failed (code '+(j.error?.code||r.getResponseCode())+'). Check token, ads_read permission and API version.');return j;
}
function syncAds_(p){
 const start=day_(p.start),end=day_(p.end);if(end<start||daysBetween_(start,end)>31||end>today_())throw Error('Sync a past/current range of up to 31 days');guardPeriod_(start,end);
 const s=getSettings_();if(!(Number(s.usdRate)>0))throw Error('Set USD to BDT rate first');const account=metaGet_('act_'+s.metaAccount,{fields:'currency,timezone_name'});if(account.currency!=='USD')throw Error('This integration expects a USD account');if(account.timezone_name!==V2.tz)throw Error('Meta account timezone is '+account.timezone_name+'. Use daily manual entries aligned to Dhaka time; automatic sync is blocked to avoid misleading comparisons.');
 const collected=[];let after='';for(let page=0;page<20;page++){const params={fields:'date_start,date_stop,spend,actions',time_increment:1,level:'account',time_range:JSON.stringify({since:start,until:end}),limit:100};if(after)params.after=after;const r=metaGet_('act_'+s.metaAccount+'/insights',params);if(!Array.isArray(r.data))throw Error('Meta insights missing data');collected.push(...r.data);if(!r.paging?.next){after='';break;}after=r.paging?.cursors?.after;if(!after)throw Error('Unsupported Meta pagination');}if(after)throw Error('Meta response exceeded safe page limit; narrow date range');
 const rows=collected.map(x=>{if(x.date_start!==x.date_stop||x.date_start<start||x.date_start>end)throw Error('Unexpected Meta report dates');const action=(x.actions||[]).find(a=>a.action_type==='onsite_conversion.messaging_conversation_started_7d');return {date:x.date_start,usd:x.spend,messages:action?Number(action.value):null,origin:'Meta API'};});
 // Only explicit API rows are imported; absent days and missing message metrics are not guessed zero.
 const results=[];for(const row of rows){try{saveAd_(row);results.push({date:row.date,saved:true});}catch(e){results.push({date:row.date,saved:false,error:e.message});}}return {results,view:adView_(),note:'VAT included. Missing conversation metrics stay unknown. This is comparison, not order attribution.'};
}
function savePurchase_(p){
 // Initialize receipt IDs before adding a new same-day purchase to avoid absorbing it into a prior count.
 for(const b of read_('Stock'))if(!Array.isArray(b.purchaseIds)){b.purchaseIds=read_('Purchases').filter(x=>x.receivedDate&&x.receivedDate<=b.date).map(x=>x.id);put_('Stock',b);}
 const r=savePurchase_Legacy(p);mirrorStock_();return r;
}
function extraCourier_(p){if(p.kind!=='balance')throw Error('Use the Steadfast portal for this operation');return refreshBalance_();}

function saveBudget_(p){if(/^(ads?|advertising)$/i.test(str_(p.category)))p.category='Ads';return saveBudget_Legacy(p);}
function expenseTotals_(expenses,budgets,start,end,today){const canonical=x=>({...x,category:/^(ads?|advertising)$/i.test(str_(x.category))?'Ads':x.category});return expenseTotals_Legacy(expenses.map(canonical),budgets.map(canonical),start,end,today);}

function includeCrossCutoffEstimates_(d){
 const cross=d.months.filter(m=>!m.historical&&m.estimate!==0),extra=cross.reduce((a,m)=>a+m.estimate,0);
 if(cross.length){d.displayNet+=extra;d.projectedNet=d.projectedNet===null?null:d.projectedNet+extra;d.net=null;d.displayNetLabel='Includes historical estimates';d.displayMargin=d.sales?d.displayNet/d.sales*100:null;cross.forEach(m=>{m.projectedNet+=m.estimate;m.net=null;});}
}

function importCommit_(p){
 const existing=read_('Orders'),maps=importMatchMaps_(existing);
 for(const n of read_('Stage')){const hit=findImportMatch_(n,maps);if(hit.problem)throw Error('Resolve import problems: '+hit.problem);const next=hit.order?importMerged_(hit.order,n):n;if(hit.order&&!importDiff_(hit.order,next))continue;
 for(const o of [hit.order,next].filter(Boolean)){guardPeriod_(o.date,o.date);const delivery=deliveryDay_(o);if(delivery)guardPeriod_(delivery,delivery);}}
 return importCommitBase_(p);
}
